Given a task instruction, a motion path and the images along it, it predicts a distribution over execution speed along the path, from which a chosen quantile defines a consistent clock for retiming each demonstration. Existing methods derive the acceleration signal from the very data they accelerate, so each new task costs another fit, annotation, or pair of predefined rates. A single trained speed prior instead covers every task in a corpus. We fit a speed prior on both robot teleoperation data and handheld demonstrations in which a human arm, rather than a robot arm, moves the gripper, and use it to retime robot demonstrations to the handheld pace before fine-tuning a vision-language-action policy. For execution, we pair the policy with a real-time optimal-control layer that tracks predicted action chunks under robot constraints using the predicted pace. On 120 RoboLab tasks, policies fine-tuned on our learned clocks succeed more often and move with up to half the jerk compared with policies fine-tuned on the original demonstration timing and uniformly accelerated to the same speed. On a real robot, our policy stacks cups in 3.50s, 1.72$\times$ faster than the default policy's 6.02s while succeeding as often, in 29 of 30 trials. Videos and further material are available at https://mllab.snu.ac.kr/projects/learned_speed_prior.

</details>


### [69] [Agentic Hazard Mapper: Emulating Expert Map-Making with AI Agents for Risk Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32738)
*Sidi Wu,Lifeng Lin,Yizi Chen,Anna Yokokubo,Lorenz Hurni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体系统Agentic Hazard Mapper，通过模拟专业制图工作流与迭代式设计权衡，自动生成灾害地图，并在用户研究中验证其优于市政地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害地图对公众风险沟通、应急准备与疏散规划至关重要，但地方政府与社区缺乏制图专业知识，导致地图设计质量难以保证。

Method: 先开展形成性研究（与制图专家合作）指导系统设计；构建多智能体系统，模拟专业制图工作流与迭代设计权衡，并针对不同数据、地点和灾害情境自适应调整设计决策；随后开展用户研究与访谈（制图专家和灾害实务人员）。

Result: 生成的地图提升了参与者的空间理解与疏散规划表现，降低了感知工作负荷与任务完成时间，且总体比对应的市政地图更受偏好。

Conclusion: 讨论了人工监督、面向不同用户与沟通情境的适配，以及将智能体支持扩展到灾害地图生产与风险沟通更广阶段的机会。

Abstract: Hazard maps are essential tools for public risk communication, preparedness, and evacuation planning, yet effective cartographic design is not always ensured due to limited access to cartographic expertise across local governments and communities. We present Agentic Hazard Mapper, a multi-agent system that automates hazard-map production by emulating professional cartographic workflows and iterative design trade-offs while adapting design decisions to different data, locations, and hazard contexts. We conducted a formative study with map experts to inform the system design, followed by a user study and interviews with map experts and hazard practitioners. The generated maps improved participants' spatial understanding and evacuation-planning performance, reduced perceived workload and task completion time, and were generally preferred over corresponding municipal maps. We further discuss human oversight, adaptation across users and communication contexts, and opportunities to extend agentic support across broader stages of hazard-map production and risk communication.

</details>


### [70] [ActionRAG: Evidence-Refining Graph Diffusion for Long-Term Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32752)
*Navyansh Mahla,Sashank Vandrangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActionRAG：图记忆架构，索引端谨慎去重+查询端图回溯重估，MAB八列平均55.1，超最强检索式基线5.4点，查询级综合成本<0.001美元，检索侧p99各分量合计440.7ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需要长期记忆，能在增长的交互历史中恢复精确且相互一致的证据���尤其当答案依赖事实间关系而非孤立语义匹配时。现有记忆管线两处脆弱：索引错误导致重复实体被碎片化或不同实体被误合并；在线检索把初始相似度分数当作最终结果，即使局部图结构提供了修正证据。

Method: 提出ActionRAG图记忆架构，将谨慎索引与查询时图回溯评估耦合。索引管线协调类型标签并执行分层实体消解；查询时通过实体与三元组双通道检索形成初始证据集并诱导有界局部图；采用查询条件化的接收者归一化扩散，利用局部路径结构精修三元组分数；并辅以种子保护、枢纽惩罚通量与三元组实体化，保留直接证据同时回收漏检连接。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench（MAB）上评估，覆盖准确检索、冲突消解、长程理解与测试时学习。八个确定性评分列上平均准确率55.1，较最强检索式记忆基线提升5.4点。按MAB查询时成本核算，四个报告成本列上平均综合成本低于每查询0.001美元；在消费级Apple Silicon环境下，单独测量的检索侧p99各分量之和为440.7ms。

Conclusion: 将索引谨慎性（类型协调+分层实体消解）与查询时基于局部图的分数重估相结合，可有效提升长程图记忆的证据一致性与检索准确率，同时保持低查询成本与毫秒级检索延迟，说明初始相似度不应作为检索终值。

Abstract: LLM agents need long-term memory that can recover precise and mutually consistent evidence from growing interaction histories, particularly when answers depend on relations among facts rather than isolated semantic matches. Current memory pipelines remain vulnerable at two stages: indexing errors can fragment repeated entities or merge distinct ones, and online retrieval often treats initial similarity scores as final even when the surrounding graph provides evidence for revising them. We introduce ActionRAG, a graph memory architecture that couples cautious indexing with query-time graph retroevaluation. Its indexing pipeline reconciles type labels and applies tiered entity resolution. Given a query, dual-channel entity and triple retrieval forms an initial evidence set and induces a bounded local graph. Query-conditioned receiver-normalized diffusion refines triple scores using local path structure. Seed protection, hub-penalized flux, and triple materialization preserve direct evidence while recovering missed connections. We evaluate ActionRAG on MemoryAgentBench (MAB), which tests accurate retrieval, conflict resolution, long-range understanding, and test-time learning. Across its eight deterministic-scoring columns, ActionRAG achieves 55.1 average accuracy, improving over the strongest retrieval-based memory baseline by 5.4 points. Under MAB's query-time cost accounting, mean synthesis cost stays below \$0.001 per query on the four reported cost columns, and the sum of separately measured retrieval-side p99 components is 440.7 ms on our consumer Apple Silicon setup.

</details>


### [71] [HarnessMM: Recursive Self-Improvement for Harness Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32813)
*Yuhua Jiang,Chang Xu,Feifei Gao,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessMM，通过受守卫的决策复用与递归自我改进（RSI）减少 agent harness 中重复的推理回调，在保持或提升成功率的同时显著降低 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent harness 在工具反馈后反复调用推理，即使前一次调用已确定下一步动作，造成大量冗余 token 开销。

Method: 在模型-工具边界设控制器，actor 权重固定；用可执行决策图（EDG）存储精确动作、字面参数、因果依赖与公开守卫；守卫决策复用依据观测到的回执激活持久决策，经认证回调替换通过原生接口执行并记录移除慢回调；遇到新语义决策、证据不足或选择器弃权时回退原 actor；RSI 基于训练反馈迭代优化 companion harness 配置，并由验证集选择部署配置。

Result: HumanEval 上 Codex 成功率保持 90.82%，Tokens/task 由 66.72k 降至 47.02k（节省 29.54%）；DeepSeek Harness 成功率由 71.43% 升至 85.71%，Tokens/task 由 96.28k 降至 11.62k（节省 87.93%）；MBPP 消融显示成本较单次使用图低 26.91%，较保留回调低 19.52%。

Conclusion: 受守卫的决策复用是一种在现有 harness 中减少重复推理的实用方法，能兼顾质量与成本。

Abstract: Agent harnesses repeatedly invoke reasoning after tool feedback, even when an earlier call has already fixed the next action. We introduce HarnessMM (Harness Minus Minus), a method for improving harness token efficiency through guarded decision reuse and recursive self-improvement (RSI). RSI iteratively refines companion harness configurations from training feedback, with validation selecting the configuration for deployment. A controller at the model-tool boundary reuses resolved decisions to replace later reasoning callbacks while actor weights remain fixed. Executable Decision Graphs store exact actions, literal arguments, causal dependencies, and public guards. Guarded Decision Reuse activates these persistent decisions from observed receipts; Certified Callback Substitution executes them through the native interface and records removal of the registered slow callback. New semantic decisions, unsupported evidence, and selector abstention invoke the original actor again. The representation restricts what feedback may change, while complete-task measurements establish quality and cost. On HumanEval, HarnessMM preserves Codex success at 90.82\% while reducing Tokens/task (k) from 66.72 to 47.02 (29.54\% saving). For DeepSeek Harness, success rises from 71.43\% to 85.71\% while Tokens/task (k) fall from 96.28 to 11.62 (87.93\% saving). A separate MBPP ablation measures 26.91\% lower cost than single-use graphs and 19.52\% lower cost than retained callbacks. These results establish guarded decision reuse as a practical way to reduce repeated reasoning in existing harnesses.

</details>


### [72] [Reimagining Enterprise RAG for Multi-Agent Systems: A Knowledge Layer That Organizes Before It Retrieves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32851)
*Garima Agrawal,Prasun Das,Akshay Sarvade,Priyanka L,Minisha N,Sravani Joshna,Hemath Manivannan,Lavanya Korada,Sambhrit Pattanayak,Srashti Gupta,Siddu R,Prasad Kalyanasundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向机器生成产物的"知识层"，将本体建在工件层（保留生产者、组件、服务、域、类型等来源元数据），通过自描述目录、稀疏元数据注册表和导航超图在检索之前完成组织，使查询解析为工件标识符以约束向量检索，从而把消歧做成结构性的而非统计性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在软件开发生命周期中部署智能体AI，需要的是系统的行为逻辑而非利益相关方文档；七智能体逆向工程流水线虽可从241个仓库、18个服务恢复出成千上万描述范围、旅程、规则和验收标准的工件，但"恢复不等于可用"。offer、eligibility、activation等实体跨服务共享词汇、名义相关却操作上彼此不同，一条端到端旅程牵涉上千条近乎相同的规则且不可混淆。扁平向量检索失败——会以高相似度返回错误服务中的同款规则且无错误信号；而内容级本体并不存在，为每个复现实体建立关联的成本超过项目本身且无法跨发布周期存活。

Method: 将本体置于工件层：利用机器生成语料中每个工件自带的provenance（producer、component、service、domain、type），在读取内容前即完成消歧。构建包含三部分的知识层——自描述目录（self-describing catalog）、稀疏元数据注册表（sparse metadata registry）、以及在工件而非抽取实体之上定义的导航超图（navigation hypergraph）。查询先解析为工件标识符，再用其约束向量检索。

Result: 竞争性规则因结构约束而不出现在候选集中，而非被排序压低，消歧成为结构性的而非统计性的。该层不到一周建成，并以约定方式接纳新服务，服务于SDLC智能体、开发者、质量工程师和分析师；支撑了100+生产缺陷的根因分析与修复（经开发者验证），并将35次发布回归失败归因到具体规则与服务。

Conclusion: 在机器生成的工件语料中，把本体建在工件层、以来源元数据先组织后检索，是一种低成本、可扩展、可跨发布周期存活的消歧方案，优于内容级本体与扁平向量检索，并已在真实缺陷与回归分析中体现工程价值。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying agentic AI across the software development lifecycle, and they need the behavioral logic of the systems they act on, not stakeholder documentation. A seven-agent reverse-engineering pipeline recovered this logic, processing 241 repositories across 18 services into thousands of artifacts describing scope, journeys, rules and acceptance criteria. Recovery does not confer usability. Entities like offer, eligibility, and activation recur across services under a shared vocabulary, related in name but operationally distinct, so one end-to-end journey implicates over a thousand near-identical rules that must not be conflated. Flat vector retrieval fails, returning an identical rule from the wrong service at high similarity with no signal of error. Content-level ontology, the traditional remedy, did not exist, and relating every occurrence of recurring entities would have cost more than the engagement and not survived a release cycle. We place the ontology at the artifact level. In this machine-generated corpus, each artifact retains its producer, component, service, domain and type, provenance that disambiguates before content is read. We present a knowledge layer that organizes before retrieving: a self-describing catalog, a sparse metadata registry, and a navigation hypergraph defined over artifacts rather than extracted entities. Queries resolve to artifact identifiers that constrain vector search, so the competing rule from another service is absent rather than outranked, making disambiguation structural, not statistical. Built in under a week and admitting new services by convention, the layer serves SDLC agents, developers, quality engineers and analysts. It supported root-cause analysis and resolution of 100+ production defects with developer verification and attributed 35 release regression failures to specific rules and services.

</details>


### [73] [A Foundation Model for Policy In-Context Longitudinal Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32863)
*Wenxin Chen,Kyra Gan,Mingyu Lu,Zilin Jing,Fei Wang,Hoifung Poon,Tristan Naumann,Peniel Argaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PICLE-PFN 是首个支持动态治疗策略的纵向因果效应估计基础模型，通过情境学习与合成先验预训练，单次前向即可直接预测 CAPO，无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果效应估计受时变混杂与治疗-混杂反馈环困扰；现有方法需针对每个数据集拟合 nuisance 模型，且多局限于预设治疗序列或预测期，部署时计算耗时、难以泛化。

Method: 将目标策略编码为情境中的历史-决策示范，统一接口覆盖广泛策略类；在由纵向结构因果模型采样的合成数据上预训练先验数据拟合网络（PFN），把因果效应估计摊还到跨数据集、策略与预测期；给定新观测数据与目标策略，一次前向直接输出条件平均潜在结果（CAPO）。

Result: 在合成与半合成纵向基准上，RMSE 与 SOTA 传统估计器（逐数据集训练与调参）相当，不确定性校准显著更优，而部署运行时间仅为其中一小部分。

Conclusion: PICLE-PFN 表明基于先验拟合网络的基础模型可作为纵向因果效应估计的通用、免调参、低延迟方案，并原生支持动态治疗策略。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal effect estimation is complicated by time-varying confounding and treatment-confounder feedback loops. Existing methods generally require data-specific nuisance-model fitting, and many are tailored to prespecified treatment sequences or prediction horizons, leading to time-consuming computation at deployment. We introduce *Policy In-Context Longitudinal causal Effect estimation with Prior-data Fitted Networks* (**PICLE-PFN**), the first foundation model for longitudinal causal effect estimation that supports *dynamic* treatment policies. PICLE-PFN represents target policies through in-context history-decision demonstrations, providing a unified interface for a broad class of treatment policies. Pretrained over synthetic data sampled from longitudinal structural causal models, PICLE-PFN amortizes causal effect estimation across datasets, policies, and horizons. Given a new observational dataset and target policy, it directly predicts conditional average potential outcomes (CAPOs) in one forward pass without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning. Across synthetic and semi-synthetic longitudinal benchmarks, PICLE-PFN achieves competitive RMSE and substantially improved uncertainty calibration with a fraction of deployment runtime, compared to state-of-the-art conventional estimators trained and tuned separately for each dataset.

</details>


### [74] [From Deliberately Constructed Systemic Representations of the World of Activity to Dialogue: A Five-Level Communication Architecture for Strong Language-Based ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32869)
*Maxim Osovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"讯息生成与理解"的五层系统—活动组织框架，论证语言模型的上下文在原理上无法呈现语句生成的基础，这些基础由后天获得的图式、活动世界的系统表征及其中被接受为真的联系预先确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM依赖上下文与训练权重运作，但二者均不能证明"有意建构的系统性表征"存在；因此需要一个可检验、可调整讯息基础的分层框架，以区分"基于上下文的文本生成"与"基于可重建系统基础的生成"。

Method: 采用理论建构与概念分析，融合三种理论资源：Shchedrovitskiy的系统方法论、Korzybski的结构微分、Mel'chuk的意义—文本理论；提出五层结构：活动世界的系统表征→从其中切分出的具体情境→情境的叙事描述→讯息的完整意义→文本。并给出逆向路径：从完整意义→扩展描述→情境图式模型→系统基础的重建。

Result: 确立关键区分：情境的图式模型不同于关于它的故事；接收者借助共同建构并达成一致的表征与共同活动，从压缩文本中重建完整意义；由此形成正向（生成）与逆向（还原）双向可操作的层级链路。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为审查与调整讯息基础、控制其转换后果提供了具体的系统—活动内容组织方式；后续研究方向是可重建系统基础上的大规模活动图模型，有别于从上下文生成文本的范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes a five-level organization of message formation and understanding within thinking and activity in which ASI participates. Its central thesis is that a language model's context is fundamentally incapable of presenting the foundations of utterance formation for examination and verification. These foundations are established in advance by acquired schemas, systemic representations of the world of activity, and connections accepted as true within them. Neither context nor trained LLM weights, in themselves, attest to the presence of deliberately constructed systemic representations. The five levels distinguish systemic representations of the world of activity, a particular situation cut out from those representations, its narrative description, the full sense of a message, and text. A situation's schema-model is not identical to a story about it. A recipient reconstructs the full sense from an abbreviated text through jointly constructed and agreed-upon representations and joint activity. Reverse work proceeds from the full sense through an extended description and the situation's schema-model to the reconstruction of systemic foundations. The theoretical foundations draw on the systems methodology of G. P. Shchedrovitskiy, the structural differential of A. Korzybski, and the Meaning-Text theory of I. A. Mel'chuk. The contribution is a specific systemic-activity organization of content for examining and adjusting message foundations and controlling the consequences of their transformation. Further research concerns large graphical models of activity on reconstructible systemic foundations, distinct from text generation from context.

</details>


### [75] [Do "True" and "False" Add Up to One? Complement Consistency in LLM-Agent Opinion Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32874)
*Anouar Imel,Jin Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用互补性检验（p与1-p配对求和应等于1）测试三个小开源模型在LLM-agent意见模拟中的概率报告，发现系统性违反互补性，且该偏差在网络反馈中放大、不能被简单平均化修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM-agent意见模拟中，若agent报告的数值表征其对命题的"立场/信念"而非对所示句子的反应，那么把命题改写成其否定、并把每个概率p换成1-p，应得到互补答案（两者之和为1）。作者希望检验这一基本一致性是否成立，从而判断模拟出的概率能否被解读为信念。

Method: 在三个小开源模型（Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、Gemma-3-4B）上进行配对提问：agent看到自身上一轮概率与邻居概率后报告某断言为真的概率。数据包括：来自运行中网络模拟的2,304个agent状态、AVeriTeC的48条真实事实核查断言，以及一个含24个世界（每个恰有两种可能结果）的受控基准。对照条件包括同义改写（paraphrase）、重复采样、不同同伴组规模与报告强度。另做双群体互补初始状态的反馈实验（五轮）以及schedule-by-targeting实验。

Result: Qwen的配对报告平均求和为1.18，在AVeriTeC真实断言上亦大于1；受控基准中三个模型均出现同样失败。偏差是同义改写所引起变化量的2–10倍，且在重复采样、不同组规模与报告强度下持续存在。互补初始状态的两个群体经五轮反馈后不再保持镜像，所有模型的分歧度均为正。将每个答案与其配对答案的补做平均可按构造恢复一致性并降低两种措辞下的平均Brier损失，但末轮仅在三个模型中的一个上优于两种原始措辞中较好的那个。schedule-by-targeting实验中因断言措辞固定，交互效应小而稳定。

Conclusion: 模型报告的概率不满足措辞互补一致性，说明其更像对特定句面的反应而非稳定信念；该不一致会在群体反馈中累积放大。因此，在把模拟概率解读为信念之前，必须单独进行改写一致性检验；简单地与补答案平均虽保证自洽，却并不能稳定提升预测表现。

Abstract: In LLM-agent opinion simulations, each agent reports the probability that a claim is true after seeing its own previous probability and the probabilities reported by its neighbors, and that number becomes its state in the next round. If the number describes the agent's position rather than the sentence it was shown, then rewriting the claim as its complement and replacing every probability p with 1 - p should return the complementary answer, so the two reports should sum to one. We test this with three small open models: Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.2-3B and Gemma-3-4B. On 2,304 agent states taken from a running network simulation, Qwen's paired reports sum to 1.18 on average, and on 48 real fact-checking claims from AVeriTeC they again exceed one. A controlled benchmark of 24 worlds, each with exactly two possible outcomes, shows the same failure in all three models. The discrepancy is two to ten times larger than the change produced by a same-meaning paraphrase, and it persists under repeated sampling and across peer group sizes and report strengths. When the reports are fed back into two populations that start from complementary states, the populations do not remain mirror images: their divergence is positive after five rounds for every model. Averaging each answer with the complement of its paired answer restores consistency by construction and lowers Brier loss averaged over the two wordings, but at the final round it beats the better of the two original wordings for only one of the three models. A schedule-by-targeting experiment on the same model gives a small, stable interaction because such sweeps keep the claim wording fixed. Consistency under rewording therefore needs its own test before simulated probabilities are read as beliefs

</details>


### [76] [Advancing SVD-based LLM Compression via Layer-Wise Error Model Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32878)
*Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Groezinger,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Emad Aghajanzadeh,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Pierpaolo Mori,Nael Fasfous,Alexander Frickenstein,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEMS 提出逐层误差建模的全局秩分配搜索，KFAC-SVD 利用 token 级统计改进分解质量，在 Mistral、Qwen3、Llama 3 上显著提升零样本精度与困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩 SVD 压缩可降低 LLM 计算成本，但现有方法存在两大局限：全局秩分配中未校准的误差代理无法刻画复杂误差传播；基于 Fisher 的分解估计器出现严重秩坍缩，导致分解质量下降。

Method: 提出两项技术：(1) Layer-wise Error Modeling Search (LEMS)，构造融合局部与全局层重要性以及传播偏置的逐层误差代理，并通过整数线性规划（ILP）实现全局秩分配；(2) KFAC-SVD，利用 token 级统计缓解先前基于 Fisher 的 SVD 中的秩亏问题，提升分解质量。

Result: 在 Mistral、Qwen3 与 Llama 3 系列上，LEMS 持续优于现有搜索策略，零样本精度最高提升 4.8 个百分点，并可泛化到 70B 参数规模；KFAC-SVD 平均带来 15% 的困惑度改善。

Conclusion: 通过误差建模驱动的全局秩分配与改进的统计估计分解，可有效提升低秩 SVD 后训练压缩的精度-效率权衡，并具备跨模型族与大模型规模的可扩展性。

Abstract: Low-rank SVD-based compression offers a powerful strategy to reduce the computational costs of LLMs. However, existing methods face two key limitations: (i) global rank allocation, where uncalibrated error proxies fail to capture complex error propagation, and (ii) decomposition quality, where Fisher-based estimators suffer from severe rank collapse. In this work, we address these limitations by introducing Layer-wise Error Modeling Search (LEMS) and KFAC-SVD. LEMS advances rank allocation by introducing a layer-wise error surrogate that integrates local and global layer importance alongside a propagation bias, enabling effective global rank allocation via an ILP formulation. KFAC-SVD improves decomposition quality by utilizing token-wise statistics, mitigating the rank deficiency observed in prior Fisher-based SVD approaches. Across Mistral, Qwen3, and Llama 3 model families, we show that LEMS consistently outperforms existing search strategies, delivering significant zero-shot accuracy gains of up to 4.8 p.p. that generalize to model sizes of 70B parameters, while KFAC-SVD achieves an average perplexity improvement of 15%. Project page and code: https://lems-svd.github.io

</details>


### [77] [NgramRL: Accelerating RL Post-Training for Structured Generation via Rollout-Aware N-gram Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32881)
*Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Jangseon Park,Jason Kong,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NgramRL是一种面向RL后训练的逐prompt n-gram草稿器，利用同一prompt近期rollout作为自更新草稿源，无需辅助模型或驻留GPU状态，并配合自适应fan-out预取器隐藏CPU查表开销，实现每目标模型前向提交3.60-7.77个token、rollout吞吐提升1.38x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练中自回归rollout生成成本主导训练时间（可超70%）。现有投机解码不适配：基于模型的草稿器占用稀缺GPU显存且随策略演化失配；无模型方法未显式利用跨训练epoch的重复性。

Method: 1) 观察到同一prompt的近期rollout是高质量、自更新的草稿源；2) 提出NgramRL：由有界近期rollout历史构建逐prompt n-gram草稿器，无需辅助模型、草稿器训练或持久GPU驻留状态，目标模型验证保证rollout分布不变；3) 提出自适应fan-out预取器，预测验证结果(j,b)（接受草稿长度j与目标模型续发token b）；4) 因果预算分配器将预取集中于高概率结果，未覆盖结果回退到串行查找且不改变已提交token。

Result: 在两个模型家族、三类推理与结构化生成工作负载上，热缓存下每次目标模型前向提交3.60-7.77个token，较SuffixDecoding最多高1.99x、较prompt-lookup decoding高7.0x。在Spider上，候选预算16时预取器覆盖Qwen3-8B 96.6%的验证结果，实测rollout吞吐较同步NgramRL提升1.38x，接近其1.40x重叠上限。

Conclusion: 利用近期rollout历史构建无模型、零驻留开销的逐prompt n-gram草稿，并结合结果预测式预取隐藏CPU侧开销，可显著加速RL后训练的rollout生成，且不改变输出分布。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but its cost is dominated by autoregressive rollout generation, which can exceed 70% of training time. Existing speculative-decoding methods are poorly suited to this setting: model-based drafters consume scarce GPU memory and may become misaligned as the policy evolves, while existing model-free methods do not explicitly exploit repetition across training epochs. We observe that recent rollouts of the same prompt provide a high-quality, self-updating draft source. Based on this insight, we propose NgramRL, a per-prompt n-gram drafter constructed from bounded recent rollout history. NgramRL requires no auxiliary model, drafter training, or persistent GPU-resident drafter state, while target-model verification preserves the rollout distribution. To hide CPU cache lookup and scheduling, we further introduce an adaptive fan-out prefetcher that predicts likely verification outcomes (j, b), consisting of the accepted draft length j and target-emitted continuation token b. A causal budget allocator concentrates prefetches on high-probability outcomes, while uncovered outcomes fall back to sequential lookup without changing committed tokens. Across two model families and three reasoning and structured-generation workloads, NgramRL commits 3.60-7.77 tokens per target-model forward in the warm-cache regime, up to 1.99x more than SuffixDecoding and 7.0x more than prompt-lookup decoding. On Spider, the prefetcher covers 96.6% of Qwen3-8B outcomes at a candidate budget of 16 and improves measured rollout throughput by 1.38x over synchronous NgramRL, approaching its 1.40x overlap ceiling.

</details>


### [78] [Closing the Modality Gap via Simplex-Constrained Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32890)
*Shubham Gupta,Siva Reddy,Perouz Taslakian,Valentina Zantedeschi,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将多模态对比模型的嵌入域从单位球面改为概率单纯形并配合softmax适配器与全变差相似度，可将质心ℓ2模态间隙降低97–99%且检索性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP、CLAP等多模态对比模型虽学到共享表征，但嵌入存在持续的"模态间隙"（modality gap），即不同模态嵌入在空间中系统性分离，影响跨模态对齐质量。

Method: 纯几何方法：将嵌入域由单位球面S^{d-1}替换为概率单纯形Δ^{d-1}。两者内在维度相同，但单纯形的非负与单位质量约束构成结构先验，作为跨模态校准机制迫使两模态锚定于同一坐标系；实现上附加轻量softmax适配器，并采用全变差（Total Variation）相似度。

Result: 在五个多模态检索基准上，质心ℓ2间隙降低97–99%，检索性能与标准无约束模型持平或更优。

Conclusion: 单纯形几何可诱导真正的语义对齐，验证了几何域选择（而非仅优化目标）是缓解模态间隙的有效手段。

Abstract: While multimodal contrastive models like CLIP and CLAP successfully learn shared representations, their embeddings typically exhibit a persistent \emph{modality gap}. We propose addressing this separation through a purely geometric approach: changing the embedding domain from the unit sphere, $S^{d-1}$, to the probability simplex, $Δ^{d-1}$. Although both manifolds share the same intrinsic dimension, the simplex acts as a structural prior; its nonnegative, unit-mass constraints serve as a cross-modal calibration that forces both modalities to ground themselves in the same coordinate system. By attaching lightweight softmax adapters and using Total Variation similarity, we reduce the centroid $\ell_2$ gap by 97--99\% across five multimodal retrieval benchmarks. This confirms that simplicial geometry induces genuine semantic alignment, matching or exceeding the retrieval performance of standard unconstrained models.

</details>


### [79] [SemantEq: Faithful Bidirectional Equivalence of Formal Statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32919)
*Xinrui Li,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Siddharth Bhat,Rianna Jamdar,Yongxi Lin,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出更强的形式化等价判据 SemantEq 及其配套审计工具，在不放松验收标准的前提下提升语句自动形式化的验证精度与召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语句自动形式化（自动形式化，把自然语言数学翻译为 Lean 4 等证明助手语言）已能规模化进行，但随着证明器变强，正确性负担从"证明"转移到"语句是否忠实翻译原文"。给定可信参考形式化后，自然的检验是逻辑等价，但该判据过强：任何两个可被库证明的语句 P、Q 都满足 P↔True↔Q，证明器越强，无关语句对通过越多。已有方法靠限制证明器并要求证明中引用对方语句来规避，仍不够原则上。

Method: 提出 SemantEq 关系：要求每个语句只能通过一系列允许步骤（逻辑重排、用库自带的等式重写等）转化为另一个，而非任意证明；程序基于 Lean 检查的证明项（proof term）而非 tactic 脚本来判定。实现两个组件：SemantEq-Eval（无模型的搜索式判定）与 SemantEq-ProofAudit（依据该关系导出的策略审计模型撰写的证明）。将等价证明的生成与接受分离，使更强证明器可提高召回而不改变接受的含义。

Result: 在 ProofNetVerif 测试划分（自标注标签）上，SemantEq-Eval 达 100% 精度、65.6% 召回，对比 BEq+ 的 95.8% 与 61.8%；其 CPU 时间增加 8.9 倍最多只多出三对判定。语言模型能找到更多证明，但在四种提示中有两种，约五分之二的非等价对被证出双向；SemantEq-ProofAudit 在导出策略下拒绝了全部 104 个非等价对，并在最佳提示下将 407 个等价对的召回从 65.4% 提升至 81.1%。

Conclusion: 以"受限变换链+证明项审计"为核心的 SemantEq 提供了更严格且可扩展的等价判定，兼顾高精度与召回；将等价证明的生成与验收解耦，使更强证明器可安全提升召回，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Statement autoformalization translates mathematics from natural language into a proof assistant such as Lean 4, and models now do it at scale. As provers get stronger, the burden of correctness shifts from the proof to the statement, that is, to whether the formal statement faithfully translates its source. Given a trusted reference formalization, the natural test is logical equivalence, and it proves too much. $P \leftrightarrow Q$ holds trivially for any two statements $P$ and $Q$ that the library proves, because a prover can prove each separately and pass through $P \leftrightarrow \mathrm{True} \leftrightarrow Q$. The stronger the prover, the more unrelated pairs pass. Earlier methods restrict the prover and check that each proof names the other statement. We introduce SemantEq, a more principled relation that instead requires each statement to turn into the other only through a chain of allowed steps, such as logical rearrangement and rewriting with the library's own equations. Our programs judge a proof by the proof term Lean checks, not by its tactic script. SemantEq-Eval, a search without a model, reaches 100% precision and 65.6% recall on the ProofNetVerif test split under our adjudicated labels, against 95.8% and 61.8% for BEq+, and an 8.9-fold increase in its CPU time adds at most three pairs. A language model finds more proofs, but under two of four prompts it proves both directions of about two in five of the non-equivalent pairs. SemantEq-ProofAudit audits each model-written proof under a policy derived from the relation and accepts none of the 104 non-equivalent pairs, and it raises recall on 407 equivalent pairs from 65.4% to 81.1% under the best prompt. Separating the generation of equivalence proofs from their acceptance lets a stronger prover raise recall without changing what an acceptance means. We release code and data at: https://github.com/hlxr89/SemantEq

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Collaborative In-Context Learning for Indoor Radio Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31702)
*Yuanzhe Peng,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将室内无线电地图估计(RME)首次形式化为协作式上下文学习(ICL)问题，提出Poly-Ripple框架：多个客户端用大自回归视觉模型在无参数更新下做ICL，并结合涟漪排序的自回归建模与重要性感知聚合，在公开室内RME基准上稳定优于代表性ICL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内RME方法多依赖监督学习，需昂贵标注数据且需任务专用训练；同时室内布局等信息隐私敏感难以集中，因此需要免训练、保护隐私的协作方案。

Method: 将室内RME建模为协作ICL问题；各客户端以私有示范为提示，用LAVM对同一场景查询做无更新推理；采用涟漪排序的自回归建模刻画以发射机为中心的传播因果结构；引入重要性感知聚合，在上下文长度约束下融合各客户端异构上下文知识；并给出理论分析。

Result: 在公开室内RME基准上，Poly-Ripple持续优于代表性ICL基线，实验结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 免训练的协作ICL可有效实现室内RME，兼顾隐私与标注成本；涟漪式自回归建模与重要性感知聚合是关键设计。

Abstract: Indoor radio map estimation (RME) is essential for wireless applications such as coverage planning, localization, and network deployment. However, most existing methods follow a supervised learning paradigm that requires costly labeled data and task-specific model training. Moreover, indoor environments involve privacy-sensitive information, such as building layouts and structures, that often cannot be centralized. To address these challenges, we make the first attempt to formulate indoor RME as a collaborative in-context learning (ICL) problem. We propose Poly-Ripple, a framework in which multiple clients perform ICL using large autoregressive vision models (LAVMs) without model updates. Each client is prompted by its private demonstrations and predicts the radio map for the same scene query. To capture the causal structure of wireless propagation outward from the transmitter (Tx), we adopt ripple-ordered autoregressive modeling. We further introduce importance-aware aggregation to exploit heterogeneous contextual knowledge distributed across clients under the context length constraint. We provide theoretical analysis and conduct extensive experiments on a public indoor RME benchmark. The experimental results show that Poly-Ripple consistently outperforms representative ICL baselines, while aligning with our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [81] [Multiplicative vs. Additive State Coupling in Learned Optimizer Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32801)
*M. Harper Langston,Pierre-David Letourneau,Dalton Jones,Roland Memisevic,Peter Scott,Mingu Lee,Richard A. Lethin,Christopher Lott,Fatih Porikli,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过展开经典求解器反向传播学习 MAX-CUT 连续惩罚松弛的调度：状态无关调度可泛化到更大实例；状态自适应双线性调度可分布外泛化；同输入同输出头的加性消融在分布内无差异但分布外劣于自身基线，说明起作用的是调度中求解器状态的进入方式（乘性/双线性）而非状态自适应性本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学习式调度（schedule）能否泛化，尤其是分布外（更大规模实例）泛化；并厘清调度中"求解器状态"应以何种架构方式被使用——是状态自适应本身，还是状态的具体进入方式——才是分布外迁移的关键。

Method: 对 MAX-CUT 的连续惩罚松弛，通过展开（unrolled）经典求解器并反向传播学习调度。设计三类调度：状态无关（state-blind）调度；状态自适应双线性（bilinear）调度；以及与双线性模型输入、输出头完全相同的加性（additive）消融模型。在 15 个随机种子下与各自未训练基线比较，并做 30 组权重衰减扫描（weight-decay sweep），不使用额外正则化。

Result: 状态无关调度可泛化到远大于训练规模的实例；双线性调度分布外优于未训练基线（t=4.1，15 seeds）；加性消融分布内与双线性模型统计不可区分，但分布外劣于自身基线（t=-11.7）；该差异在权重衰减扫描中持续存在，双线性优势在所有测试值下均显著，加性模型始终未能恢复。

Conclusion: 在该实验设定下，决定分布外迁移的架构区分因素是求解器状态"如何"进入调度（乘性/双线性交互），而非单纯的"状态自适应"能力；加性 vs 双线性是有效的机制性消融，且该结论不依赖于正则化强度的选择。

Abstract: We learn schedules for a continuous penalty relaxation of MAX-CUT by backpropagating through an unrolled classical solver. A state-blind schedule generalizes to instances substantially larger than those used for training. A state-adaptive bilinear schedule also generalizes out of distribution, outperforming its untrained baseline across 15 seeds ($t=4.1$). By contrast, an additive ablation with identical inputs and output heads is statistically indistinguishable from the bilinear model in distribution but performs worse than its own baseline out of distribution ($t=-11.7$). This separation appears without regularization and persists across a $30$ round weight-decay sweep: the bilinear advantage remains significant at every tested value, whereas the additive model never recovers. The results in this setting isolate how solver state enters the schedule, rather than state adaptivity alone, as the architectural distinction associated with out-of-distribution transfer.

</details>


### [82] [The Path to NDE 4.0: A Review of Algorithms, Techniques, and Strategies for Autonomous Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31704)
*Ashiqur Rahmana,Venkata Devesh Reddy Seethi,Zachary Kral,Austin Yunker,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Hamed Alhoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇无损检测（NDI）领域综述，从计算算法、物理技术、工业应用、研究目标四个维度梳理现状，指出NDI正由人工模拟判读向以数据为中心的NDE 4.0范式演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代质量保证与基础设施、制造部件安全可靠性对无损检测高度依赖；领域技术演进迅速但缺乏系统梳理，且存在标准化开放数据集稀缺、AI"黑箱"等落地瓶颈，亟需厘清发展脉络并识别研究空白。

Method: 采用综述性方法，综合广泛学术文献，以计算算法、物理技术、工业应用与研究目标四个视角对NDI现状进行系统分析与归纳。

Result: 分析显示超声与视觉检测为主导物理手段，缺陷自动识别研究显著转向深度学习与生成模型；同时存在开放获取标准化数据集稀缺与AI模型黑箱性两大关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 未来NDI研究应聚焦智能自主的检测数据融合、先进传感技术与资产生命周期管理的深度集成，并优先推进可解释AI（XAI）与标准化数字孪生协议。

Abstract: Non-Destructive Inspection (NDI) is a cornerstone of modern quality assurance, critical for ensuring the safety and reliability of infrastructure and manufactured components without causing damage. This paper presents a comprehensive review of the current NDI landscape, analyzing the field through four distinct lenses, namely, computational algorithms, physical techniques, industrial applications, and research objectives. We synthesize a broad range of academic literature to illustrate the rapid evolution from manual, analog interpretation to a digitized, data-centric paradigm known as NDE 4.0. Our analysis highlights the dominance of ultrasound and visual inspection as primary physical techniques, while revealing a significant shift toward deep learning and generative models for automated defect recognition. Despite these technological strides, the industry faces critical bottlenecks, notably the scarcity of standardized open-access datasets and the "black box" nature of AI models, which hinders widespread adoption. By charting these developments and identifying key research gaps, such as the need for explainable AI (XAI) and standardized digital twin protocols, this review offers a strategic roadmap for future research. It concludes that the future of NDI research should focus on the successful integration of intelligent, autonomous fusion of inspection data with advanced sensor technologies into asset lifecycle management.

</details>


### [83] [Does LeWorldModel Preserve Local Action Geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31716)
*Vasil Vasilev,Siddharth Nayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以Gram矩阵在三种扰动尺度下量化LeWM的动作敏感性与模拟器的一致性，发现最敏感方向偏差约36°/42°，且误差随尺度增大而减小，主张按尺度分别评估动作敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验预训练LeWM是否可靠复现"小幅动作变化"引发的效果，即其动作敏感性的局部几何是否与环境真实响应一致。

Method: 在Reacher与OGBench Cube近千个状态上做匹配的模拟器—模型对照实验：以Gram矩阵在三个扰动尺度上度量动作敏感性，比较最敏感动作方向夹角、归一化Gram差异，并对比像素几何、嵌入几何与物理状态几何；另在留出方向上评估嵌入响应误差，并与同迹各向同性参考比较。

Result: 最小尺度下最敏感方向中位夹角约36°（Reacher）与42°（Cube）；扰动增大10倍时归一化Gram差异中位下降约70%（Reacher）与47%（Cube）。像素与嵌入几何的跨尺度变化大于物理状态几何。留出方向上环境Gram的嵌入响应误差中位为17%与28%，同迹各向同性参考为26%与38%；该尺度下LeWM误差更高。

Conclusion: LeWM对动作敏感性的复现存在方向与幅度上的系统性偏差，且结论强烈依赖扰动尺度，故应对动作敏感性做分尺度评估；该偏差对下游控制的影响仍待研究。

Abstract: We examine how closely pretrained LeWorldModel (LeWM) reproduces the effects of small action changes. Using matched simulator and model responses, we measure action sensitivity with Gram matrices at three perturbation scales. Across nearly a thousand states from Reacher and OGBench Cube, the most sensitive action directions differ by median angles of about $36^\circ$ and $42^\circ$, respectively, at the smallest scale. Increasing the perturbation tenfold reduces median normalized Gram discrepancy by roughly 70\% on Reacher and 47\% on Cube. Geometry measured in pixels and embeddings varies more across scales than the chosen physical-state geometry. On held-out directions, environment Grams achieve median embedding response errors of 17\% and 28\%, compared with 26\% and 38\% for an isotropic reference with the same trace. LeWM's errors are higher at this scale. These findings support scale-specific evaluation of action sensitivity; its effect on control remains an open question.

</details>


### [84] [Towards Adversarially Robust Multigraph Neural Networks for Financial Crime Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31718)
*Dennis Heijmans,H. Çağrı Bilgi,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出首个针对多重图神经网络的可扩展结构攻击与对抗训练框架，基于节点-边关联矩阵优化结构扰动，并验证多重图GNN在AML任务上易受学习型结构攻击、对抗训练可有效提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融交易网络天然为多重图（同一账户对间重复交易建模为不同边），近期多重图GNN在反洗钱任务上表现优异；但其面对操纵交易结构以规避检测的对抗攻击时的鲁棒性尚缺乏研究。

Method: 以节点-边关联矩阵为扰动载体，将投影梯度下降（PGD）与投影随机块坐标下降（PRBCD）适配到该矩阵上，实现可扩展的结构攻击与对抗训练；并引入基于环的正则项，惩罚有向2-环与3-环全局计数的变化，作为攻击合理性的结构度量。

Result: 在合成模式检测与真实AML数据集上，学习型攻击在降低检测性能（PR-AUC）方面显著优于不可学习的扰动基线；对抗训练能持续提升模型对这些攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多重图神经网络易受学习型结构攻击，对抗训练是有效防御手段；代码已开源。

Abstract: Financial transaction networks are naturally represented as multigraphs, where repeated transactions between the same pair of accounts are modeled as distinct edges. Recently proposed multigraph neural network architectures leverage this structure explicitly to achieve a strong performance on anti-money laundering (AML) tasks. However, their robustness to adversaries that manipulate transaction structures to evade detection remains largely unexplored. We present the first scalable framework for structural attacks and adversarial training of multigraph neural networks using node-edge incidence matrices, adapting projected gradient descent and projected randomized block coordinate descent methods to optimize structural perturbations over such matrices. We further introduce a cycle-based regularizer that penalizes changes in the global counts of directed 2- and 3-cycles, providing a structural measure of attack plausibility. Experiments on synthetic pattern-detection and realistic AML datasets show that our learned attacks substantially outperform non-learnable perturbation baselines in reducing detection performance (PR-AUC), while adversarial training consistently improves robustness against these attacks. These results demonstrate that multigraph neural networks are vulnerable to learned structural attacks and that adversarial training provides an effective defense. Our code is available at https://github.com/denheijmans/robust_multignn.

</details>


### [85] [End-to-End Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning for Physics-Informed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31721)
*Ricardo Luna Gutierrez,Vineet Gundecha,Rahman Ejaz,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Riccardo Betti,Sahand Ghorbanpour,Aarne Lees,Soumyendu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微扩散条件化（D2C），利用确定性采样使逆去噪链可微，从而把冻结的条件扩散模型变为端到端梯度优化器，用于离线模型基优化（MBO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维物理系统在昂贵评估与复杂约束下优化困难；离线MBO只能依赖固定数据集且无测试时真值反馈。现有方法存在过度保守、无约束代理模型利用，或仅作静态推理采样器、无法针对精确物理目标精修候选解等问题。

Method: 基于确定性采样令逆向去噪链对条件信号可微，在测试时直接优化条件变量，将冻结代理目标与物理约束惩罚项的梯度经逆向链反传，把冻结条件扩散模型转为端到端梯度优化器；并采用“提议器—评估器”（proposer-evaluator）组合框架实现。

Result: 在惯性约束聚变激光脉冲优化、可持续数据中心负载调度两个真实任务上取得最优结果；在四个Design-Bench任务上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: D2C通过让扩散逆过程可微、以物理惩罚约束梯度优化，兼顾代理利用与可行性，为离线MBO提供了可精修候选解的有效范式，并在真实物理与调度任务上验证了优越性。

Abstract: Optimizing high-dimensional physical systems under expensive evaluations and complex constraints is a central challenge in science and engineering. In offline model-based optimization (MBO), methods must improve designs using only a fixed dataset and no test-time access to the true objective. However, existing methods typically suffer from conservatism, unconstrained surrogate exploitation, or function as static inference-time samplers that cannot refine candidates against precise physical objectives. In this paper, we propose Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning (D2C) for offline MBO, which leverages the fact that deterministic sampling makes the reverse denoising chain differentiable with respect to the conditioning signal. D2C turns a frozen conditional diffusion model into an end-to-end gradient-based optimizer by directly optimizing conditioning variables at test time via gradients from frozen surrogate objectives and physics-informed penalties backpropagated through the reverse chain. We implement this idea in a composed proposer-evaluator framework and evaluate it on laser pulse optimization for inertial confinement fusion, sustainable data-center workload scheduling, and four Design-Bench tasks. Our method achieves the strongest results on the two real-world tasks and the best average rank across four Design-Bench tasks.

</details>


### [86] [Autoregressive Multimodal Anomaly Detection with Large Language Models for Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31722)
*Yixiang Tang,Fagui Liu,Guoxiang Zhong,Xi Yao,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLMMAD——基于大语言模型的自回归多模态微服务异常检测框架，通过统一序列建模与模态专属重建头兼顾跨模态融合与模态特性保留，并用预测驱动的自回归目标消除身份捷径问题，在三个真实数据集上F1-score分别提升3.1%、1.4%、0.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统中故障与性能异常会跨服务快速传播，有效异常检测对可靠性与可用性至关重要。现有微服务多模态异常检测方法存在两大局限：(i) 偏重跨模态融合而忽视模态特有特征；(ii) 特征重建过程中存在"身份捷径"问题（模型倾向于直接复制输入而非真正学习表征）。

Method: LLMMAD采用基于LLM的自回归多模态框架。其一，将多模态数据嵌入并重组为统一序列输入LLM以建模模态间关系，同时为各模态设置独立的模态级重建头以保留模态特有信息，从而调和融合与特性保留的矛盾。其二，设计预测驱动的自回归目标，令模型预测未来状态而非复制当前输入，从根本上消除身份捷径。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LLMMAD一致优于现有最先进方法，F1-score分别提升3.1%、1.4%和0.9%，为微服务监控提供了更可靠的方案。

Conclusion: LLMMAD验证了以LLM统一序列建模结合模态专属重建与预测式自回归目标的有效性，可同时缓解融合-特性权衡与身份捷径两个关键问题，提升微服务系统多模态异常检测性能。

Abstract: Microservice systems have become a dominant architecture for large-scale cloud applications, enabling rapid deployment and elastic scaling. However, ensuring the reliability and availability of microservice systems critically relies on effective anomaly detection, as failures and performance anomalies can quickly propagate across services and severely impact end-to-end system behavior. Existing multimodal anomaly detection approaches for microservice systems often struggle with two fundamental limitations: (i) the neglect of modality-specific characteristics in favor of cross-modality fusion, and (ii) the "identity-shortcut" problem that affects the feature reconstruction process. To address these issues, we propose LLMMAD, an autoregressive multimodal anomaly detection framework for microservice systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs). Initially, to reconcile cross-modality fusion with modality-specific preservation, LLMMAD embeds and recombines multimodal data into a unified sequence for LLM processing to model inter-modality relationships, while employing modality-wise reconstruction heads to retain modality-specific information. Subsequently, to eliminate the identity-shortcut, we implement a prediction-driven autoregressive objective, under which the model predicts future states rather than copies current inputs. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments on three real-world datasets. The results demonstrate that LLMMAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving F1-score improvements of 3.1%, 1.4%, and 0.9%, respectively, thereby providing a more reliable solution for microservice monitoring.

</details>


### [87] [Cognitively Constrained Partners for Zero-Shot Coordination in Real-Time Partitioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31726)
*Ash Simmons,George De Ath,Richard M. Everson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以"认知约束伙伴"（基于人类生理文献的反应延迟与感知局限）作为模拟训练伙伴的框架，用于人机协作中的零样本协作；在改进的Lunar Lander中，此类伙伴行为更接近真人，训练出的智能体匹配最强交叉对局基线且性能不依赖于伙伴生成方式，并设计了可直接与交叉对局比较排名的人类被试研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作要求智能体能协同不可预测的人类伙伴。依赖真人在线反馈或大规模行为数据成本高；纯模拟训练则易过拟合人为约定，在零样本协作中失效。现有模拟伙伴的多样性多来自奖励塑形或历史训练检查点，缺乏对人类真实认知特征的可迁移刻画。

Method: 构建"认知约束伙伴"（Cognitively Constrained Partners）框架：伙伴的多样性由基于人类生理学文献的过程性约束产生，具体为反应延迟与感知局限，而非奖励塑形或训练检查点。在修改版Lunar Lander环境中进行训练与交叉对局评估，并设计人类被试研究，使其排名结果可与交叉对局指标直接比对。

Result: 初步证据显示：(1) 认知约束伙伴的对局行为比无约束智能体更接近真人；(2) 对抗此类伙伴训练的智能体达到最强交叉对局基线的水平，且是唯一性能不受训练伙伴生成方式影响的方案；(3) 人类被试研究尚在描述阶段，用于检验交叉对局这一标准评估对真实人机协作的预测力。

Conclusion: 以人类认知约束而非奖励塑形/检查点驱动伙伴多样性，是一种更贴近真人、更具泛化性且对伙伴生成方式不敏感的团队智能体训练路径；交叉对局作为评估指标的有效性有待人类被试数据检验。

Abstract: Effective human-machine teaming requires agents that coordinate with unpredictable human partners. Reinforcement learning from live human feedback or large behavioural datasets is expensive, while purely simulated training overfits to artificial conventions and fails at Zero-Shot Coordination with real people. We propose a framework for training teaming agents against Cognitively Constrained Partners: simulated partners whose diversity emerges from procedural constraints grounded in human physiological literature, specifically reaction delays and perceptual limitations, rather than from reward shaping or historical training checkpoints. In a modified Lunar Lander environment, we report preliminary evidence that these partners play more like real people than unconstrained agents do, and that agents trained against them match the strongest cross-play baselines while being the only ones whose performance does not depend on how their training partners were generated. We also describe a human-participant study designed so that its rankings can be compared directly against cross-play, testing how well that standard evaluation predicts real human-agent teaming.

</details>


### [88] [When and How Does Muon Precondition? The Role of Structured Gradient Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31728)
*Xiaoran Chen,Binghui Li,Liwei Wang,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从梯度噪声几何角度解释Muon优化器的预条件优势：经典各向同性噪声下Muon仅等价于学习率缩放、无加速；在异构任务频率的联想记忆小批量采样噪声下，Muon可将任务频率依赖由线性降为平方根，从而平衡频繁与稀有任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon已广泛用于大语言模型训练，但其理论基础薄弱，尤其缺乏对其预条件收益来源的机理理解。

Method: 理论分析结合实证：先在各向同性噪声假设下分析Muon与学习率缩放的关系；再构建刻画异构任务频率的联想记忆小批量采样结构化噪声模型，推导两任务精确平衡阈值并推广至多任务；同时分析SignSGD的旋转诱导负迁移与动量的降噪-跟踪权衡。

Result: 证明并验证各向同性噪声下Muon无加速；给出两任务精确平衡阈值及多任务下的任务覆盖与梯度可靠性联合作用规律；揭示Muon将任务频率依赖由线性软化为平方根；指出SignSGD存在旋转诱导负迁移而Muon可规避；受控实验验证上述机制，初步LLM预训练显示快慢动量组合的实用潜力。

Conclusion: Muon的收益依赖于梯度噪声的结构而非各向同性噪声；结构化噪声下其预条件可改善稀有任务学习并避免负迁移，为快慢动量等实践设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Muon has become a popular optimizer for large language model training, yet its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In this work, we study how gradient noise geometry shapes Muon's preconditioning benefits. We first show, theoretically and empirically, that under the classical isotropic noise assumption, Muon offers no acceleration beyond a rescaling of the learning rate. This motivates us to investigate structured noise arising from mini-batch sampling in associative memory with heterogeneous task frequencies. We establish an exact balancing threshold for two tasks and extend the analysis to multiple tasks, revealing how task coverage and gradient reliability jointly shape learning. Our analysis shows that Muon can soften the dependence on task frequency from linear to square-root, helping balance the learning of frequent and rare tasks. We further identify rotation-induced negative transfer in SignSGD that Muon avoids, and explain how momentum reduces sampling noise while tracking changing gradients. Controlled numerical experiments validate these mechanisms, and a preliminary LLM pretraining study illustrates the practical promise of fast-slow momentum.

</details>


### [89] [ConsMSA: Semantic Distribution Consistency Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31731)
*Pan Wang,Lipeng Ke,Huajun Ying,Pritish Mohapatra,Rohan Sarkar,Suresh Lakhani,Sankar Venkataraman,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConsMSA框架，将跨模态语义分布一致性显式形式化为$I^2CS$得分，并据此构建一致性正则化、自适应token重加权与冗余/冲突token剪枝三重机制，在三大MSA基准上实现SOTA且仅保留10% token仍具可比精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始多模态序列存在模态内与模态间的语义不一致（冗余或冲突），既阻碍鲁棒理解又增加计算开销，缺乏对语义分布一致性的显式建模。

Method: 将多模态token特征投影至共享情感空间，计算模态内与模态间一致性得分$I^2CS$；将该得分与预测相关性耦合形成一致性感知重要性信号，分别用作 (i) 训练期对齐隐分布的一致性正则项，(ii) 生成语义感知权重进行自适应token重加权，(iii) 作为剪枝冗余/冲突token的实用判据。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI与CH-SIMS上取得SOTA性能；在激进token压缩下仍保持鲁棒，仅保留10% token即可获得可比精度。

Conclusion: 语义分布一致性可作为协同提升预测鲁棒性与计算效率的有效基础，为MSA提供统一的一致性感知建模范式。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to predict human sentiments by integrating signals from different modalities such as text, video, and audio. However, raw multimodal sequences often suffer from semantic inconsistency--exhibiting redundancy or conflicts within and across modalities--which hinders robust understanding and increases computational cost. To this end, we introduce ConsMSA, which explicitly formalizes semantic distribution consistency across both $\textit{intra}$- and $\textit{inter}$-modality, providing a consistency-aware mechanism for robust and efficient multimodal sentiment prediction. Specifically, ConsMSA projects multimodal token features into a shared sentiment space to compute an Intra- and Inter-modality Consistency Score ($I^2CS$). By coupling this score with predictive relevance, we formulate consistency-aware importance signals that are utilized: (i) as a consistency regularizer to align latent distributions during training, (ii) to derive semantic-aware weights for adaptive multimodal token reweighting, and (iii) as a practical criterion to prune redundant or conflicting tokens. Extensive experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS demonstrate that ConsMSA achieves state-of-the-art performance while remaining robust under aggressive token compression--retaining only 10% of tokens yields comparable accuracy. These results establish semantic distribution consistency as a promising foundation for synergizing predictive robustness with computational efficiency.

</details>


### [90] [Learning to Be Monitored: Causal Bounds and Intervention-Aware Post-Training Recipes for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31733)
*Qiang Fu,Xinyue Li,Andre Wibisono,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出常用的干预式 CoT 可监控性指标 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 只是因果检测率的上界，可能高估监控器发现"由干预导致的目标行为"的比例；作者给出互补下界，构建更紧的评估框架，并基于配对控制-干预数据集用三种训练方法提升 LLM 的 CoT 可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT 监控用于从思维链中检测 LLM 的潜在有害行为。现有基于干预的评估用最小准则敏感度 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 衡量监控器能否从 CoT 中识别干预的作用，但在干预下观察到的目标行为不一定由干预引起，因此该指标可能高估监控器对"真正由干预引发的目标行为"的检测频率，缺乏因果层面的严谨性。

Method: 1）理论分析：证明 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 是因果检测率的上界，并推导互补下界，构成更紧的 CoT 可监控性评估框架。2）数据构建：构建覆盖多种干预类型的配对控制-干预任务训练数据集。3）训练方法：基于离策略自蒸馏、强化学习、同策略自蒸馏三种干预特定方法，为每个基座模型训练对应变体。

Result: 在多种模型-监控器配置下，基于扩展评估框架，三种训练方法相较对应基座模型普遍提升了 CoT 可监控性。

Conclusion: $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 会高估真实的因果检测率，需以上下界结合的框架评估 CoT 可监控性；同时 LLM 可通过针对性训练（离策略/同策略自蒸馏、强化学习）被"训练得更易被监控"，为提升模型可监控性提供了可行路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring aims to detect potentially harmful behaviors in large language models (LLMs) from their CoTs. Intervention-based evaluations measure this property by asking whether a monitor can detect the role of an intervention from the CoT when the targeted behavior occurs, typically using minimal-criterion sensitivity ($\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$) proposed by~\citep{guan2025monitoring}. However, a target behavior observed under intervention is not necessarily caused by it, and we show that this metric can overestimate how often a monitor detects target behaviors actually caused by the intervention. Specifically, we establish $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ as an upper bound on this causal detection rate and derive a complementary lower bound, which provides a tighter evaluation framework for CoT monitorability. With this framework, we study whether LLMs can be trained to make their CoTs easier to monitor. We first construct a training dataset of paired control--intervention tasks spanning multiple intervention types. Using the dataset, we design and apply three intervention-specific training methods based on off-policy self-distillation, reinforcement learning, and on-policy self-distillation to train separate variants of each base model. Across various model-monitor configurations, all three methods generally improve CoT monitorability over the corresponding base models under our extended evaluation framework. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyue-lxy/learning-to-be-monitored.

</details>


### [91] [Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Model Inference via Per-Query Modality Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31768)
*Shuqi Liu,Yuzhou Cao,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出查询级模态选择方法，在固定Omni-LLM的前提下为每个查询自适应选择模态，以在精度-成本权衡上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni-LLM联合处理文本、音频、视频等多模态可增强理解与推理，但推理成本高昂；且额外模态带来的收益因查询而异，固定的全模态配置并不高效。

Method: 将查询级模态选择建模为精度-成本优化问题，并采用成本敏感学习（cost-sensitive learning）方法进行模态选择，同时保持底层Omni-LLM不变。

Result: 在WorldSense上基于Qwen2.5-Omni与Qwen3-Omni，以输入token、延迟、金钱成本三种指标评估，查询级模态选择持续改善精度-成本权衡；Qwen2.5-Omni下达到或超过全模态精度，仅需约41%输入token、39%延迟、61%金钱成本。

Conclusion: 查询级模态选择是提升Omni-LLM推理效率的有效手段，可在多数场景下优于全模态输入的标准配置。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) can jointly process a broad range of modalities, such as text, audio, and video, enabling stronger multimodal understanding and reasoning capabilities, but often at substantial inference cost. We observe that the benefit of additional modalities varies across queries, indicating that a fixed modality configuration can be inefficient. Motivated by this, we study query-wise modality selection, which keeps the underlying Omni-LLM fixed while adaptively selecting which modalities to provide for each query. We formulate the problem as an accuracy-cost optimization problem and develop a cost-sensitive learning approach for modality selection. We evaluate our approach on WorldSense using Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni under three inference-cost measures: input tokens, latency, and monetary cost. Across both models, query-wise modality selection consistently improves the accuracy-cost trade-off and can even outperform the standard setting in which all available modalities are provided to the Omni-LLM. For Qwen2.5-Omni, our cost-sensitive approach matches or exceeds this full-modality accuracy while requiring only approximately 41% of the input tokens, 39% of the latency, and 61% of the monetary cost.

</details>


### [92] [Benchmarks Are Surprisingly Compaction-Friendly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31778)
*Jeanne Salle,Hardik Bhatnagar,Matthias Bethge,Sahar Abdelnabi,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在固定 scaffold（OpenCode）下系统评测 5 种 context compaction 策略，覆盖 3 个前沿模型与 3 个长程基准；发现 1M 上下文模型的性能对压缩阈值不敏感，而上下文窗口最小的模型在低阈值下性能下降，且没有任何压缩方法优于默认的自我摘要，压缩还会随次数单调丢弃约三分之一的防作弊规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 agentic 任务使 LLM agent 超出上下文窗口，需要 compaction，而该过程设计上是有损的，可能静默丢失任务约束、指令与早期决策理由。尽管 compaction 是编码 agent scaffold 的标准组件，但此前缺乏在前沿模型与真实长程任务上的系统研究，已有工作仅在玩具/合成轨迹上评测小型开放权重模型。

Method: 固定 agent scaffold（OpenCode），在其中实现 5 种 compaction 策略，并在三个长程基准（PaperBench、ProgramBench、PostTrainBench）上评测三个前沿模型；同时分析真实用户-智能体会话（SWE-chat）中被压缩掉的内容类型分布。

Result: 两个 1M 上下文模型的性能对压缩阈值无可测影响；上下文窗口最小的 GPT-5.3-Codex 在 PaperBench 上低阈值下表现更差（PostTrainBench 上趋势较弱）。无压缩方法优于默认自我摘要，丢弃内容更多的策略可使部分模型损失最高 20 分，而其他模型不受影响。ProgramBench 上首个压缩摘要平均丢失约三分之一的防作弊规则，并随压缩次数单调上升；SWE-chat 中关于"agent 应如何工作、不应做什么"的指令被丢弃的频率约为"要构建什么"的规格说明的 6 倍。

Conclusion: 现有基准是"对 compaction 友好"的：目标固定且 agent 进度落盘，因此被丢弃的信息大多可恢复，唯独那些不存在于其他位置的约束无法恢复。作者据此对基准设计与压缩策略提出反思。

Abstract: Long horizon agentic tasks are now pushing LLM agents past their context window. To continue the task, the agent scaffold has to free up the context window, a process known as context compaction. Compaction is generally lossy by design: agents can silently lose task constraints, instructions, or their reasoning behind earlier decisions. Although compaction is a standard component of coding agent scaffolds, it has not been studied systematically with frontier models on realistic, long horizon agentic tasks. Previous work evaluate smaller open weight models on toy or synthetic trajectories. We fix the agent scaffold (OpenCode), implement five compaction strategies inside it, and evaluate three frontier models on three long horizon benchmarks (PaperBench, ProgramBench, PostTrainBench). For the two 1M context models, the compaction threshold does not measurably affect the task performance, whereas GPT-5.3-Codex, the model with the smallest context window, performs worse at lower thresholds on PaperBench and, less clearly, on PostTrainBench. No compaction method outperforms default self-summarization, and methods that discard more content cost some models up to 20 points while leaving other models the same. Compaction can, however, silently remove task constraints: on ProgramBench, the first compaction summary already drops about one third of the task's anticheating rules on average, and this rises monotonically with the number of compactions. On real user-agent sessions from SWE-chat, instructions about how the agent should work and what it should not do are dropped around 6 times more than specifications of what to build. We argue that current benchmarks are compaction-friendly: i.e., the objective is fixed and the agent's progress stays on disk, so most of what compaction discards can be recovered, except constraints that don't exist anywhere else.

</details>


### [93] [Not All Retained Clients Are Equal: Client Selection for Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31786)
*Thanh Linh Nguyen,Quoc-Viet Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDF：一种路径依赖的联邦遗忘客户端选择框架，利用CLP加权历史挑选与目标弱耦合的保留客户端，实现高效遗忘与保持效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法将遗忘视为事后、无状态操作，忽略了客户端影响力的路径依赖性（尤其关键学习期CLP的贡献）；同时知识重叠与客户端间歇可用性使依赖全部保留客户端效率低下且不切实际。

Method: 训练期间维护轻量、与目标无关、CLP加权的历史记录，汇总每个客户端的累积更新方向与全局模型对齐度；收到遗忘请求后，据此识别并排序"全局信息量大但与目标弱耦合"的保留客户端，仅用top-k客户端进行少量轮次的重训练式遗忘。

Result: 在FMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100的IID与非IID设置下验证：非IID CIFAR-10上实现0%遗忘精度且MIA AUC接近重训练水平；CIFAR-100保留精度较最强基线最高提升6.91个百分点；相较Retrain通信降低约69%，计算降低57%–61%。

Conclusion: 保留客户端对遗忘的价值并不均等；充分利用路径依赖信息进行客户端选择，可在显著减少保留客户端数量与通信/计算开销的同时实现有效遗忘与强保留效用。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) aims to remove a designated target client's influence from a trained model without the prohibitive cost of retraining from scratch. Existing methods largely treat unlearning as a post-hoc, stateless operation, overlooking that client influence is path-dependent: contributions made during training, particularly during critical learning periods (CLPs), can have disproportionate and persistent effects. Moreover, knowledge overlap and intermittent client availability make relying on all retained clients inefficient and impractical. We introduce PDF, a path-dependent client-selection framework based on the insight that retained clients are not equally valuable for unlearning. During training, PDF maintains lightweight, target-agnostic, CLP-weighted histories summarizing each client's accumulated update direction and global-model alignment. Upon an unlearning request, these histories identify and rank retained clients that are globally informative yet weakly coupled to the target, enabling retraining-based unlearning with only the top-$k$ clients over a small number of rounds. Experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 under IID and non-IID settings show that PDF achieves effective forgetting and strong retained utility with substantially fewer retained clients. In particular, PDF achieves $0\%$ forgetting accuracy with retraining-like MIA AUC on non-IID CIFAR-10, improves retained accuracy by up to $6.91$ percentage points over the strongest competing FU baseline on CIFAR-100, and reduces communication by approximately $69\%$ and computation by $57$--$61\%$ relative to Retrain.

</details>


### [94] [PaperScorer: How Predictable Is Paper Acceptance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31791)
*Sachin Konan,Jonathan Liu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PaperScorer：仅凭论文文本或页面截图即可预测同行评审的录用/拒稿决定，视觉（页面图像）模型预测力最强（ICLR 68%、arXiv ML 77% 准确率），优于纯文本与前沿推理模型，且在低分与高分论文上表现最好、置信度校准良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究的评测多依赖基准分数，但研究价值难以归结为单一分数；人类通过同行评审判断整体贡献，而评审噪声大、影响因素多、难以建模。作者因此追问：在仅给定论文的条件下，能否直接预测其同行评审决定。

Method: 构建 PaperScorer 模型族，从论文提取的文本或页面截图中预测录用/拒稿；训练时去除作者身份与排版捷径，仅使用最终决定作为标签；并对比视觉模型、纯文本模型与前沿推理模型，另做鲁棒性实验（更小模型、将全文压缩为单张图像）。

Result: 视觉模型预测力最强：ICLR 论文 68%、arXiv 机器学习论文 77% 准确率，优于纯文本与前沿推理模型；页面图像保留图表、版式与呈现信息，该信号稳健，在更小模型乃至单图压缩下依然有效；预测准确性分布不均，在最低与最高评分论���上最强，中间评分论文对本文模型与前沿系统均不可预测；置信度经过校准，最自信的预测也最准确。

Conclusion: PaperScorer 表明论文本身承载了相当一部分评审决定信息，是迈向 AI 驱动研究评测的第一步。

Abstract: Automated research is largely evaluated by benchmark performance, but the value of research rarely reduces to a single score. Humans judge a work's holistic contribution through peer review, which is hard to model because it is noisy and depends on many factors. However, what if, given only the paper, we could predict its peer-review decision directly? We introduce PaperScorer, a family of models that predict a paper's accept or reject decision from its extracted text or page screenshots, training only on the decision after removing author identity and formatting shortcuts. Vision models are the most predictive (68% accuracy on ICLR papers and 77% on ML papers from arXiv), beating text-only and frontier reasoning models. Unlike plain text, page images retain figures, layout, and presentation. This signal is robust, surviving smaller models and even compressing the whole paper into a single image. But accurate predictions are not evenly spread. PaperScorer is strongest on the lowest and highest rated papers, while papers rated in the middle stay unpredictable for our models and frontier systems alike. Additionally, its confidence is calibrated, so its most confident predictions are also its most accurate. Overall, PaperScorer shows how much of the decision a paper carries on its own, marking a first step toward AI-driven research evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [CuraLoc: Repository-Level Fault Localization via Planned Code Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31809)
*Sangeeta Warrier,Anush Sankaran,Ryen White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuraLoc 将仓库级缺陷定位中的代码检查决策前置为一次性规划：先用紧凑的行为/结构描述表示候选文件符号，再由模型单次决策选出起始符号、待检查的关系与编辑假设，最后用确定性检索收集源码并据此排序。在 SWE-Bench Verified 上 Func@1 达 59.3%，较 LocAgent 提升且 token 减少 90.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷报告只描述问题现象，很少指出代码应修改的位置。现有仓库级定位方法多依赖语言模型迭代决定下一步检查哪些代码，导致重复模型调用与大量 token 消耗，推理开销高。

Method: CuraLoc 把"检查决策"从检索过程中解耦、前移到检索之前：1) 先筛选候选文件，并用紧凑的行为与结构描述表示其符号；2) 在单次规划决策中，模型联合选择起始符号、指定从每个符号出发要检查的代码关系、并形成可能的编辑假设；3) 确定性检索按请求收集源码，并利用这些假设指导最终排序。模型推理集中于"选择与比较代码"，而非逐步指挥每次检索。

Result: 在 SWE-Bench Verified（GPT-5.2）上，Func@1 由 LocAgent 的 53.8% 提升至 59.3%，记录 token 用量减少 90.3%，并优于 RepoSearcher（56.2%）与 Agentless（39.6%）。在 SWE-bench Lite 上实现 83.8% 的 Func@5，相对 Agentless 提升超过 15%。方法支持不同模型骨干，在 GPT-5.2 与 GPT-6 Astra 上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仓库级代码检查可以被有效"预先规划"，无需依赖迭代式、由模型控制的导航，即可在显著降低 token 开销的同时获得更高的定位精度。

Abstract: A bug report describes what went wrong, but rarely where the code should change. Existing repository-level localization methods often let language models iteratively decide what code to inspect next, requiring repeated model calls and substantial token usage. We investigate whether these inspection decisions can instead be planned before source retrieval. In this research, we introduce CuraLoc, which first selects candidate files and represents their symbols using compact behavioral and structural descriptions. In a single planning decision, the model jointly selects starting symbols, chooses code relationships to inspect from each, and forms hypotheses about likely edits. Deterministic retrieval collects the requested source for a final ranking informed by those hypotheses, concentrating model reasoning on choosing and comparing code rather than directing each retrieval step. We demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed approach on the SWE-Bench Lite and Verified datasets. On SWE Bench Verified with GPT-5.2, CuraLoc raises Func@1 from LocAgent's 53.8% to 59.3% while using 90.3% fewer recorded tokens, and also outperforms RepoSearcher (56.2%) and Agentless (39.6%) on Func@1. On SWE-bench Lite, CuraLoc achieves a high 83.8% Func@5 accuracy improving on Agentless by more than 15% in relative accuracy. Additionally, CuraLoc supports different model backbones, with strong localization accuracy demonstrated using both GPT-5.2 and GPT-6 Astra. These results provide evidence that repository-level code inspection can be planned effectively without iterative model-controlled navigation.

</details>


### [96] [Nested Sparsity for Scalable Pretraining and Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31818)
*Tianzhe Chu,Druv Pai,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Nestor，通过在注意力算子中引入与MoE稀疏性可嵌套组合的条件计算机制，在等算力等稀疏度下优于传统MoE Transformer，并在长上下文与持续预训练中表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件计算几乎只体现在MoE算子（稀疏化MLP）上，注意力算子缺乏类似机制；作者希望探索可类比推广到注意力、并能与MoE稀疏性层级组合的新条件计算形式。

Method: 将条件计算推广到注意力算子，构造与MoE稀疏性自然嵌套（层次组合）的稀疏机制，得到Nestor架构；在语言模型预训练中与等算力、等稀疏度的传统MoE Transformer对比，并考察持续预训练场景（长上下文适配、新领域迁移与旧领域遗忘）。

Result: 等算力等稀疏度下，Nestor预训练表现优于传统MoE；持续预训练中更有效地适配更长上下文，迁移到新领域更高效且对旧领域遗忘更少；随模型规模、数据规模与上下文长度增长，这些优势持续存在。

Conclusion: 除MoE之外的条件计算机制对深层网络架构具有潜力，嵌套稀疏是一种有效的设计方向；代码与检查点已开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers, the standard backbone for modern large-scale deep learning, employ conditional computation: only a sparse subset of model weights is used in each forward pass. In MoE transformers, this sparsity arises in the MoE operator, which generalizes the multi-layer perceptron in the classical transformer. In this work, we explore an alternative mechanism for conditional computation that analogously generalizes the attention operator. The sparsity induced by this mechanism naturally nests (i.e., hierarchically composes) with the sparsity induced by the MoE operator. We show that a transformer employing this nested sparsity mechanism, which we call Nestor, performs better than a conventional MoE transformer at equal compute and equal sparsity in language model pretraining. We also show that Nestor is well-suited for continual pretraining: it can be adapted to longer contexts more effectively than conventional MoEs, as well as adapt more efficiently to new domains while forgetting less on old domains. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these benefits persist as model size, dataset size, and context length grow. Our results demonstrate the potential of alternative conditional computation mechanisms for deep neural network architectures. Code and checkpoints are available on GitHub at https://github.com/Ma-Lab-Berkeley/Nestor .

</details>


### [97] [Speculative Diffusion is a Tale of Two Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31819)
*Aaron Zorel,Guy Van den Broeck,Aditya Grover,Daniel Israel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究以扩散模型为草稿模型的推测解码中"扩散努力程度"与"接受宽松度"两个参数的联合调节，通过遍历两者组合刻画完整的速度-质量Pareto前沿，发现联合变化可扩展前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码对验证器分布是无损的，而扩散LLM（dLLM）可通过降低扩散努力换取速度但损失质量。既然推测解码本身也可通过更宽松的接受准则实现有损加速，两种"以质换速"机制之间存在可探索的权衡空间，而此前工作多只单独变化其中之一。

Method: 以扩散LLM作为草稿模型，将扩散努力（diffusion effort，如去噪步数）与推测解码接受宽松度（acceptance leniency，相对验证器分布的接受准则放宽程度）作为两个可控参数，联合遍历其取值组合，实验测量各组合下的速度与质量，从而刻画可达的Pareto前沿。

Result: 实验表明，与仅单步扩散草稿（single step diffusion drafting）相比，联合变化扩散努力与接受宽松度可将速度-质量Pareto前沿向外扩展，即获得更优的速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 推测扩散中的有损加速不应仅依赖扩散侧的努力削减或推测侧的接受放宽单一手段，两参数的协同调节能解锁更优的Pareto前沿，为扩散草稿推测解码的实际部署提供更灵活的速度-质量配置选择。

Abstract: Speculative decoding is a technique that accelerates the inference speed of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) by sampling from a faster, lower quality draft model. Traditionally, a fundamental characteristic of speculative decoding is that it is lossless with respect to the verifier distribution, guaranteeing speed without sacrificing quality. This differs from recently developed diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which are notably able to achieve substantial speedups by degrading the quality of their outputs with less diffusion effort. Because diffusion models are able to sample faster than autoregressive models by generating tokens in parallel, they are considered strong candidates for draft models in speculative diffusion. While it is widely understood that diffusion models can sacrifice quality for speed, speculative decoding itself can also be generalized to generate lossy samples faster using more lenient acceptance criteria with respect to the verifier distribution. Presented with the two parameters of diffusion effort and speculative decoding leniency, this paper explores the full achievable Pareto frontier via joint variations of these parameters. Our experiments show that jointly varying diffusion effort and acceptance leniency can extend the speed-quality Pareto frontier over single step diffusion drafting.

</details>


### [98] [Rethinking Multi-View Fusion for Urban Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31850)
*Sean Bin Yang,Zongyi Xu,Jilin Hu,Ying Sun,Kristian Torp,Bin Yang,Torben Bach Pedersen,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRISM自适应多视图框架，用稀疏MoE路由实现区域特定视图分配，并用PID式调节机制缓解梯度冲突，提升城市区域表示学习的稳定性与均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域表示学习依赖全局共享融合与联合优化，隐含假设各模态重要性一致、多目标训练自然均衡；但实证分析发现两个结构性问题：一是模态效用随区域显著变化，静态融合会过度强调某些模态；二是异质目标存在持续的梯度不平衡，导致表示偏置和训练不稳定。

Method: PRISM框架包含两部分：将稀疏MoE（混合专家）路由适配于多视图学习，学习区域特定的视图分配（自适应融合）；引入受PID（比例-积分-微分）启发的调节机制，在优化过程中缓解梯度冲突。

Result: 在三个大规模城市数据集上，PRISM与强多视图基线相比取得有竞争力的性能，同时表现出更稳定的优化行为和更均衡的区域表示。

Conclusion: 针对城市数据的异质性，区域自适应视图分配与梯度冲突调节是提升多视图区域表示学习有效性与稳定性的关键，PRISM为此提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Urban region representation learning encodes heterogeneous urban signals, such as mobility flows, points of interest, and land-use patterns, into unified embeddings for downstream tasks, including check-in prediction, crime forecasting, and service demand estimation. Existing approaches rely on globally shared fusion and joint optimization, implicitly assuming uniform modality importance and naturally balanced multi-objective training. However, urban data is inherently heterogeneous. Our empirical analysis reveals two structural issues: (i) modality utility varies markedly across regions, causing static fusion to overemphasize signals from certain modalities; and (ii) heterogeneous objectives exhibit persistent gradient imbalance, causing representational bias and unstable training. We present PRISM, a self-adjusting framework for adaptive multi-view learning. PRISM adapts sparse MoE routing to learn region-specific view allocations, while a PID (Proportional Integral Derivative)-inspired regulation mechanism mitigates gradient conflicts during optimization. Experiments on three large-scale urban datasets show that PRISM achieves competitive performance compared with strong multi-view baselines, while providing more stable optimization behavior and more balanced region representations.

</details>


### [99] [Cautious Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31855)
*Damian Hattler,Qiang Liu,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Cautious ConFIG，以三行代码扩展修复 ConFIG 在 Adam 等自适应优化器中丧失的无冲突更新保证，在三个 PINN 基准上稳定优于原始 ConFIG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 训练为多目标优化，边界、初值与残差损失间存在梯度冲突；ConFIG 可消除冲突，但与 Adam 等自适应优化器结合时，优化器对无冲突方向再做处理，最终权重更新不再相对任务梯度无冲突。

Method: Cautious ConFIG：在 ConFIG 生成的无冲突方向基础上加入谨慎掩码（cautious masking），仅保留与原始任务梯度方向一致的更新分量，以三行代码实现，使权重更新前恢复无冲突性质；并用简单 toy 系统分析掩码的稳定作用。

Result: 在 1D 粘性 Burgers 方程、1D 非线性薛定谔方程、2D Kovasznay 流三个基准上一致优于标准 ConFIG；toy 实验揭示谨慎掩码的稳定化效应及其对冲突强度、任务数量与优化器滞后的响应规律。

Conclusion: 与自适应优化器耦合会破坏 ConFIG 的无冲突保证，轻量级谨慎掩码可经验性恢复该保证并带来稳定一致的效果，为 PINN 多目标训练提供简洁有效的改进。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) are indispensable tools for modeling complex phenomena in physics and engineering. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to traditional numerical solvers by directly incorporating physical constraints into neural network loss functions. Despite their advantages, PINN training requires multi-objective optimization, which often leads to significant gradient conflicts among boundary, initial, and residual loss terms. The Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient (ConFIG) method addresses these conflicts; however, integrating ConFIG with adaptive optimizers such as Adam introduces a critical vulnerability: the conflict-free direction generated by ConFIG is further processed by the optimizer, and the resulting weight update may no longer be conflict-free with respect to the task gradients. To address this issue, Cautious ConFIG is proposed as a concise three-line extension to the ConFIG algorithm that empirically restores the conflict-freeness guarantee prior to the weight update. The method is evaluated on three PINN benchmarks: the 1D Viscous Burgers' equation, the 1D Nonlinear Schrödinger equation, and the 2D Kovasznay flow. Experimental results demonstrate consistent performance improvements over the standard ConFIG baseline. Additionally, the stabilizing effect of cautious masking and its reaction to conflict severity, task number, and optimizer lag are investigated using a simple toy system.

</details>


### [100] [Aligning Large Language Models with Human Feedback: Mathematical Foundations and Algorithm Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31862)
*Siliang Zeng,Luca Viano,Chenliang Li,Jiaxiang Li,Volkan Cevher,Markus Wulfmeier,Stefano Ermon,Alfredo Garcia,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 综述大语言模型对齐的数学基础与算法框架，涵盖SFT、RLHF、DPO，并从逆强化学习与离散选择模型视角提供理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要与人类意图、偏好和价值观对齐，但相关方法分散于监督微调、强化学习与偏好学习等多个体系，缺乏统一的理论与数学刻画；研究者与实践者也缺少兼顾理论基础与实现指导的系统性资源。

Method: 以教程式（tutorial）综述方式展开：介绍标准对齐技术SFT、RLHF与DPO；建立从人类偏好学习的理论联系，包括逆强化学习（IRL）与离散选择模型；并给出具体算法的实现要点。

Result: 梳理了主流对齐算法的数学原理与算法流程，明确其优势与局限性，并提供实用实现层面的洞见，形成层次化的知识框架。

Conclusion: 该文为研究人员与实践者提供关于对齐方法的综合性基础理解，以及开发更鲁棒、更可扩展对齐技术的框架性指导。

Abstract: This article provides an introduction to the mathematical foundations and algorithmic frameworks used to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human intentions, preferences, and values. We discuss standard alignment techniques, such as fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF), and direct preference optimization (DPO). We also explore the theoretical underpinnings of learning from human preferences, drawing connections to inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and discrete choice models. We present state-of-the-art algorithms in a tutorial style, discuss their advantages and limitations, and offer insights into practical implementation. Our exposition is intended to serve as a comprehensive resource for researchers and practitioners, providing both a foundational understanding of alignment methodologies and a framework for developing more robust and scalable alignment techniques.

</details>


### [101] [Rewriting Scaling Laws for Synthetic Data with Compute and Data Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31867)
*Nicholas Roberts,Ahmed Ahmed,Suhas Kotha,Percy Liang,Yu Wang,Alex Renda,Nitya Mani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“重写缩放律”，将损失建模为模型规模、训练token数、重写token占比与自然数据epoch数的函数，并把生成重写的算力计入总开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成重写已成为预训练语料的标配，但两个关键问题缺乏理解：应为重写训练数据投入多少算力；以及以重写数据训练的模型能否超越重写器本身。

Method: 在两种设定下拟合该缩放律：算力受限设定（自然数据充足，重写器为算力更高的第三方模型）；数据受限设定（仅有固定种子集，重写器与目标模型同规模并在种子集上预训练）。

Result: 算力受限设定下，最优重写占比随算力上升：3e18 FLOPs时不足10%，1e22 FLOPs时达32%；外推33倍FLOPs训练的22B模型，最终损失与预测误差仅1.1%，且在多个网络域上以重写器1/10的训练算力取得更优BPB。数据受限设定下最优重写占比同样随算力上升，最高至3B的各规模上，用同规模重写器重写自身预训练数据训练的模型均优于纯自然数据的最佳运行。

Conclusion: 合成重写可通过缩放律视角研究：最优方法学选择是可预测的，且缩放不会因超出重写器能力而失效。

Abstract: Synthetic rewrites, where a language model rewrites a natural document e.g. into a target style, are now a standard ingredient of pretraining corpora. However, critical aspects of their use remain poorly understood, such as how much compute should be spent rewriting training data and whether a model trained on rewrites can outperform the rewriter. We study these two questions by proposing a rewriting scaling law that predicts loss from model size, training tokens, the fraction of training tokens that are rewrites, and the number of epochs over the natural data, charging the compute spent generating rewrites. We fit this scaling law in two settings. In the compute-limited setting, natural data is plentiful and rewrites come from a third-party rewriter trained with more compute than our largest run. We find that the estimated optimal rewrite fraction increases with compute, from under $10\%$ at $3\times10^{18}$ FLOPs to $32\%$ at $10^{22}$ FLOPs. Our scaling forecast extrapolates $33\times$ in FLOPs to train a 22B-parameter model, whose final loss is within $1.1\%$ of our prediction. This model achieves a better BPB than its rewriter on several web domains at one-tenth the rewriter's training compute. In the data-limited setting, we assume we only have a fixed seed set of natural data and that our rewriter is a model of the same size as the target, pretrained on that seed set. Again, we find the optimal rewrite fraction increases with the compute budget. We also demonstrate that a model trained on rewrites of its own pretraining data, generated by a rewriter of the same size, beats the best run on natural data alone at every model size up to 3B. Our results show that synthetic rewriting can be studied through the scaling law lens: that optimal methodological choices are predictable and scaling does not necessarily break down beyond the rewriter's capabilities.

</details>


### [102] [Forecasting Associations, Not Interventions: A Preregistered Causal Audit of Covariate-Aware Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31869)
*Zong-Han Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 协变量感知时间序列基础模型（TSFM）被用于what-if干预式查询时，其回答实际是条件斜率∂E[Y|X]而非干预效应∂E[Y|do(X)]；理论、行为与表征三层次证据一致，并通过少量干预式后训练显著缩小混淆差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM接受控制变量的未来路径并返回预测，诱使用户进行what-if（干预）查询，但模型仅在观测数据上训练。作者试图判定这类模型给出的效应估计究竟是关联性的条件斜率还是因果性的干预效应，即模型是否具备因果理解。

Method: 1) 理论：分析Bayes最优观测预测器，并在in-context最小二乘下推导混淆差距（观测情境效果与随机化情境效果之差）的闭式表达，作为无参数参考线。2) 行为：在含精确反事实真值的预注册网格上测试Chronos-2、TiRex-2、TabPFN-TS、TimesFM-2.5，覆盖线性与非线性结构模型，并构造观测律相同的模型对以排除可识别性限制。3) 表征：用线性探针从嵌入中解码数据生成机制与真实效应。4) 干预式后训练：在3000条合成序列上做数分钟的微调，结合replay与低秩适配。

Result: 所有模型均贴合推导的理论参考线（R² 0.90–1.00），且比回归本身更"混淆"（斜率1.3–1.8）。相同观测律的模型对表明并非可识别性极限：效应可识别时最小二乘恢复100%真实效应差，Chronos-2为75%，TiRex-2为34%。线性探针可从被预测忽略的嵌入中以94–96%解码生成机制、以R² 0.7–0.9解码真实效应。两个随机驱动器的物理系统上，预训练模型响应不足2–5倍且忽略窗口内混淆。干预式后训练将差距最多削减83%，并可迁移至训练先验之外。回放模型自身预训练先验可将MASE代价由+47%降至+10%，配合低秩适配在三随机种子下达到预注册标准（差距减少52–61%，代价+1–3%），同时提升DoTime基准。

Conclusion: 协变量感知TSFM的干预式回答本质上是关联性的，存在系统性混淆偏差，但该偏差具有可预测的闭式结构；它部分源于表征未被利用，可通过少量干预式后训练配合回放与低秩适配以极小预测代价有效缓解，且具迁移性。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time series foundation models (TSFMs) accept a future path for a control variable and return a forecast, inviting what-if queries. The query is interventional; the models are trained on observational data. We determine whether their answers are the conditional slope $\partial E[Y|X]$ or the interventional effect $\partial E[Y|do(X)]$, at three levels. Theory: a Bayes-optimal observational predictor answers interventional queries associationally unless the adjustment set is available and used; for in-context least squares we derive the resulting confounding gap -- a model's effect estimate under an observational minus under a randomized context -- in closed form, a parameter-free reference line. Behaviour: on a preregistered grid with exact counterfactual ground truth, Chronos-2, TiRex-2, TabPFN-TS and TimesFM-2.5 all track this line ($R^2$ 0.90-1.00) and respond more confounded than regression itself (slopes 1.3-1.8), on linear and nonlinear structural models.
