Pairs of models with identical observational laws prove this is not an identifiability limit: when the effect is identifiable, least squares recovers 100% of the true effect difference, Chronos-2 75%, TiRex-2 34%. Representation: linear probes decode the data-generating regime (94-96%) and the true effect ($R^2$ 0.7-0.9) from embeddings that the forecasts ignore. On two physical systems with randomized actuators, pretrained models under-respond by 2-5x and ignore in-window confounding. Minutes of interventional post-training on 3,000 synthetic series cut the gap by up to 83% and transfer outside the training prior. Its forecasting cost is a replay-distribution effect: replaying the model's own pretraining prior cuts it from +47% to +10% MASE, and with low-rank adaptation yields a recipe meeting the preregistered criterion over three seeds (52-61% gap reduction at +1-3% cost) while improving the DoTime benchmark.

</details>


### [103] [Effectiveness of Curriculum Learning Depends on Reward Sparsity and Competing Optima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31884)
*John J. Vastola,Ann Huang,Satpreet H. Singh,Samuel J. Gershman,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文构建并分析一个极简策略学习模型（开环分幕式目标拦截任务），从数学与数值上分析REINFORCE在稠密/稀疏奖励下的学习动力学，解释课程学习为何在强化学习中远比对监督学习有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习（先在简单任务上训练）在生物学习中有效，但在人工设置中效果差异大，且在强化学习中显著优于监督学习，其成因缺乏机制性解释。

Method: 设计一个开环、分幕式目标拦截任务，其参数（尤其"接近目标多远才能获得有信息奖励"）可调节，从而产生稠密或稀疏奖励；对两种情形下REINFORCE策略学习动力学进行解析推导与数值实验，并引入"最小化努力"与"最大化奖励"的冲突目标以形成相互竞争的不同奖励景观最优。

Result: 稠密奖励下课程收益甚微；稀疏奖励下课程学习几乎是学习发生的前提。当存在努力最小化与奖励积累的冲突目标时尤为明显，因为会产生相互竞争的多个奖励景观最优。

Conclusion: 有效课程在该场景中通过两种机制起作用：(i) 降低奖励稀疏性以加速学习；(ii) 重塑奖励景观，使智能体远离糟糕的局部最优。这为课程学习在RL与监督学习间的效果差异提供了机理层面的解释。

Abstract: While animals and people tend to learn a task more quickly and reliably when they first train on simpler versions of it, curriculum learning's effectiveness in artificial settings varies, and appears substantially greater in reinforcement learning than in supervised learning. In this work, we construct and analyze a minimal model of policy learning to better understand why. We consider an open-loop, episodic target interception task whose parameters---especially how close the agent must be to the target to receive an informative reward signal---can be chosen so that the agent either receives informative rewards frequently ('dense' rewards) or rarely ('sparse' rewards). We mathematically and numerically analyze the dynamics of REINFORCE-based policy learning in both cases. In the dense reward case, curricula have only marginal benefits; in the sparse case, curricula can be required for learning to occur at all. We find that this is especially true when the agent has the conflicting goals of minimizing effort and accumulating high reward, since this can produce distinct reward landscape optima which compete with one another. Our characterization of useful curricula in this setting indicates that they address two issues: they (i) make rewards less sparse to speed up learning, and (ii) shape the reward landscape to steer agents away from bad local optima.

</details>


### [104] [Scaling Transformer Energy-based Models For High Quality Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31889)
*Jungsoo Lee,Alexi Gladstone,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Transformer Energy-Based Model (TEM)，在潜空间内构建可扩展的Transformer能量函数，配合多层能量求和、正表示对齐(PRA)与改进采样策略，首次使对比式EBM在高分辨率图像生成上达到高保真水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBM将概率建模与生成过程解耦，天然支持组合式生成与推理时缩放，但高维采样困难与深层架构训练不稳定使其长期局限于低维场景，作者旨在突破这两大瓶颈。

Method: 以Transformer作为能量主干并完全在潜空间中运作；从多个网络层抽取能量并求和，融合局部纹理到全局语义的互补约束；引入Positive Representation Alignment (PRA)，用视觉基础模型监督TEM的中间表示；并结合先进的推理期采样策略提升样本质量。

Result: TEM-XL在类条件ImageNet 256×256上无分类器引导取得FID-50K 3.82；ImageNet 512×512上FID-50K 8.24，表明方法可扩展至更高分辨率。

Conclusion: 对比式EBM能够生成高保真图像并具备良好扩展性，同时验证了EBM的核心优势：组合式生成、零样本修复与分布外检测。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) are a flexible class of generative models that decouple probabilistic modeling from the generation process. This decoupling directly enables compositional generation and inference-time scaling, where sample quality can be refined simply by increasing sampling steps. However, severe challenges in high-dimensional sampling and training instabilities with deep architectures have restricted EBMs to low-dimensional regimes. To resolve these bottlenecks, we introduce the Transformer Energy-Based Model (TEM), a scalable transformer energy function operating entirely within a latent space. On top of this backbone, we extract energies at multiple layers and sum them, composing complementary constraints from local texture to global semantics. Furthermore, two components make this high-quality at scale: Positive Representation Alignment (PRA), which supervises TEM's intermediate representations with a vision foundation model, and advanced sampling strategies that improve sample quality at inference. TEM-XL achieves an FID-50K of 3.82 on class-conditional ImageNet 256$\times$256 without classifier-free guidance, showing that a contrastive EBM can generate high-fidelity images. On ImageNet 512$\times$512, TEM achieves an FID-50K of 8.24, demonstrating that the approach scales to higher resolution. We further validate the core promises of EBMs: compositional generation, zero-shot inpainting, and out-of-distribution detection.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Structured Sparsity for Large Models: Bridging Model Compressibility and Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31897)
*Tianxiang Xia,Kai Lion,Niao He,Michael Muehlebach,Melanie Zeilinger,Hao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 S-SALAAD 统一框架，在训练中联合学习权重及其低秩与结构化稀疏分量，配合预算感知压缩与专用 CPU/GPU 内核，实现单检查点在多内存预算下兼具低内存与低延迟的弹性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型规模扩张带来算力与内存需求，推高对云推理的依赖，但网络不可靠、通信延迟与隐私要求使云端不切实际，故需边缘本地部署。本地部署要求模型在有限内存下高效执行。已有工作用"稀疏+低秩"表示实现单检查点弹性压缩到多内存预算，但参数减少不等于推理加速：非结构化稀疏的索引开销和不规则内存访问会抵消计算节省。这是关键部署障碍。

Method: 提出 S-SALAAD 统一框架：训练时联合学习模型权重及其低秩分量与结构化稀疏分量；支持一族硬件友好的结构化稀疏模式；将结构学习与预算感知压缩耦合；并配套专用的 CPU 与 GPU 内核，利用所学结构进行加速，从而由单一检查点服务多种内存预算。

Result: 在 Llama2 与 Qwen3 上（涵盖预训练与蒸馏）实验表明，相较被评估基线，S-SALAAD 在困惑度、内存与推理延迟三维度上推进了经验帕累托前沿，且跨多种硬件平台成立。

Conclusion: 结构化学习可将弹性可压缩性转化为更低的内存占用与推理延迟，使大模型在边缘设备上的实际部署成为可能。

Abstract: Scaling laws continue to reward increases in model scale, while the resulting computational and memory demands drive greater reliance on cloud infrastructure for inference. Yet unreliable network connectivity, communication delays, and privacy requirements can make such reliance impractical, motivating local execution on edge devices. Local deployment requires models to fit within limited memory and execute efficiently with the available computational resources. Recent work has shown that learning sparse-plus-low-rank representations enables elastic compression of a single checkpoint to multiple memory budgets. However, fewer parameters do not necessarily translate into faster inference. Indexing overhead and irregular memory access in unstructured sparse components can offset computational savings. To address this critical deployment barrier, we introduce S-SALAAD, a unified framework that jointly learns the model weights and their low-rank and structured sparse components during training. The framework supports a family of hardware-compatible structured sparsity patterns. It couples this structure learning with budget-aware compression and dedicated CPU and GPU kernels that exploit the learned structure for acceleration, enabling efficient inference from a single checkpoint across memory budgets. Experiments on Llama2 and Qwen3 models, covering both pretraining and distillation, show that S-SALAAD advances the empirical Pareto frontier in perplexity, memory, and inference latency across multiple hardware platforms relative to the evaluated baselines. These results show that structured learning can translate elastic compressibility into reduced memory requirements and lower inference latency, enabling practical deployment of large models on edge devices.

</details>


### [106] [Topological Equivalence of Spectral and Density Cluster Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31909)
*Sajjad Hashemian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较从密度函数恢复层次聚类的两种范式——密度聚类树（超水平集的连通分量）与扩散距离下单链接得到的谱层次，并给出两者在适当扩散时间下拓扑等价（高度单调重标定意义下）的条件，进而说明k-NN图实现可由扩散距离的一致近似继承该等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度聚类树与基于拉普拉斯的谱/扩散层次是两类典型层次聚类构造，但二者关系缺乏理论刻画；作者希望建立二者在拓扑层面的等价性，从而把谱图构造问题归约为密度水平集拓扑问题。

Method: 对比分析：一侧为密度f的超水平集{f≥λ}的连通分量构成的密度聚类树；另一侧为由拉普拉斯算子诱导的扩散距离、对其施加单链接得到的谱层次。通过设定条件（适当的扩散时间）证明扩散层次与密度聚类树在高度单调重标定下拓扑等价；并利用扩散距离的一致近似，将结论推广至k-NN图实现。

Result: 在给定条件下，适当扩散时间处的扩散层次与密度聚类树拓扑等价（至多相差高度的单调重标定）；k-NN图实现因一致近似扩散距离而继承该等价性，得到从谱图构造到密度水平集拓扑的有限样本归约。

Conclusion: 谱（扩散）层次与密度聚类树在理论上可视为同一层次结构的两种表示，k-NN实现保持该等价性，从而为谱图聚类提供密度水平集视角的理论依据与有限样本保证。

Abstract: We compare two canonical ways to recover hierarchical clustering from an underlying density $f$: the \emph{density cluster tree} given by connected components of superlevel sets $\{f\geλ\}$, and a \emph{spectral hierarchy} obtained by applying single linkage to diffusion distances induced by a Laplacian operator. We give conditions under which, at an appropriate diffusion time, the diffusion hierarchy is \emph{topologically equivalent} to the density cluster tree up to a monotone rescaling of heights. It further shows that a $k$-NN graph implementation inherits this equivalence from uniform approximation of diffusion distances, yielding a finite-sample reduction from spectral graph constructions to density-level-set topology.

</details>


### [107] [Decode Late, Multiply Once: Rethinking the Dot Product for Quantized Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31913)
*Jonathan Tapson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将低比特权重点积重构为“按权重码寻址累加、末尾一次性译码乘”的精确等价算法，使乘法次数下降为原来的1/2^k，并配套设计了scatter-accumulate处理单元（SA-PE）用于权重驻留脉动阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特神经网络（1-4 bit权重、16/32 bit激活）已广泛部署，但点积仍为每个元素配一个乘法器，乘法资源与算术能耗未随权重位宽降低而受益。作者希望把“码字→权重值”的译码从逐元素前置步骤改为末尾融合步骤，从而在保持数值精确的前提下减少乘法器数量、节省权重存储。

Method: 核心重构：长度n、k比特权重码的点积可用n+2^k次加法、至多2^k次译码与2^k次乘法完成。每个权重码直接作为地址偏移，选中2^k个累加器之一；每个激活值累加进被选中的累加器；末端的融合“译码-乘”步骤仅对累加和整体译码并相乘一次，解码后的权重向量从不显式构造。该变换对整数运算精确，兼容整数/浮点操作数与任意（含非线性）译码器，并可推广到分组scale及矩阵、张量积。硬件上提出SA-PE替代权重驻留脉动阵列中的MAC单元，采用load/stream/reduce/drain调度，使小型可时分复用的乘法器每tile仅运行2^k次；由于不存储解码权重，本地权重存储最多缩小w_d/k（w_d为解码权重位宽）。以代价模型量化总算术能耗收益，并给出FPGA综合RTL与匹配MAC阵列的对比。

Result: 乘法器数量降为n/2^k；总算术能耗改善因子较小但有界，由代价模型给出。FPGA综合结果显示：非共享的SA-PE面积大于MAC PE，只有当约简乘法器在多个PE间共享时才能体现面积/资源节省。软件模型验证整数结果精确等价，浮点误差不劣于传统流水线。权重本地存储缩减至多w_d/k倍。

Conclusion: 该工作把低比特权重点积的译码-乘运算后移并融合，实现了乘法次数与权重存储的显著下降，且数值上与常规流水线等价；其硬件收益依赖跨PE的乘法器共享，作者亦声明该恒等式本身并非首创，并讨论了权重共享与查表类内核的相关工作。

Abstract: Large neural networks are increasingly deployed with 1-4 bit weights and 16- or 32-bit activations, yet their dot products still use one multiplier per element. We show that a length-n dot product with k-bit weight codes can be computed with n+2^k additions and at most 2^k decodes and 2^k multiplications. Each weight code is used directly as an address offset that selects one of 2^k accumulators; each activation is added into the selected accumulator; and the accumulated sums are decoded and multiplied only once, at the end, in a fused decode-and-multiply step. The decoded weight vector is never formed. The refactoring is exact for integer arithmetic, works with integer or floating-point operands and with arbitrary, including nonlinear, decoders, and extends to group-wise scales and to matrix and tensor products. The multiplier count falls by n/2^k, while total arithmetic energy improves by a smaller, bounded factor that we quantify with a cost model. We describe a scatter-accumulate processing element (SA-PE) that replaces the MAC unit in a weight-stationary systolic array, with a load/stream/reduce/drain schedule in which a small, time-shareable multiplier runs only 2^k times per tile. Because decoded weights are not stored, local weight memory shrinks by up to w_d/k for w_d-bit decoded weights. An RTL implementation synthesized for FPGA and compared with a matched MAC array shows that an unshared SA-PE is larger than a MAC PE, and that the saving appears when the reduction multiplier is shared across PEs. Software models confirm exact integer equivalence and floating-point error no worse than the conventional pipeline. We do not claim the underlying identity is new, and we discuss related work on weight sharing and lookup-based kernels.

</details>


### [108] [Every Weight Is a Path of Shared Discrete Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31931)
*Ammar Ali,Stamatis Lefkimmiatis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TWIG（带继承门控的树形权重）表示：将网络中每个权重视为一条离散决策路径（符号、2的幂移位、零门、共享表索引）的终点，路径上层决策共享存储与遗传；使剪枝、量化、低秩分解、码本与权重共享统一为同一对象的编辑，并将决策选择形式化为高阶玻尔兹曼机基态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法各自独立：每种方法自带格式与求解器；固定权重的电路需为每个权重配专用常数乘法器；离散求解器仅能处理舍入等窄子问题。已有权重格式（组缩放、共享指数、微指数、稀疏权重）正朝共享决策演进，作者希望将该趋势推到极致。

Method: 构建树形权重表示：权重由一条小离散决策路径定义（符号、2的幂移位、零门、共享表索引），同组权重共享路径上部，每个决策被其下所有权重继承，只存一次、算一次。当决策为2的幂时，权重层变为几乎只由连线与加法器构成的电路。统一优化器可在整个网络范围内于剪枝、量化、低秩、码本、权重共享之间选择；决策选择被表述为路径结构决定相互作用的高阶玻尔兹曼机基态问题。

Result: 在大语言模型的五个权重矩阵上，对电路代价逐权重选择带符号2的幂项、不使用组缩放、采用扁平树时，在典型矩阵上3位与4位精度下达到同等层误差所需加法数比GPTQ少9–15%；在具有强离群值的矩阵上少20–55%。

Conclusion: TWIG将共享决策式权重格式推向终点，所有常见格式都是其浅层特例且无额外开销；任何已训练网络均可表示为TWIG；把多类压缩统一为单一对象编辑，并将权重选择落到可被退火器与量子优化器求解的基态问题上，在电路代价指标上优于GPTQ。

Abstract: We introduce TWIG (Tree-structured Weights with Inherited Gates), a representation in which every weight of a neural network is the end of a path of small discrete decisions: a sign, a shift by a power of two, a zero gate, an index into a shared table. Weights that belong together share the upper part of their paths, and each decision is inherited by every weight below it, so it is stored once and applied once. This simple change has three consequences. Any trained network can be written as a TWIG, and when the decisions are powers of two its weight layers become circuits made almost entirely of wires and adders, suited to chips whose weights are fixed at fabrication. Pruning, quantization, low-rank factorization, codebooks and weight sharing become edits of one object, so a single optimizer can choose among all of them for a whole network. And choosing the decisions is the ground-state problem of a higher-order Boltzmann machine whose interactions follow the paths of the tree, the problem class that annealers and quantum optimizers are built to solve. Today these questions are handled apart: each compression method brings its own format and solver, a circuit with fixed weights needs a dedicated constant multiplier for every weight, and discrete solvers reach only narrow subproblems such as rounding. Weight formats have long been moving toward shared decisions, through group scales, shared exponents, microexponents and sparse weights; TWIG takes this trend to its end, and every common format is a shallow TWIG at no extra cost. On five weight matrices of a large language model, a flat tree whose signed power-of-two terms are chosen per weight for circuit cost, with no group scales, needs 9--15\% fewer additions than GPTQ at equal layer error at 3- and 4-bit accuracy on typical matrices, and 20--55\% fewer on matrices with strong outliers.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Local Accuracy: Benchmarking the Rollout Reliability of PDE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31935)
*Jaewan Park,Seid Koric,Giri Krishnan,Sudhakar Pamidighantam,Surresh Marru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究PDE基础模型的短时预测优势在自回归长时推演中是否持续，定义误差放大比 G=E(H_f)/E(H_s)，在7个二维时序PDE数据集上评测7个模型，发现短时排名优势与长时误差放大并存，建议基准报告分horizon绝对误差与放大统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE基础模型虽能数据高效适配，但实际按自回归方式部署，短时精度不代表可靠的长时模拟；需检验其短时优势在反复反馈输入的长时推演中是否保持。

Method: 定义误差放大度量 G=E(H_f)/E(H_s)（全horizon误差相对短horizon误差之比）；在7个二维时间相关PDE数据集上评测7个模型：从头训练的FNO与DeepONet，微调的DPOT、GPhyT、Poseidon、Walrus，以及冻结的上下文学习模型VICON；采用VRMSE、相对L2、相对MAE三项指标；6个数据集提供成对短/全horizon分数，CE-RM仅提供短rollout扫描。

Result: 在30个短rollout的“数据集–预算”单元中，至少26个单元有微调基础模型在全部指标上优于两个从头训练基线；但144个成对训练配置中有140个全horizon VRMSE高于短horizon，误差放大中位数为2.04；VRMSE低于1的计数从104降至53，12次全rollout超过10而短horizon无一超过；Walrus在24个单元中16个取得最低全horizon VRMSE，但其误差放大中位数达2.70；相对L2与相对MAE呈相同定性趋势。

Conclusion: 短horizon的有利排名可与显著的误差放大共存，PDE基础模型基准不应只报单一局部预测分数，而应报告分horizon绝对误差、放大统计量与失败发生率。

Abstract: PDE foundation models enable data-efficient adaptation but are deployed autoregressively, so short-horizon accuracy need not imply reliable simulation. We ask whether the short-horizon advantage of a foundation-model recipe persists when its predictions are repeatedly fed back as inputs for a long horizon. We define error amplification as G=E(H_f)/E(H_s), which measures full-horizon error relative to short-horizon error. We evaluate seven models on seven two-dimensional time-dependent PDE datasets: scratch-trained FNO and DeepONet; fine-tuned DPOT, GPhyT, Poseidon, and Walrus; and frozen in-context VICON. We report VRMSE, relative L_2, and relative MAE. Six datasets provide paired short- and full-horizon scores, while CE-RM contributes short-rollout sweeps only. At least one fine-tuned foundation model outperforms both scratch baselines in 26 of 30 short-rollout dataset--budget cells under every metric. Nevertheless, full-horizon VRMSE exceeds short-horizon VRMSE in 140 of 144 paired trained configurations, with a median error amplification of $2.04$. The count below VRMSE one falls from 104 to 53, and 12 full rollouts exceed ten although none does at the short horizon. Walrus achieves the lowest full-horizon VRMSE in 16 of 24 cells, yet its median error amplification is $2.70$. Relative L_2 and relative MAE show the same qualitative pattern. These results show that favorable short-horizon rankings can coexist with substantial error amplification. PDE foundation-model benchmarks should therefore report horizon-resolved absolute errors, amplification statistics, and failure incidence rather than a single local forecast score.

</details>


### [110] [Agreement rather than normalisation keeps depth reusable in looped language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31943)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环语言模型的深度可复用性取决于“训练循环与推理循环一致”，而非循环边界归一化：移除Ouro的归一化会使精度崩塌，但该崩塌源于读出层失配；从头训练时去掉归一化反而不差甚至外推更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型仅在"深度可复用"时才有收益，即推理时超出训练预算的循环步数仍可用。作者追问这种可复用性究竟依赖于循环步间的状态归一化（如Ouro模型所做），还是依赖于训练循环与推理循环的一致性。

Method: 1) 在公开权重上无参数地移除循环边界归一化（读出路径保持逐位一致，单步时为恒等以作实现校验）；2) 在二元均衡任务上测试Ouro-1.4B/2.6B；3) 从头训练19M参数模型，两个合成任务、每配置16个随机种子，对比有无归一化；4) 在冻结修改后的循环上重拟合读出头与最后块层，定位损伤位置；5) 在匹配过冲量条件下比较循环深度与堆叠深度。

Result: 移除归一化后，Ouro-1.4B与2.6B在训练预算内精度归零（机会水平0.500）。原因并非状态范数增长（范数波动仅2.021倍，而输出头锁定到单个错误token）。从头训练的19M模型中去掉归一化从不更差、外推更优（超出预算后Cliff's delta达0.867）。损伤集中于读出层与最后块层：仅重拟合读出头恢复约三分之一精度并饱和，连最后块层一起重拟合则完全恢复。在匹配过冲时，循环相对堆叠深度的优势在一个任务上消失、另一个上缩小。

Conclusion: 保持深度可复用的是训练循环与推理循环之间的一致性，而非归一化本身；归一化只是与该一致性相耦合的代理。循环深度的"优势"部分来自比较时过冲量不匹配的混淆。

Abstract: Looped language models apply one block of layers repeatedly, and the design pays off only if depth is reusable: loop steps beyond the trained budget must remain usable at inference. We ask whether that rests on normalising the state between loop steps, as the released Ouro models do, or on agreement between the loop a model is trained with and the loop it is run with. On released weights this loop-boundary normalisation can be removed without adding parameters and with the readout path bit-identical, and the operation is an identity at one loop step, which serves as an implementation check. Removing it takes Ouro-1.4B and Ouro-2.6B to zero accuracy by the trained budget on a balanced binary task (chance 0.500). State-norm growth, the failure that prior accounts predict, is not the cause: the norm stays within a factor of 2.021 while the head commits to a single wrong token. Yet in 19M-parameter models trained from scratch on two synthetic tasks, 16 seeds per configuration, training without the normalisation is never worse than training with it and extrapolates better: the two are closest at the trained depth and separate on both sides of it (Cliff's delta up to 0.867 beyond the budget). Both results follow if agreement, and not the normalisation itself, is what keeps depth reusable. The damage sits in the readout together with the last layer of the block: in the models trained from scratch, refitting the head over the frozen modified loop recovers a third of the lost accuracy and plateaus, while refitting the last block layer with it recovers all of it. Finally, comparing looped with stacked depth at an equal inference loop count is confounded by unequal overshoot of the trained budget; at matched overshoot the advantage of looping vanishes on one task and shrinks on the other.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Trustworthy AI-Enabled Emergency Medical Dispatch: Uncertainty-Aware Ambulance Travel Time Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31944)
*Muhammad Adil Abid,Johan Holmgren,Fabian Lorig,Jesper Petersson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的救护车行程时间估计方法，生成上下文自适应预测区间，提升急救调度决策的风险敏感性与透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助急救调度依赖准确的行程时间估计，但现有方法仅给出单点预测，无法反映单次估计的可靠性与不确定性；而安全攸关场景中，期望行程时间相近的出行可能具有显著不同的不确定性。此外真实行程日志含域特有伪影（如共址医疗机构、物理上不合理的速度），既损害点预测精度，又影响后续不确定性估计所依赖的任务。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的救护车行程时间估计方法，生成上下文自适应预测区间；采用瑞典Skåne地区两年救护车行程数据进行回顾性真实场景评估。

Result: 该方法超越平均情形点预测，在各操作性重要上下文下提供具有稳定经验覆盖率的预测区间。

Conclusion: 预测区间显式刻画预测不确定性，可支持更透明、风险敏感的救护车调度决策。

Abstract: Emergency medical dispatch increasingly relies on AI-enabled decision support systems when making ambulance dispatch decisions. In this process, accurate ambulance travel time estimates are essential for timely resource allocation and prehospital care. However, existing ambulance travel time estimation approaches typically produce only single point predictions. Consequently, they do not indicate how reliable an individual estimate is or how much uncertainty it carries. This limitation is particularly problematic in safety-critical emergency medical service dispatch operations, where trips with similar expected travel times may exhibit substantially different levels of uncertainty. Furthermore, real-world ambulance trip logs may contain domain-specific artefacts, such as co-located healthcare facilities and physically implausible travel speeds. These artefacts can degrade point prediction accuracy and affect the prediction task on which subsequent uncertainty estimation depends. To address these challenges, we propose an uncertainty-aware methodology for ambulance travel time estimation and evaluate it through a retrospective real-world scenario study using two years of ambulance trip data from the Skåne region in Sweden. The proposed methodology moves beyond average-case point predictions by generating context-adaptive prediction intervals with stable empirical coverage across the evaluated operationally important contexts. The resulting prediction intervals provide explicit information about predictive uncertainty that can support more transparent and risk-sensitive ambulance dispatch decision-making.

</details>


### [112] [Sharp Bounds on the Winner's Bias under Independent and Nonnegatively Correlated Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31948)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文确定了「从 M 个含噪无偏得分中取最大值」所导致胜者价值高估的尖锐常数 c，在独立非同分布噪声下为 √((M−1)/(2M))，在成对非负相关噪声下为 (M−1)/M，并给出有限构造、交叉验证偏差的尖锐界与 Lean 4 形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用「取最大」进行模型/候选选择，但最大值会被噪声放大，从而高估胜者的真实价值。此前的尖锐常数仅在两个极端已知：任意依赖 (√((M−1)/M)) 与 i.i.d. 噪声；中间依赖结构下的常数未知，且期望极差的最大化版本问题悬而未决。

Method: 在总噪声方差 V 固定的设定下，按噪声依赖结构分类推导尖锐常数：独立非同分布类与成对非负相关类，并构造达到界的显式有限实例；将结论用于交叉验证估计量偏差的尖锐界推导；全部定理在 Lean 4 中形式化验证；并在 448 个语言模型与分类器选择流水线上做实证检验（比较「共享条目」与「每候选独立条目」两种设计）。

Result: 得到两项尖锐常数：独立非同分布噪声为 √((M−1)/(2M))，成对非负相关噪声为 (M−1)/M；无相关噪声情形同时解决了期望极差最大化版本的开放问题。两类界均有显式有限构造可达。实证上，采用每候选独立条目的设计仅实现独立类上限的中位数 0.75；在固定条目预算下，让所有候选共享条目远不如给每个候选分配独立条目那样乐观。

Conclusion: 最大值选择的高估幅度由噪声依赖结构决定，本文在两个中间类中给出并形式化验证了尖锐常数，为选择偏差（含交叉验证偏差）提供了可计算的精确刻度；实践上，固定预算时应优先让候选共享评测条目，以降低乐观偏差。

Abstract: Reporting the best of $M$ noisy, unbiased scores overstates the winner's value by up to $c\sqrt{V}$, where $V$ is the total noise variance and the sharp constant $c$ depends on how the noise is dependent. It was known only at the two extremes, arbitrary dependence ($\sqrt{(M-1)/M}$) and i.i.d. noise. We determine it for two classes in between: for independent, not identically distributed noise it is $\sqrt{(M-1)/(2M)}$, and for pairwise nonnegatively correlated noise it is $(M-1)/M$; for uncorrelated noise this answers the maximum version of an open question about the expected range. Explicit finite constructions attain both. As a corollary, the bias of the cross-validation estimator is bounded sharply within each class. All theorems are formally verified in Lean 4. Across 448 language-model and classifier selection pipelines, designs with per-candidate items realize a median 0.75 of the independent-class ceiling. At a fixed item budget, scoring all candidates on shared items is markedly less optimistic than giving each candidate its own items.

</details>


### [113] [A Mathematical Theory of the Jacobian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31955)
*Mitchell Ostrow,Daoyuan Qian,Toni Liu,Ila Fiete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 J-lens（Jacobian lens）的数学理论，证明其优先识别"相干特征"——即在层间放大的、跨 token 位置近似恒定、跨上下文一致的线性方向（类似空间相干 Lyapunov 主模态），并在 Bayes filter 与 toy transformer 上验证，同时提出 ω-J-lens 族以刻画不同时间尺度的信息处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Anthropic 的 J-lens 在前沿语言模型隐状态中找到了类工作记忆的线性方向，但其为何能检测到这些特征的机制性质不明；作者希望从数学上刻画 J-lens 的选择偏好。

Method: 建立 J-lens 的解析理论，将可检测方向刻画为"相干特征"：沿该方向的微小扰动在层间放大（Lyapunov 式增长）、跨 token 位置近似不变、跨上下文一致；在最优预测 HMM 下一 token 的 Bayes filter 与 toy transformer 两类模型上进行验证，并通过调节任务参数改变特征相干性来检验 J-lens 响应的可预测变化。

Result: 理论分析导出的 J-lens 偏好可解释此前在两类模型上的经验现象；实验中，改变 Bayes filter 特征相干性的任务参数会相应且可预测地调制 J-lens 响应。

Conclusion: J-lens 的偏好可由相干性（层间放大、位置不变、上下文一致）解释，作者对"Global Workspace"关联持不可知态度；并进一步提出 ω-J-lens 族以突出 LLM 中不同时间尺度的信息处理。

Abstract: Recent work from Anthropic introduced a new interpretability tool, called the Jacobian lens (J-lens, Gurnee et al., 2026). This method identified linear directions in the hidden states of frontier language models that had properties reminiscent of working memory-like representations. However, it is unclear what mechanistic properties of such features make them detectable to the J-lens. To this end, we introduce a mathematical theory of the J-lens, and show that it preferentially identifies coherent features -- directions that act like leading, spatially coherent Lyapunov modes. Small perturbations along these directions amplify across layers, remain roughly constant across token positions, and are consistent across different contexts. While we are agnostic to whether these properties are necessarily associated with a ``Global Workspace'', the analytically derived preferences of the J-lens nevertheless explain prior empirical applied to both model classes. We validate our analysis on a Bayes filter and a toy transformer, each optimally predicting next tokens emitted from a Hidden Markov Model (HMM). We show how modulating task parameters that change the coherence of features in the Bayes filter correspondingly and predictively modulates the response of the J-lens . We also introduce of a family of J-lenses built to emphasize different temporal scales of information processing in LLMs, which we call $ω$-J-lenses.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Heuristics and Commercial Navigation: An AI-Augmented Decision-Support for Ambulance Travel Time Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31968)
*Muhammad Adil Abid,Johan Holmgren,Fabian Lorig,Jesper Petersson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于瑞典Skåne地区真实救护车运行数据，构建可解释机器学习系统以更准确估计救护车行程时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有急救调度决策支持常依赖静态查表或简化假设（如救护车仅比普通乘用车快固定百分比，如5%），这些假设鲜少用真实救护车运营数据验证，且无法刻画行程优先级、时间上下文与起讫点（OD）的影响。

Method: 使用瑞典Skåne地区历史救护车行程数据，分析行程时间在小时、星期与行程优先级上的差异，并检验商业导航系统对急救动态特征的捕捉能力；随后构建机器学习系统估计救护车行程时间，并采用SHAP与置换重要性方法提升模型可解释性。

Result: 分析表明救护车行程时间随小时、星期和行程优先级显著变化，商业导航系统无法捕捉急救特有的动态特征；所提出的机器学习系统能够更准确地估计救护车行程时间，且可解释性分析显示模型捕捉到具有运营意义的关键模式。

Conclusion: 简化假设与静态查表不足以为急救调度提供可靠行程时间估计，应基于真实运营数据与可解释机器学习方法构建估计系统。

Abstract: Accurate ambulance travel time estimation is critical for emergency medical dispatch, yet existing decision-support practices often rely on static lookup tables or simplified assumptions regarding how ambulance travel time differs from ordinary passenger-car travel. For instance, they assume ambulances simply travel a fixed percentage (e.g., 5%) faster than ordinary passenger cars. However, such assumptions are rarely validated using real ambulance operational data and may fail to capture the effects of trip priority, temporal context, and origin-destination. We address these limitations using historical ambulance trips from the Skåne region in Sweden. Our analysis reveals that ambulance travel times vary substantially by hour, day, and trip priority, and that commercial navigation systems fail to capture emergency-specific dynamics. We contribute a machine learning system to more accurately estimate ambulance travel time. To make the system interpretable, we explored SHapley Additive exPlanations and permutation importance techniques to ensure that our system captures operationally meaningful patterns.

</details>


### [115] [Compatible Likelihoods for Flow Matching on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31981)
*Lucas Ng,Georgios Batzolis,Mark Girolami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示黎曼流匹配（扩展版）存在"兼容性"失效：在1D平坦环面上，其全局最优速度场会把样本推离数据分布。作者给出兼容性的一般条件并据此分析现有方法，提出基于端点指数族、以条件速度为充分统计量的新目标，既兼容又只支撑于可行速度，表现为损失重加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出的黎曼流匹配扩展（基于似然的flow matching目标）看似自然，但在最简单的非欧流形（1D平坦环面）上出现病态：其总体最优解对应的速度场竟使样本远离数据分布。作者希望解释这一失败的根源，并构造正确的目标函数。

Method: 1）提出"兼容性"（compatibility）概念：基于似然的flow matching目标，若其总体最小化器能恢复由数据与耦合诱导的边缘速度场，则称其兼容；2）给出兼容性的一般判定条件，并用于检验现有方法；3）以在兼容性下消失的残差分量来界定最坏情况误差；4）指出已有兼容目标会将密度置于紧流形上不可行的速度上，遂构造基于端点指数族、以条件速度为充分统计量的新目标——该目标兼容且仅支撑于可行速度；5）在常曲率流形上，该目标表现为flow matching损失的重加权，对流速与流向误差区别对待。

Result: 实验表明：新方法在1D环面测试用例上恢复出正确的速度场；在高维乘积环面上优于已有方法；在球面四个标准地理空间基准中的两个上取得显著NLL改善且运行时间开销适中；在高维人体姿态大数据集上，按基于采样的指标优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 现有黎曼flow matching目标可能不兼容，导致最优解把样本推离数据；兼容性是甄别目标函数正确性的关键准则。所提指数族目标兼顾兼容性与速度可行性，在环面、球面地理数据及人体姿态任务上一致超越先前方法，验证了失效机理分析与重加权设计的有效性。

Abstract: On the simplest non-Euclidean manifold, the 1D flat torus, we exhibit a distribution where a recently proposed extension of Riemannian flow matching is globally optimized by a velocity field that pushes samples away from the data distribution. We trace this failure mode to a property we call compatibility; a likelihood-based flow matching objective is compatible if its population minimizer recovers the marginal velocity field induced by the data and coupling. We give a general condition for compatibility, apply it to existing methods, and bound the worst-case error in terms of a residual component vanishing under compatibility. Next, we note that existing compatible objectives put density on velocities that are infeasible on compact manifolds. We propose an objective built from an exponential family over endpoints, with the conditional velocity as sufficient statistic, which is both compatible and supported only on feasible velocities. This manifests as a reweighting of the flow matching loss which, on constant-curvature manifolds, treats errors in flow speed and direction differently. Empirically, our method recovers the correct velocity field on the test case, outperforms prior methods on high-dimensional product tori, and obtains substantial NLL improvements over prior work on two of four standard geospatial benchmarks on the sphere at moderate runtime overhead. On a large dataset of high-dimensional human poses, our method outperforms prior work according to sample-based metrics.

</details>


### [116] [REDAR: Reinforcement-Driven Adaptive Regularisation for Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31991)
*Akila Ayanthi,Darshana Priyasad,Tharindu Fernando,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REDAR，用轻量强化学习智能体逐阶段自适应输出蒸馏正则化系数，替代离线调优的固定系数，缓解类增量学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化类增量学习（CIL）方法用离线调好的固定系数控制稳定性-可塑性权衡，与持续学习目标脱钩，且无法感知各阶段的样本量、任务难度与类相似度变化，存在根本性错配。

Method: 将正则化强度选择建模为序列决策问题：每个增量阶段，轻量RL智能体以当前性能指标与新增数据统计构成的紧凑状态为输入，输出该阶段的蒸馏系数；为遵守CIL不可访问历史数据的约束，策略仅在由初始阶段构造的伪CIL任务上训练，之后直接部署于增量序列且不再适应。

Result: 在CIFAR-100与Tiny-ImageNet上，REDAR相较固定系数与启发式基线降低遗忘并提升前向迁移；在短伪任务视野上训练的策略可有效迁移到更长的增量运行。

Conclusion: 正则化系数应作为随阶段动态决策的变量而非静态超参数；伪任务上训练的策略具备跨阶段长度的泛化性，为CIL中自适应权衡提供可行范式。

Abstract: Regularisation-based class-incremental learning (CIL) methods control the stability-plasticity trade-off through fixed coefficients tuned offline, independently of the continual learning objective and blind to phase-specific variations in data volume, task difficulty, and class similarity. We argue this is a fundamental mismatch and propose REDAR: Reinforcement-Driven Adaptive Regularisation for Class Incremental Learning, which treats regularisation strength selection as a sequential decision problem. At each incremental phase, a lightweight reinforcement learning (RL) agent observes a compact state of current performance metrics and incoming data statistics, and outputs the distillation coefficients for that phase. To respect CIL's constraint on historical data access, the policy is trained entirely on pseudo-CIL tasks constructed from the initial phase, then deployed on the incremental sequence without further adaptation. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that REDAR reduces forgetting and improves forward transfer over fixed and heuristic baselines, and that policies trained on short pseudo-task horizons transfer effectively to longer incremental runs.

</details>


### [117] [GS-MoE: Training-Free MoE Compression via Groupwise Subspace Sharing and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32009)
*Carson Cary,Yukai Jin,Yihao Huang,Zhankai Ye,Fangyi Li,Ting Hua,Xin Liu,Shangqian Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GS-MoE：无需训练的MoE压缩框架，沿"共享子空间秩"与"专家中间宽度"两个轴消除专家间与专家内冗余，并以分层预算分配实现高压缩率下的性能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE通过稀疏激活降低计算量，但专家参数存储成为主要内存瓶颈；现有专家参数存在跨专家与专家内部的冗余，亟需无需训练的高效压缩方案。

Method: 提出训练无关的双轴压缩：1）组内低秩表示捕捉跨专家共享结构，同时保留专家特有投影；2）基于Nyström的神经元压缩降低每个专家的中间宽度。对固定共享子空间，从重构目标推导各专家的重构误差下降量以指导专家分组。采用无梯度的层级重要性分数进行分层预算分配，再由特征值与杠杆分数将秩预算非均匀分配到各组、将宽度预算分配到各专家。

Result: 实验显示两轴压缩优势互补，在细粒度MoE高压缩率下尤为明显。DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base上总参数减少60%时，GS-MoE保留约90%的平均推理性能（D²-MoE为73%），且域外困惑度低20%。

Conclusion: 跨专家与专家内冗余可沿共享子空间秩和中间宽度两轴联合压缩，配合基于重构误差与重要性的分层预算分配，可在免训练条件下显著优于基线；代码已开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models reduce computation through sparse activation, but storing their expert parameters remains a major memory bottleneck. We introduce GS-MoE, a training-free compression framework that addresses redundancy across and within experts through two compression axes: shared-subspace rank and expert intermediate width. Groupwise low-rank representations capture shared structure across experts while retaining expert-specific projections, and Nyström-based neuron compression reduces intermediate width within each expert. For fixed shared subspaces, we derive per-expert reconstruction-error reductions from the reconstruction objective and use them to guide expert grouping. Budgets are allocated hierarchically using a gradient-free layer-importance score to guide the allocation of rank and width budgets across layers, which eigenvalues and leverage then distribute as non-uniform ranks across groups and intermediate widths across experts. Experiments demonstrate the complementary benefits of compression along both axes, particularly at high compression rates on fine-grained MoE models. On DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base, at a 60% reduction in total model parameters, GS-MoE retains approximately 90% of the uncompressed model's average reasoning performance, compared with 73% for D$^2$-MoE, while achieving 20% lower out-of-domain perplexity. We provide a code implementation at: https://github.com/cbacary/GS-MoE

</details>


### [118] [Rethinking Representation Geometry in Supervised Contrastive Learning under Long-Tailed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32039)
*Joonhyeok Jeong,Jy-yong Sohn,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立理论框架分析监督对比学习及其均衡变体在最优点是否形成正则单纯形几何，指出代表性目标因依赖每类样本数而无法形成正则单纯形，并提出结构均衡与全均衡对比目标使其在最优点可证明形成正则单纯形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析认为长尾分布下监督对比学习的最优表示可能具有正则单纯形几何，但该几何是否真被实现仍缺乏充分理解；同时典型目标依赖每类样本数，难以获得均衡表示。

Method: 构建刻画最优点是否形成正则单纯形的理论框架；将其应用于代表性目标以判定几何性质；提出可施加于对比目标的"结构均衡"（structural balancing），并设计全均衡对比目标（fully balanced contrastive objective）。

Result: 理论分析表明代表性目标在最优点因依赖每类样本数而无法形成正则单纯形；所提目标可证明在最优点形成正则单纯形几何。理论与实验均显示其产生更均衡的表示空间，并提升长尾识别与迁移学习性能。

Conclusion: 表示几何的均衡性取决于目标是否消除类样本数依赖；带有结构均衡的全均衡对比目标可实现正则单纯形几何，从而改善长尾场景下的表示质量与下游性能。

Abstract: Supervised contrastive learning and its balanced variants have demonstrated their effectiveness for representation learning under long-tailed distributions. However, although prior analyses suggest that their optimal representations may exhibit a regular simplex geometry, whether such a geometry is indeed exhibited remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present a theoretical framework for these objectives that characterizes whether a regular simplex is formed at the optimum. By applying this to representative objectives, we show that these objectives fail to form a regular simplex at the optimum because they remain dependent on the number of samples per class. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce structural balancing that can be applied to contrastive objectives, and propose a fully balanced contrastive objective that provably forms a regular simplex geometry at its optimum. Both theoretical and empirical results demonstrate that the proposed method yields a more balanced representation space and improves performance on long-tailed recognition and transfer learning tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Controlled Evaluation of EEG Foundation Models: Random Initialisation, Band-Power and Validation-Selected Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32067)
*Mingyu Jeon,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在统一协议下加入三类对照（未训练编码器、同数据拟合的频谱特征、不看测试分数选层），重新评估5个公开EEG基础模型与1个自研JEPA模型，发现多数下游增益在对照下消失或反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型常报告下游任务收益，但其评估协议普遍缺少关键对照，导致收益可能来自选层时的测试集泄漏、特征基线不足或预训练本身并无贡献，从而高估了预训练的价值。

Method: 在同一协议下评估5个公开EEG基础模型和1个JEPA风格自研模型，覆盖5个基准与4类新增任务族，多次随机种子重复；设置三类对照：同架构随机初始化编码器、在同数据上拟合的五频带对数功率特征基线、以及在验证集上选层而非按测试分数选层。

Result: 按测试分数选层使逐层峰值平均虚高+0.034，并在36个单元中9个反转"峰值层与输出层"的优劣关系；改为验证集选层后，随机初始化在睡眠与情绪任务上对半数模型追平或超越预训练，且无模型在情绪与运动想象上超过五频带对数功率基线；全微调后30对比较中仅5对增益超过两倍种子波动，唯一的显著损失-0.290 AUROC在匹配学习率下依然存在。

Conclusion: 现有EEG基础模型的多数下游报告增益在严格对照下不稳健，论文据此提出四项报告规范，并公开全部结果单元以供再分析。

Abstract: EEG foundation models report downstream gains, yet their protocols seldom include three controls. These are the same encoder untrained, spectral features fitted on the same data, and a layer picked without looking at test scores. We therefore evaluate five publicly released EEG foundation models and an in-house JEPA-style model under one protocol with all three controls and several seeds, on five benchmarks and four added task families. The controls change the reading in three ways. First, picking the probed layer by its test score raises the layer-wise peak by an average of +0.034 and reverses the peak-versus-output gap in 9 of 36 cells. Second, with the layer picked on validation, random initialisation matches or beats pretraining for half of the models on sleep and emotion, and no model beats a five-band log-power baseline on emotion or motor imagery. Third, after full fine-tuning only 5 of 30 pairs gain beyond twice their seed spread, and the one clear loss of -0.290 AUROC persists under matched learning rates. From these results we draw four reporting practices, and we release every cell for re-analysis.

</details>


### [120] [DBCM: Dual-Branch Calibration Module for Test-Time Adaptation in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32071)
*Jaebyeong Jeon,Kunwoo Kang,Sang Yup Lee,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双分支校准模块（DBCM），通过跨通道共享适配器加低秩通道级适配器减少通道独立参数化的冗余，在测试时适应中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中的非平稳性导致测试时分布漂移，产生预测残差。现有测试时适应方法采用通道级适配器，隐含假设残差是通道特异的，但观察发现残差在通道间存在大量共享结构，完全独立的通道级参数化可能冗余。

Method: 在冻结的预测器周围引入轻量校准模块DBCM，采用双分支结构：一支为跨通道共享适配器以共享适应能力，另一支为低秩通道级适配器保留有限的通道特异灵活性；并辅以通道混合与非线性门控调制。模块利用测试时逐步可得的观测进行更新。

Result: 在多种数据集、预测步长与骨干网络上，DBCM 持续优于先前的测试时适应方法。

Conclusion: 残差的跨通道共享结构表明通道级适配器存在冗余；共享加低秩通道特异的双分支校准是更有效的测试时适应设计。

Abstract: Non-stationarity induces test-time distribution shifts in time series forecasting (TSF), resulting in forecast residuals between predictions and the corresponding ground truth. Recent works on test-time adaptation for TSF address this by introducing lightweight calibration modules around frozen forecasters, updated using observations that become incrementally available at test time. Existing approaches typically employ channel-wise adapters, implicitly assuming that forecast residuals are inherently channel-specific. However, we observe that forecast residuals often exhibit substantial shared structure across channels, suggesting that fully independent channel-wise parameterization may be redundant. Motivated by this observation, we propose the Dual-Branch Calibration Module (DBCM), which shares adaptation capacity across channels through a shared adapter while retaining limited channel-specific flexibility through a low-rank channel-wise adapter, further enhanced by channel mixing and nonlinear gated modulation. Extensive experiments show that DBCM consistently outperforms prior methods across diverse datasets, horizons, and backbones.

</details>


### [121] [PRISM: Promoting Sampling Diversity of Large Language Models with Implicit Stochastic Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32101)
*Qixin Xu,Zihan Zhu,Xiaojiang Liu,Bohan Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出训练期框架PRISM，将多样性内化为模型能力，缓解LLM模式坍缩，在保持最佳生成质量的同时维持强语义多样性并为RL提供更好基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在模式坍缩，输出分布集中于熟悉的语义模式；多样性消失后重复采样沦为语义重复而非发现，稀有但更强的解难以浮现，自演化与持续学习难以维持。现有方法多在推理期干预，仅塑形采样而未使多样性成为模型自身能力。

Method: PRISM为训练期框架，含两部分：(i) 子模（submodular）筛选，为每个提示配对多样化且正确的多教师解答；(ii) 随机嵌入调制，将特殊token扰动转化为采样不同输出的隐式信号；并给出该机制稳定性的理论分析。

Result: 在WritingPrompts与BookMIA上，PRISM将质量-多样性前沿外推，在所有模型-基准设置下取得最佳生成质量，并在两个开放权重模型族上保持最强或接近最强的语义多样性；在数学与代码基准上提升采样效率；相较仅推理期方法，还为强化学习提供更强基础，维持多样化rollout并缓解RL训练中常见的多样性坍缩。

Conclusion: PRISM通过训练期将多样性内化，可同时提升质量与多样性并改善采样效率与RL训练稳定性，为持续学习与自演化奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit mode collapse, where their output distribution concentrates on familiar semantic modes. When diversity disappears, repeated sampling becomes semantic repetition rather than discovery, making rare but stronger solutions unlikely to surface and leaving self-evolution and continual learning difficult to sustain. Most remedies intervene only at inference time, shaping sam- ples without making diversity a model capability. We propose PRISM, a training-time framework that internalizes diversity within the model. PRISM combines (i) submodular curation that pairs each prompt with diverse-yet-correct multi-teacher solutions, and (ii) stochastic embedding modulation, which turns special-token perturbations into implicit signals for sampling distinct outputs. A theoretical analysis establishes the stability of this mechanism. On WritingPrompts and BookMIA, PRISM moves the quality-diversity frontier outward, achieving the best generation quality in all model-benchmark settings while maintaining the strongest or near-strongest semantic diversity across two open-weight model families. It also improves sampling efficiency on math and code benchmarks. Beyond inference, PRISM yields a stronger foundation for reinforcement learning, sustaining diverse rollouts and mitigating the diversity collapse commonly observed during RL training.

</details>


### [122] [ReMedI: Inference-Time Refinement and Self-Distillation for Controllable Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32103)
*Jia Yi Zhang,Shivanshu Shekhar,Allison McLevige,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMedI将分子语言模型作为提议分布，结合Metropolis-Hastings推理时搜索进行先导化合物多目标优化，性能超越C-MORAL且训练数据需求大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物研发中先导化合物优化需同时改善渗透性、降低毒性等多个竞争性质，同时限制骨架改动以保持活性。现有RL后训练方法（如C-MORAL）依赖昂贵的任务特定数据集，且难以实现多目标优化（MOO）；此外零样本生成搜索能力有限，难以高效触及预训练模型中已有的有用分子编辑。

Method: 提出ReMedI：以分子语言模型作为推理时对齐的基础提议器，给定先导分子与多目标任务，模型基于当前分子提出编辑；采用高效的Metropolis-Hastings搜索策略改进对提议分布的探索，在目标函数（性质提升、性质保持、先导相似性）引导下对序列编辑进行精炼。搜索轨迹随后可蒸馏回基础模型，提升零样本生成与后续搜索能力。

Result: 在C-MuMOInstruct与SMDD基准上，精炼过程使三个骨干网络的冻结基础模型均获提升，多目标优化成功率显著高于基线；蒸馏时所需训练先导分子约为C-MORAL的1/7.8；ReMedI的严格成功覆盖率约为C-MORAL的2.8倍，且在分布外任务上同样有效，并保持相当的先导相对相似度。

Conclusion: 推理时Metropolis-Hastings搜索可有效激发预训练分子语言模型的内在编辑能力，实现高效多目标先导优化；结合轨迹蒸馏，可在显著降低数据成本的同时超越RL后训练基线，具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: In drug development, lead optimization must improve competing properties, such as increasing permeability and reducing toxicity, while limiting scaffold changes that could compromise the lead's potency. RL-based post-training, such as C-MORAL, addresses this problem by fine-tuning molecular language models to directly optimize molecular leads for objectives of interest, which relies on expensive task-specific datasets and faces challenges in achieving multi-objective optimization (MOO). We argue a pre-trained model may already contain useful molecular edits that zero-shot generation cannot efficiently reach. Motivated by the limited search capabilities of zero-shot generation, we develop an efficient Metropolis-Hastings search strategy to better explore the proposal distribution. We propose ReMedI, which uses the molecular language model as a base proposer for inference-time alignment. Given a lead and a set of multi-objective tasks, the model proposes edits conditioned on the current molecule, while Metropolis--Hastings refinement guides sequential edits under an objective for property improvement, preservation, and lead similarity. On both C-MuMOInstruct and SMDD benchmarks, refinement improves frozen base models across three backbones, reaching significantly higher multi-objective optimization success rates compared to baselines. These search traces can then distilled into the base model which improves zero-shot generation and subsequent search using approximately 7.8 times less training molecular leads than that of C-MORAL. ReMedI achieves approximately 2.8 times the strict-success coverage of RL baseline C-MORAL, including on out-of-distribution tasks, with matched lead-relative similarity.

</details>


### [123] [Putting Transformer Theories to the Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32104)
*Awni Altabaa,Tobias Schnabel,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统梳理了7种Transformer能力理论，将其结论统一到同一预测矩阵中，并用控制实验检验，发现没有单一理论能解释全部成功与失败模式，进而归纳出Transformer理论必须刻画的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多种Transformer形式化理论各自基于不同抽象与假设，对"哪些任务容易/困难"给出相互冲突的预测，缺乏统一对照与实证校验，因此需要建立共同基准并检验其有效性。

Method: 梳理Star-free、AC⁰、TC⁰、C-RASP、MPC、BAPO、Sensitivity七种理论，将其结果翻译为覆盖不同计算结构的任务套件上的统一预测矩阵，并补充新的理论结果以补全矩阵；随后通过控制实验测量分布内样本复杂度、长度泛化和预训练语言模型表现来验证预测。

Result: 无单一理论能解释观测到的成功与失败模式。当理论未刻画任务相关的Transformer行为时预测失效：C-RASP因未建模内容依赖注意力而将k-hop induction判为困难，但小型Transformer可训练并实现长度泛化；AC⁰、BAPO等硬注意力假设预测二元多数问题困难，而实践中同样成功。

Conclusion: Transformer理论须同时刻画：软注意力而非硬注意力、动态内容依赖检索、每位置的有界计算、可学习性与归纳偏置的说明，以及上界与下界的结合。

Abstract: Several formal theories have been developed to characterize the capabilities and limitations of the transformer architecture, each employing different abstractions that capture different features of the architecture. Due to the differences in their underlying assumptions and conceptual frameworks, these theories can disagree in their predictions about what tasks transformers find easy or hard. We map out seven theories of transformer capability (Star-free, $\mathsf{AC}^0$, $\mathsf{TC}^0$, C-RASP, MPC, BAPO, and Sensitivity) and translate their results into a common matrix of predictions across a suite of tasks covering different computational structures. We provide new theoretical results to complete this matrix. We then evaluate theoretical predictions through controlled experiments measuring in-distribution sample complexity, length generalization, and pretrained language model performance. We find that no single theory explains the observed pattern of successes and failures. When a theory does not capture the transformer behaviors relevant to a task, its predictions break down; for instance, we show that the $k$-hop induction task is predicted hard by C-RASP because it does not model content-dependent attention, but we train small transformers on this task that length-generalize. Similarly, theories assuming hard attention like $\mathsf{AC}^0$ and BAPO predict binary majority to be hard, for which we also find success in practice. Together, our results point to several specific features that a theory of transformers must capture: soft rather than hard attention, dynamic content-dependent retrieval, bounded computation at each position, an account of learnability and inductive biases, and a combination of upper and lower bounds.

</details>


### [124] [REACT: Representational Early Analysis and Control of Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32113)
*Hanwen Zhang,Enyi Jiang,Ruike Zhu,Qinan Yu,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REACT在早期CoT隐藏状态注入安全方向，发现最强控制效果集中在2-3个token的早期窗口，在8B模型上将完全安全输出率从29.06%提升至82.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究风险最易检测的时间点是否也是控制最有效的时间点，即区分"风险可读性"与"有效控制"。

Method: 条件激活干预REACT：由配对的安全/不安全轨迹学习安全方向，在早期CoT隐藏状态施加一次干预；模型权重冻结，后续token无需再编辑。分别在前5个CoT位置训练方向，进行无门控编辑与完整重生成，在3个蒸馏推理模型和3个随机种子上评估。

Result: 最强增益集中在2-3 token早期窗口；DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上第4、5个token效果降至近零，尽管后段状态风险预测性更强。该模型上StrongREJECT-273完全安全CoT-答案输出率由29.06%升至82.05%，良性拒绝与有用性接近基线。

Conclusion: 治理干预时机应以其对后续生成的影响来评估，而非仅凭风险可读性；有效控制窗口与可读性窗口并不一致。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) generation offers an opportunity to intervene before a reasoning model completes its answer. But does the point at which risk is easiest to detect also offer the most effective control? We study this question with REACT, a conditional activation intervention that learns a safety direction from paired safe and unsafe trajectories and applies it once to an early CoT hidden state. The model weights remain frozen, and all subsequent tokens are generated without further edits. To study timing, we train directions separately at each of the first five CoT positions and evaluate ungated edits with full post-edit regeneration. Across three distilled reasoning models and three generation seeds, the strongest gains concentrate in a two- to three-token early window; on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, the effect falls to approximately zero at the fourth and fifth tokens, even though later states can be more predictive of risk. This separates risk readability from effective control: intervention timing must be evaluated through its effect on subsequent generation. On the same 8B model, REACT raises the rate of fully safe CoT-answer outputs on StrongREJECT-273 from 29.06% to 82.05%, while keeping benign refusal and usefulness near baseline. An anonymized implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/REACT_official2-333B.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Data Schedule: Fast LLM Pretraining on Data Mixtures by Loss-Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32141)
*Jiazheng Li,Huaqing Zhang,Jing Xu,Tianle Cai,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LARE（Loss-Adaptive Reweighting under fixed Exposure）方法：在数据暴露量固定的前提下，通过依据运行中的损失统计自动调整各领域损失权重，缓解大模型分阶段预训练中的知识遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过连续的多阶段预训练获得专门能力，但后期阶段常导致早期知识被遗忘。作者希望探究：在不改变各领域数据采样比例（数据暴露固定）的情况下，仅改变各领域对学习的贡献强度（损失重加权）能否改善这一权衡。

Method: 在受控的知识—数学实验中验证损失重加权可产生单一checkpoint同时超过两条阶段轨迹上的最佳知识得分与推理得分，说明这是数据混合比例之外的一个独立控制维度。据此提出LARE：基于梯度噪声的理论分析，采用归一化逆平方根规则（normalized inverse-square-root），由运行中的损失统计自动设定各领域权重，以平衡相对损失尺度，而不改变采样混合比、也无需额外运行来搜索权重。

Result: 在知识、数学、代码三个领域、1.5B模型、240B token训练设置下，LARE在全部五个评测基准上均提升，相比匹配的直接混合平均提升+7.84个百分点，其中GSM8K提升+17.06 pp、MATH提升+9.00 pp。

Conclusion: 结果表明，调整领域损失尺度可以在不增加领域数据暴露的情况下提升联合能力；损失重加权是数据配比之外的一个有效且独立的控制手段。

Abstract: Large language models often acquire specialized capabilities through successive pretraining stages, but these gains can come at the cost of forgetting earlier knowledge. In this work, we investigate whether such trade-offs can be improved by changing how strongly each domain contributes to learning while keeping its data exposure fixed. We find that, in controlled knowledge--mathematics experiments, loss reweighting produces a single checkpoint that surpasses the best knowledge and reasoning scores attained along either stage trajectory. This reveals an independent control that data-mixture ratios alone do not capture. Building on this finding, we propose LARE (Loss-Adaptive Reweighting under fixed Exposure), which automatically adjusts domain weights from running loss statistics. Motivated by a theoretical analysis of gradient noise, its normalized inverse-square-root rule balances relative loss scales without changing the sampled mixture or requiring additional runs to search for weights. Across knowledge, mathematics, and code, \Method{} improves all five evaluated benchmarks for a 1.5B model trained on 240B tokens, averaging +7.84 pp over a matched direct mixture, including gains of +17.06 pp on GSM8K and +9.00 pp on MATH. These findings demonstrate that adjusting domain loss scales can improve joint capabilities without additional domain exposure.

</details>


### [126] [Rethinking LoRA Initialization for Robust Asymmetric Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32142)
*Disen Liao,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对LoRA对学习率敏感、LoRA+的非对称学习率比例η_B/η_A易导致训练不稳定与性能崩塌的问题，通过信号-噪声分解揭示初始化诱导噪声的来源，提出按σ_A²∝n⁻²缩放A的初始化方案InitAA，显著拓宽稳定学习率区域并稳定非对称LoRA训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA对学习率选择高度敏感且脆弱；已有实证表明调好学习率的原版LoRA可与诸多变体竞争，因此核心问题从"设计新变体"转为"降低学习率选择的成本与脆弱性"。LoRA+用非对称学习率（η_B>η_A）缓解该问题，但比例η_B/η_A本身成为新超参：在标准LoRA初始化下，大比例虽可提升适配效果，却会导致训练不稳定和依赖比例的性能崩塌。作者因此要定位这种脆弱性的根源并加以消除。

Method: 对LoRA动力学做信号-噪声分解，指出在标准A初始化下更新中存在初始化诱导的噪声项，其量级受η_B、初始化尺度β和适配器秩r影响，且在大模型微调中不趋于零，从而限制LoRA+式非对称学习率的收益。为此提出InitAA：将A的方差按σ_A²∝n⁻²缩放，同时保持B₀=0，从而保持初始适配器输出为零。在秩与训练时间匹配的条件下，该初始化使初始化噪声能量再降低1/n倍。

Result: 实验上，InitAA在初始化尺度、适配器秩和LoRA更新乘子等维度上大幅拓宽稳定学习率区间；跨任务与模型降低最优比例漂移，避免标准初始化下的高比例崩塌，并使宽度准则η_B≈nη_A在有限预训练模型上成为有用的起点。

Conclusion: LoRA+式非对称学习率的脆弱性源于标准A初始化带来的非消失初始化噪声；通过方差缩放σ_A²∝n⁻²的InitAA初始化可在保持零初始输出的同时将噪声能量再降1/n倍，从而稳定训练、拓宽稳定学习率区域，并把η_B≈nη_A从理论启发变为有限预训练模型上的实用调参起点。核心贡献在于以低成本初始化改进替代不断设计新变体，降低学习率调优的脆弱性。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is often highly sensitive to learning-rate (LR) choices. Recent empirical studies suggest that, once LRs are carefully tuned, vanilla LoRA can remain competitive with many proposed LoRA variants. This shifts the central practical question from designing ever more variants to reducing the cost and brittleness of LR selection. LoRA+ addresses this issue with asymmetric LRs, using a larger LR for the up-projection matrix $B$ than for the down-projection matrix $A$. However, the ratio $η_B / η_A$ itself becomes an additional hyperparameter: large ratios can improve adaptation, but under the standard LoRA initialization they can also cause unstable training and ratio-dependent performance collapse. We identify the source of this brittleness through a signal--noise decomposition of LoRA dynamics. Under standard \initA, the update contains an initialization-induced noise term whose magnitude can be affected by the LR for $B$, $η_B$, the initialization scale $β$, and the adapter rank $r$. In practical LLM fine-tuning, this non-vanishing noise can limit the performance gains expected from LoRA+ style asymmetric LRs. To suppress this effect, we propose \initAA, a simple initialization that scales the variance of $A$ as $σ_A^2 \propto n^{-2}$ while keeping $B_0=0$. Under matched rank and training time for both initializations, this reduces the initialization-noise energy by an additional factor of $1/n$, stabilizing asymmetric LoRA training while preserving zero initial adapter output. Empirically, \initAA substantially widens the stable LR region across initialization scales, adapter ranks, and LoRA update multipliers. Across tasks and models, \initAA reduces best-ratio drift, prevents the high-ratio collapse observed with standard \initA, and makes the width-based guideline $η_B \approx n η_A$ a useful starting point for finite pretrained models.

</details>


### [127] [Sparsity-driven emergence of functional modularity in multitask neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32153)
*Bowen Shi,Qi Ouyang,Long Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏正则化可在无预设模块边界的多任务网络中诱导出功能性模块化：每层可识别出紧凑、任务选择性的"功能令牌"子网络，其在深层优先发育、可提取并重组以重建复合计算而无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络能学习多任务，但学习如何将共享计算组织为专门化、可复用的组件仍不清楚；该文探究稀疏性诱导的正则化能否在无预设模块边界下促成功能模块化。

Method: 在多任务MLP与轻量Transformer上进行功能剖析（functional profiling），借助稀疏诱导正则化定位紧凑的任务选择性子网络（功能令牌）；分析其随训练深度的发展、正则化强度与形式对模块紧凑性与专门化的影响、任务加权对功能分配的影响，并将提取的令牌通过替代通路重组重建复合计算。

Result: 规则类任务族中发现紧凑、任务选择性的功能令牌：选择性保留这些令牌可保持对应任务功能、降低其他任务表现；专门化优先在深层训练中形成而浅层保留共享计算；正则化强度与形式调节模块紧凑性与专门化，任务加权改变功能分配；提取的令牌可经替代通路重组，在保留连接与权重条件下无需重训练即重建学到的复合计算。

Conclusion: 稀疏约束优化不仅塑造参数使用方式，还塑造所学计算的组织与复用方式，为无监督结构发现与计算模块复用提供机制性见解。

Abstract: Neural networks can learn multiple tasks, but how learning organizes shared computation into specialized and reusable components remains poorly understood. In this work, we show that sparsity inducing regularization promotes functional modularity in multitask networks without predefined module boundaries. Using functional profiling in multilayer perceptrons and a lightweight transformer, we identified compact, task-selective subnetworks, termed functional tokens. Across rule-based task families, selectively retaining these tokens preserves their associated functions while reducing performance on other tasks. Such specialization develops preferentially in deeper layers during training, while earlier layers retain shared computation. Regularization strength and form shape module compactness and specialization, while task weighting alters functional allocation. Moreover, extracted functional tokens can be recombined through alternative pathways to reconstruct a learned composite computation without retraining, preserving their connectivity and learned weights. Our results suggest that sparsity-constrained optimization shapes not only parameter use but also the organization and reuse of learned computation.

</details>


### [128] [Curvature-Aware Clipping: Mitigating Bias in DPSGD via Approximate Second-Order Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32175)
*Xincheng Xu,Thilina Ranbaduge,Thierry Rakotoarivelo,David Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CA-DPSGD框架，利用近似二阶信息构建曲率感知的最优裁剪空间，并结合修正的曲率矩阵估计与各向异性噪声机制，缓解DPSGD中的裁剪偏差与几何失配问题，实验优于现有最优裁剪方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPSGD是深度网络隐私训练的事实标准，但其效用受梯度裁剪偏差与DP噪声限制。已有工作尝试缓解，但裁剪偏差引起的几何失配（geometric misalignment）仍是关键未解难题。

Method: 提出CA-DPSGD：借助近似二阶信息构造曲率感知的最优裁剪空间；引入针对曲率矩阵的修正估计器（rectified estimator），并配合各向异性噪声机制（anisotropic noise）以满足隐私保证。

Result: 大量实证实验表明，该方法在效用上优于当前最先进的裁剪方法。

Conclusion: 曲率感知的裁剪空间设计可有效缓解裁剪偏差导致的几何失配，为在严格隐私约束下提升DPSGD效用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DPSGD) has become the de facto standard for training deep neural networks with rigorous privacy guarantees. However, the utility of DPSGD is constrained by gradient clipping bias and DP noise. While existing work has tried to address these issues, the geometric misalignment caused by clipping bias remains a critical challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel framework, Curvature-Aware Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (CA-DPSGD), which leverages approximate second-order information to construct an optimal, curvature-aware clipping space. CA-DPSGD integrates a rectified estimator for the curvature matrix and is coupled with an anisotropic noise mechanism to ensure privacy guarantees. Extensive empirical experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art clipping methods.

</details>


### [129] [Searching with Sparse Histories: Cross-Task Architecture Search from Incomplete Search Logs and Zero-Cost Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32179)
*Yuefeng Xu,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种风险鲁棒策略，通过冠军初始化与成对排序器联合预训练，融合稀疏源日志与零成本代理，缓解跨任务NAS迁移的稀疏历史与失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）代价高昂，跨任务迁移可复用历史知识以降低成本；但现有方法在搜索历史稀疏时表现不佳，且存在任务特定的迁移失败风险。

Method: 一种风险鲁棒的迁移策略：结合稀疏源任务日志与零成本代理（zero-cost proxies）；采用基于冠军（champion-based）的初始化；并进行成对排序器（pairwise-ranker）的联合预训练。

Result: 在TransNAS-Bench-101上显著缓解最坏情况失效：Micro autoencoding的最差10%排名从290降至43，Macro room layout从465降至45；在13个cell-space目标任务中，有10个任务在均值与最坏情况性能上均优于纯代理排序器，同时保持有竞争力的平均性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效应对稀疏搜索历史与任务特定迁移失败，在多数目标任务上兼顾平均性能与最坏情况鲁棒性。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) is expensive, but cross-task transfer can reduce costs by reusing knowledge from previous tasks. However, existing methods struggle with sparse search histories and task-specific transfer failures. We propose a method, a risk-robust strategy that combines sparse source logs with zero-cost proxies through champion-based initialization and joint pairwise-ranker pretraining. On TransNAS-Bench-101, the proposal mitigates severe worst-case failures, reducing worst-10% ranks from 290 to 43 for Micro autoencoding and from 465 to 45 for Macro room layout. Across 13 cell-space targets, it outperforms a proxy-only ranker on 10 targets in both mean and worst-case performance, while maintaining competitive average performance.

</details>


### [130] [CFBrainAug: Modularity-Induced Counterfactual Brain Network Augmentation for Interpretable fMRI-based Autism Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32184)
*Yifan Qiao,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFBrainAug反事实脑网络学习框架，通过生成"标签翻转但拓扑相似"的反事实脑网络来实现数据增强与内在解释的统一，配合轻量SVM在ABIDE I的NYU站点达到74.1%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rs-fMRI脑网络机器学习用于ASD诊断易在小样本上过拟合，且事后统计归因（post-hoc attribution）的临床可解释性有限。需要一种同时缓解过拟合与提供内在可解释性的方法。

Method: CFBrainAug：先检索一个标签相反但拓扑相似的候选网络，再在模块数（module-number）约束下精化，得到标签改变的反事实脑网络；将真实（factual）与反事实网络联合训练，在决策边界附近扩充训练分布；分类器采用基于特征选择的轻量SVM，并以受试者与其反事实网络之间的连接差异作为内在解释。

Result: 在ABIDE I数据集的NYU站点上达到74.1%准确率，与近期图深度学习方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 反事实增强可补充复杂模型，适用于小样本脑网络分析，并在增强的同时提供内在可解释性。

Abstract: Machine learning on resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) brain networks is promising for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD), but existing methods are prone to overfitting on limited training data, and their post-hoc statistical attribution offers limited clinical interpretability. We propose CFBrainAug, a counterfactual brain network learning framework that unifies data augmentation and counterfactual explanation by learning label-altered brain networks. Unlike conventional label-preserving augmentation, CFBrainAug first retrieves a label-opposite yet topologically similar candidate and then refines it under a module-number constraint. Jointly training on factual and counterfactual networks enriches the training distribution around the decision boundary, enabling a lightweight feature-selection-based SVM to perform competitively with recent graph deep learning methods. Meanwhile, the counterfactuals provide intrinsic explanations by identifying the connectivity differences between each subject and its counterfactual. Experiments on the ABIDE I dataset show that CFBrainAug reaches an accuracy of 74.1% on the NYU site, suggesting that counterfactual augmentation can complement more complex models in small-sample brain network analysis.

</details>


### [131] [EVOLVE: Uncertainty-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Prediction with Adaptive Prototype Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32189)
*Jianxin Jin,Kai Zhao,Shiyan Hu,Yang Shu,Peng Chen,Meng Wang,Zhongwen Rao,Yijie Li,Jing Wang,Bin Yang,Chenjuan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督多变量时间序列异常预测框架 EVOLVE：用变量-上下文条件化扰动+不确定性感知的未来推理解耦现有扰动式范式的缺陷，在六个真实基准上稳定超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督 AP 方法遵循"扰动范式"，存在两点不足：(1) 扰动在不同变量与上下文间共享，忽略异常演化的变量/上下文依赖性——同一扰动在某变量中放大为异常动态，在另一变量中仅被视为正常波动；(2) 学习确定性的历史→未来映射，忽略未来演化的多模态性（相似历史可导致渐进退化或突变等多种合理轨迹）。

Method: EVOLVE 由两部分组成：(1) 自适应异常原型记忆库，动态更新并在变量级检索，构造上下文特定的异常扰动；(2) 不确定性感知推理：采样多个潜历史状态，经"平滑演化"与"偏差放大演化"互补假设解码，以多条未来轨迹间的分歧度估计异常风险。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，EVOLVE 一致优于最先进的异常预测基线。

Conclusion: 通过变量/上下文条件化扰动与多轨迹不确定性推理，EVOLVE 统一刻画了异常演化的依赖性与多模态性，验证了该范式在无监督异常预测中的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Unsupervised multivariate time series anomaly prediction (AP) aims to estimate future anomaly risks from current observations, providing early-warning signals for proactive intervention and maintenance in complex systems. Existing unsupervised AP methods follow a perturbation-based paradigm, where predefined or random perturbations are injected into historical windows to simulate abnormal future evolution. However, these methods share perturbations across different variables and contexts, and learn deterministic history-to-future evolution. In reality, anomaly evolution is variable- and context-dependent: the same perturbation may be amplified into abnormal future dynamics in one variable or context, while being absorbed as normal fluctuation in another. In addition, future evolution is not uniquely determined by a historical window, since similar histories may lead to multiple plausible trajectories, such as gradual degradation or abrupt changes. To address these problems, we propose EVOLVE, a unified framework that models unsupervised AP via variable- and context-conditioned perturbations and uncertainty-aware future reasoning. EVOLVE constructs context-specific anomaly perturbations through an adaptive anomaly prototype memory, which can dynamically update and retrieve relevant perturbations at the variable level. EVOLVE models uncertainty by sampling multiple latent historical states and decoding through complementary smooth and deviation-amplifying evolution hypotheses, and the disagreement among decoded future trajectories is used to estimate future anomaly risk. Extensive experiments on six real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that EVOLVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art anomaly prediction baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Dual-Adaptive Z-Score PPO: Dynamic Clipping Using Action Confidence and Normalized Advantage Magnitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32194)
*Taha M. Mahmoud,Naima Kaabouch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究PPO的自适应裁剪变体，提出PPO-DAZ3等方法，用样本相关的裁剪阈值替代固定裁剪参数，并在三个经典控制基准上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PPO的裁剪阈值固定，所有训练样本获得相同宽度的信任域，不考虑对应策略更新的可靠性，可能限制学习效率。

Method: 将常数裁剪参数替换为由策略与回报统计量计算的样本相关阈值。核心方法PPO-DAZ3用两个信号确定每个样本的裁剪区间：动作置信度（当前策略下所选动作的概率）和更新置信度（归一化的优势幅度）；同时提出平滑优势变体PPO-AZ3S。其余标准PPO训练流程保持不变。

Result: 在LunarLander-v3、CartPole-v1、Acrobot-v1三个基准上评估：PPO-DAZ3在LunarLander-v3上表现最佳，PPO-AZ3S在Acrobot-v1上领先，标准PPO在CartPole-v1上仍具竞争力。自适应裁剪在部分设定下提升学习效果，但并非全部。

Conclusion: 自适应裁剪在更困难的控制任务上收益最明显，简单任务上优势不明显。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) remains a popular on-policy reinforcement learning algorithm owing to its stability and ease of implementation. Its clipping threshold, however, is fixed, so every training sample receives the same trust-region width regardless of how reliable the corresponding policy update is. This work examines adaptive-clipping variants of PPO in which the constant clipping parameter is replaced by sample-dependent thresholds computed from policy and return statistics. The central method, Dual-Adaptive Z-Score PPO (PPO-DAZ3), sets the clipping interval for each sample from two signals: action confidence, given by the probability of the selected action under the current policy, and update confidence, given by the normalized advantage magnitude. The rest of the standard PPO training pipeline is left unchanged. Evaluation on three classic control benchmarks (LunarLander-v3, CartPole-v1, and Acrobot-v1) indicates that adaptive clipping improves learning in some settings but not all. PPO-DAZ3 performs best on LunarLander-v3, the smooth advantage-based variant PPO-AZ3S leads on Acrobot-v1, and standard PPO stays competitive on CartPole-v1, suggesting that adaptive clipping is most beneficial on harder control tasks.

</details>


### [133] [Principia: Test-Time Hypotheses to Principle Construction for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32195)
*Dongki Kim,Sangwoo Park,Jinheon Baek,Soyeong Jeong,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Principia——一个基于LLM的框架，在测试时从累积观测中构建并持续精炼科学原理（假设→原理管线），用于科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需从观测中提炼潜在的empirical principles以指导后续探索，但该翻译阶段依赖大量领域专家、时间与精力，成为发现的瓶颈。

Method: 模仿人类研究者：从相关输入及其观测结果形成比较性因果假设，跨假设综合出重复出现的关系归纳为原理；将构建的假设与原理应用于任务获得新观测，据此修正假设并由更新后的假设重建原理，实现测试时持续演化的知识。

Result: 在分子优化、蛋白质工程、基因扰动、分子性质预测等基准上优于依赖内部知识、外部来源或经验总结的基线，且生成的原理具有合理性并与科学文献一致。

Conclusion: Principia可在测试时有效构建与精炼任务特定的假设与原理，为科学发现提供可解释、可演化的知识支撑。

Abstract: Scientific discovery requires identifying the scientific principles underlying observed phenomena to guide further investigation. As these empirical principles are often unknown in complex discovery processes, they are constructed via a hypotheses-to-principle pipeline to obtain recurring causalities from progressively accumulated observations during test-time discovery. However, constructing this knowledge requires substantial domain expertise, time, and effort, making this translation stage a bottleneck for scientific discovery. To address this limitation, we propose Principia, an LLM-based framework that constructs and continually refines scientific principles at test time. Drawing on how human researchers develop principles, Principia forms comparative causal hypotheses from related inputs and their observed outcomes, then synthesizes recurring relationships across these hypotheses into inductive principles. By applying the constructed hypotheses and inductive principles to the task, it obtains new observations, revises the hypotheses used based on these new observations, and reconstructs principles from the updated hypotheses, allowing scientific knowledge to continuously evolve throughout test-time discovery. Across benchmarks spanning molecular optimization, protein engineering, gene perturbation, and molecular property prediction, Principia outperforms baselines relying on internal knowledge, external sources, or experience summaries, while generating principles that are plausible and consistent with the scientific literature, demonstrating its effectiveness in constructing and refining task-specific hypotheses and principles at test time for scientific discovery. Our code is available at https://github.com/DongkiKim95/Principia.

</details>


### [134] [Head Dimension Matters in Language Models for Long-Context Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32213)
*Seiya Tokui,Shoichiro Yamaguchi,Sosuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定模型规模下，增大注意力头维度（更少更宽的头）可显著提升长上下文检索性能，而预训练损失和短上下文非检索任务基本不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力中头维度决定每个头的表示空间大小，但在长上下文处理中，固定参数预算下应分配给"更少更宽的头"还是"更多更窄的头"尚不明确，本文旨在厘清头维度对长上下文检索能力的影响。

Method: 在固定模型规模（参数预算）条件下系统改变头维度开展实证研究，比较预训练损失、短上下文非检索任务与长上下文检索任务表现；统计并分析"检索头"比例；并通过在长上下文持续预训练中冻结注意力模块投影权重的消融实验，考察收益来源。

Result: 预训练损失与短上下文非检索任务性能对头维度不敏感；长上下文检索性能随头宽增大而提升；更宽头的模型具有更高比例的检索头，即更多参数参与检索；即使冻结注意力投影权重，更宽头仍带来检索性能提升。

Conclusion: 头维度是长上下文检索的关键设计变量，更宽的头不仅有利于注意力模块自身的更新，还能促进嵌入与MLP等组件的更有效适配，从而提升检索性能。

Abstract: Multi-head attention retrieves and combines context representations through query--key interactions and value aggregation, with head dimension constraining the per-head representation space. In long-context processing, it remains unclear whether allocating a fixed parameter budget to fewer, wider heads improves information retrieval. In this paper, we investigate how head dimension affects the performance of long-context retrieval tasks. Our empirical studies reveal that pretraining loss and short-context non-retrieval task performance are not affected much by head dimension, while long-context retrieval performance improves with wider heads at fixed model sizes. We find that models with wider heads have a higher proportion of retrieval heads, associating more parameters with retrieval. Our ablation studies also show that the retrieval performance improves with wider heads even when the projection weights in attention modules are frozen during long-context continual pretraining, suggesting that wider heads not only benefit updates in attention modules but lead to more effective adaptation of components such as embeddings and MLPs.

</details>


### [135] [MS-TTRL: Breaking the Frequency Conflict in Multiple-Solution Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32234)
*Zhixia Zhang,Binqian Xu,Huaiyang Wang,Yikun Ban,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MS-TTRL，通过结果级自验证分离“正确性估计”与“频率”，以解决测试时强化学习中的频率冲突问题，在多个多解推理任务上显著提升解集恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学、工程与数学推理任务存在多个有效解，而完整标注全部解集成本极高。测试时强化学习（TTRL）可在无标签测试实例上做强化学习，但在此场景下生成频率同时承担两个相互冲突的角色：正确性估计偏好高频结果，而结果层多样性保持偏好低频结果，作者将其称为“频率冲突”（Frequency Conflict）。

Method: 提出MS-TTRL：用结果级自验证（Outcome-Level Self-Verification）获得与生成频率无关的正确性估计，仅将频率用于多样性保持。具体包含三个组件：三态结果缓存（Three-State Outcome Cache）与候选状态（Candidate State），用于在重复观测间复用验证结果并对模糊结果做置信度感知处理；置信度门控逆概率缩放（Confidence-Gated Inverse Probability Scaling），仅对稀有但被可靠验证的结果增强学习信号。

Result: 在2个基准、4个多解任务、3个骨干模型上各以5个随机种子评估：MS-TTRL平均将Recovery Rate相比最佳无标签基线提升19.13个百分点，并接近有监督IPS-GRPO的性能。

Conclusion: 将正确性估计与生成频率解耦，可在无标签条件下有效缓解频率冲突，在保持解集多样性的同时提升多解推理的解集恢复能力，展现出接近有监督方法的潜力。

Abstract: Scientific, engineering, and mathematical reasoning tasks can admit multiple valid solutions, while exhaustive annotation of the full solution set is often prohibitively costly. Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) applies reinforcement learning to unlabeled test instances, offering a label-free approach to multiple-solution reasoning. In this setting, generation frequency serves two opposing objectives: correctness estimation favors frequent outcomes, whereas outcome-level diversity preservation favors less frequent outcomes. We term this tension the \textbf{Frequency Conflict}. To resolve this conflict, we propose MS-TTRL, which obtains frequency-independent correctness estimates through Outcome-Level Self-Verification while reserving generation frequency solely for diversity preservation. Three-State Outcome Cache and the Candidate State support verification reuse across repeated observations and confidence-aware handling of ambiguous outcomes, while Confidence-Gated Inverse Probability Scaling selectively strengthens the learning signals of rare, reliably verified outcomes. Experiments across two benchmarks, four multiple-solution tasks, and three backbone models, each evaluated with five random seeds, show that MS-TTRL improves Recovery Rate by 19.13 percentage points on average over the best-performing label-free baseline and approaches the performance of the supervised IPS-GRPO.

</details>


### [136] [Correct but Unselectable: The Hidden Interface Tax in Multi-Candidate Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32235)
*Qianli Ma,Zhiqing Tang,Fanshuai Meng,Zhi Yao,Hanshuai Cui,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文识别出多候选生成-重排序中的"hidden interface tax（隐藏接口税）"瓶颈：正确答案虽在候选池中，却因不完整、不可解析或与固定选择器期望的答案模式不匹配而无法被选中；提出覆盖-转换分解区分oracle覆盖与可选覆盖，并设计CACC兼容性感知候选构建层修复片段、对齐答案模式、去除干扰噪声，无需重训任一组件即可提升覆盖与最终准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在成本、延迟、隐私约束下无法在推理失败时切换到大模型，多候选生成-重排序是自然的测试时扩展途径；但候选增多并不必然提升准确率，生成与选择之间存在被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 提出覆盖-转换分解，将原始oracle覆盖与可选覆盖（正确答案能被选择器实际消费的子集）区分开；设计CACC作为候选源与固定选择器之间的接口层，进行片段修复、答案模式对齐、去除令选择器困惑的噪声，且不重训生成器或选择器。

Result: GSM8K与竞赛数学上，CACC同时提升oracle覆盖与最终准确率；GPQA Diamond上CACC单独将准确率从5.6%提至19.2%（+13.6 pp），结合proposer侧强化达25.8%（+20.2 pp）；MMLU-Pro上出现覆盖-转换缺口：答案模式兼容的构建提升oracle覆盖，但原选择器下最终准确率下降0.85 pp，加入proposer侧强化后最终准确率改善。

Conclusion: 生成-选择之间的接口失配是测试时扩展的关键瓶颈，仅提升覆盖不等价于提升准确率；CACC能有效弥合该缺口，但存在场景依赖——当选择器本身能力受限时（如MMLU-Pro），单纯接口修复不足，需配合proposer侧强化。

Abstract: Small language models deployed under cost, latency, or privacy constraints cannot simply switch to larger models when reasoning fails. Multi-candidate generate-then-rerank offers a natural test-time scaling route. However, more candidates do not always yield higher accuracy. We identify a previously overlooked bottleneck between generation and selection: a correct answer may appear in the candidate pool yet fail to be selected because it is incomplete, unparseable, or misaligned with the answer mode the fixed selector expects. We term this loss the hidden interface tax and formalize a coverage-conversion decomposition that distinguishes raw oracle coverage from selectable coverage, the subset of problems where a correct candidate can actually be consumed by the selector. We propose Compatibility-Aware Candidate Construction (CACC), an interface layer between the candidate source and the fixed selector that repairs fragments, aligns answer modes, and removes selector-confusing noise without retraining either component. On numeric reasoning (GSM8K, competition_math), CACC raises both oracle coverage and final accuracy. On GPQA Diamond, CACC alone improves final accuracy from 5.6% to 19.2% (+13.6 pp); combined with proposer-side strengthening it reaches 25.8% (+20.2 pp). On MMLU-Pro, CACC exposes a coverage-conversion gap: answer-mode-compatible construction raises oracle coverage, but final accuracy decreases by 0.85 pp under the original selector; adding proposer-side strengthening improves final accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [BiLoCo: Binary Low-Rank Corrections for Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32243)
*David Jin,Beshr IslamBouli,Tarushii Goel,Han Guo,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BiLoCo：将SVD低秩修正的左右因子存为±1二值符号向量，每秩一组件存储降16倍，修正变为加减法可在CUDA核上与FP4张量核GEMM并发执行；在4B–72B LLM上达到或优于同等内存预算的SVDQuant，B300融合内核在batch=1、rank≤512时最多仅慢11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4后训练量化（PTQ）可显著降低LLM推理成本，且Blackwell GPU原生支持FP4张量核，但直接PTQ到FP4仍存在明显精度差距。SVDQuant通过加入高精度低秩修正来缓解扩散模型的FP4量化误差，然而该低秩修正每秩需要大量比特，且与主FP4 GEMM争用同一张量核，导致精度与效率难以兼顾。

Method: BiLoCo将低秩修正的左右因子以二值{±1}符号向量存储，替代BF16数值；该有符号格式使每秩一组件存储量减少16倍，且修正运算由乘法转为加减，因而可放在CUDA核上，与FP4张量核GEMM并行执行。在B300上实现为单一融合内核：FP4 GEMM运行于张量核，位打包（bit-packed）修正运行于同一launch的CUDA核，两者在GEMM epilogue中合并。

Result: 在4B–72B LLM上，BiLoCo在内存匹配条件下达到或超过SVDQuant的PTQ精度。B300融合内核在batch size为1时，对所有不超过512的秩，最多比纯FP4 GEMM慢11%；在秩为128、batch size不超过8时最多慢20%。

Conclusion: 用二值符号向量表示低秩修正，可在极小存储开销下保持精度，并通过张量核（FP4 GEMM）与CUDA核（二值修正）的异构并发及epilogue融合，使附加延迟可控，为FP4 PTQ提供高精度、低开销的实用方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) to FP4 has emerged as a key technique for reducing large language model inference cost, especially on Blackwell GPUs, which offer native FP4 tensor core support. Direct PTQ to FP4, however, still leaves a noticeable accuracy gap. Recent work on SVDQuant mitigates the errors introduced through FP4 quantization for diffusion models by adding high-precision low-rank corrections obtained via SVD. However, this high-precision low-rank correction requires many bits per rank, and it runs on the same tensor cores as the main FP4 GEMM. We introduce BiLoCo, a low-rank correction method whose left and right factors are stored as binary ${\{\pm 1\}}$ sign vectors rather than as BF16 numbers. The signed format uses $16{\times}$ less storage per rank-one component, and since the correction now consists of additions/subtractions, this can be run on CUDA cores concurrently with the FP4 tensor-core GEMM. On 4B--72B LLMs, BiLoCo matches or improves memory-matched SVDQuant for PTQ. We implement BiLoCo on B300 as a fused single kernel, where the FP4 GEMM runs on tensor cores, the bit-packed correction on the CUDA cores of the same launch, and the two are combined in the GEMM epilogue.
