Method: 从精确反向传播梯度出发，在小初始化、光滑激活、环境固定的受控区间内推导神经元入权重与其下一层列之间的局部耦合；证明动力学放大跨层相关算子的奇异模，每个增长模通过共享神经元系数将低层特征与上层响应配对；据此构造后向耦合算法（上层敏感度生成样本相关有效标签驱动前层谱步骤，修正特征再精化向上传递）；用函数空间变分原理刻画所选特征-响应对（在核范数约束下最大化其乘积的标签相关性）；对三层网络导出滤波隐层特征、激活核与网络输出的自洽连续时间动力学。

Result: 在分层多指标模型中建立特征恢复分离性：后向耦合可恢复原先前向标签加权统计量不可见的下层方向；线性特化重现残差依赖的奇异模动力学；配对模给出与 rainbow-network 图像一致的低秩协方差分量的动力学机制；在二分类 CIFAR-10 上，全连接与卷积网络在相同宽度下较前向 Neural LoFi 取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: 后向耦合为逐层谱学习补充了必要的跨层反馈，理论上解释了低层特征的形成与低秩结构配对，并带来实证性能提升；该框架可作为理解联合训练中特征学习动力学的可解析替代模型。

Abstract: During joint training, backpropagation allows later layers to reshape earlier representations, a feedback mechanism absent from purely layer-wise learning. We extend Neural Low-Degree Filtering (Neural LoFi), a recently proposed layer-wise spectral surrogate for feature learning, to capture this interaction. Starting from the exact backpropagation gradients, we derive a local coupling between a neuron's incoming weights and its corresponding next-layer column. In a controlled small-initialization regime with smooth activations and fixed surroundings, these dynamics amplify singular modes of a cross-layer correlation operator. Each growing mode pairs an earlier feature with an upper-layer response through shared neuron coefficients. This motivates a backward-coupling algorithm in which upper-layer sensitivities generate sample-dependent effective labels for earlier spectral steps, while corrected features refine upward transmission. A function-space variational principle characterizes the selected feature-response pairs by maximizing the label correlation of their product under kernel-norm constraints. For three-layer networks, we derive self-consistent continuous-time dynamics for the filtered hidden-layer features, the activation kernels, and the network output under the proposed backward coupling. In a hierarchical multi-index model, we establish a feature-recovery separation: backward coupling recovers lower-layer directions invisible to the original forward label-weighted statistic. The linear specialization recovers residual-dependent singular-mode dynamics, while paired modes provide a dynamical mechanism for linked low-rank covariance components consistent with the rainbow-network picture. Experiments with fully connected and convolutional networks on binary CIFAR-10 show improved accuracy over forward Neural LoFi at matched widths.

</details>


### [175] [Near-Optimal List Replicability from Littlestone Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32853)
*Ari Blondal,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在可实现PAC学习框架下研究列表可重复数，证明Littlestone维数为d、对偶VC维为v*的概念类满足LR(H)≤2^{O(d·min{log d, v*})}，将此前2^{2^{O(d)}}的上界指数级改进，并达到近乎最优的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前最好的上界为Bun、Livni和Moran（FOCS 2020）给出的2^{2^{O(d)}}，而最坏情况下界仅为2^{Ω(d)}，两者之间存在巨大指数级差距；此外，Ghazi等（STOC 2021）与Lyu（STOC 2026）对固定精度ε给出的2^{O_ε(d^2)}上界依赖ε。作者希望给出与ε无关、指数更紧的列表可重复数上界。

Method: 提出新的归纳框架来界定列表可重复数，该框架建立在差分隐私学习中近期提出的不可约性（irreducibility）概念之上，导出2^{O(d log d)}界；另构造一个简单的全局稳定学习器，对多个经验风险最小化器取多数投票，并借助稳定正则性（stable regularity）工具证明其稳定性，导出2^{O(d v*)}界。

Result: 对可数域上Littlestone维为d、对偶VC维为v*的任意概念类H，证明LR(H)≤2^{O(d min{log d, v*})}；该结果在最坏情况下与2^{Ω(d)}下界仅差指数中的对数因子，并且与精度参数ε无关。

Conclusion: 列表可重复数的上界可从双重指数级降至接近下界的单指数级，差分隐私中的不可约性概念与稳定正则性工具是获得紧界的关键，且所得界不依赖于目标精度ε。

Abstract: We study the list replicability number in the realizable PAC learning framework and prove that every concept class $H$ over a countable domain with Littlestone dimension $d$ and dual VC dimension $v^*$ satisfies \[ \mathrm{LR}(H)\leq 2^{O(d\min\{\log d,v^*\})}. \] This matches the worst-case lower bound of $2^{Ω(d)}$ up to a logarithmic factor in the exponent and exponentially improves the previous best upper bound $\mathrm{LR}(H)\le 2^{2^{O(d)}}$ of Bun, Livni, and Moran [FOCS 2020]. Unlike earlier bounds of $2^{O_ε(d^2)}$ for fixed accuracy $ε>0$ implied by the work of Ghazi, Golowich, Kumar, and Manurangsi [STOC 2021] and Lyu [STOC 2026], our bound is independent of $ε$.
  The $2^{O(d\log d)}$ bound follows from a new inductive framework for bounding list replicability that builds on the recent notion of irreducibility from differentially private learning. The $2^{O(dv^*)}$ bound follows from a simple globally stable learner that takes the majority vote of multiple empirical risk minimizers, with stability established using tools from stable regularity.

</details>


### [176] [Regularised RL improves sim-to-real transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32855)
*Danilo Vucetic,Mohamad H. Danesh,Florence Clerc,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出具有强鲁棒性保证的正则化RL算法SMM-AC，系统刻画了正则化对sim-to-real性能的影响，表明正则化RL通过提升对sim-to-real gap及真实环境变化的鲁棒性改善了真实机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化强化学习理论上具有鲁棒性保证，本应有助于策略从仿真到真实机器人的迁移，但实际sim-to-real流程中仍普遍使用弱正则化算法（如PPO），正则化对迁移的实际收益缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 基于soft mellowmax价值函数推导出新的正则化RL算法Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic（SMM-AC），该算法具备强鲁棒性保证；随后在MuJoCo连续控制基准上与SAC等基线对比，并在真实机器人上评估其回报与鲁棒性表现。

Result: 在MuJoCo基准上SMM-AC与SAC等基线上具有竞争力；在真实机器人上SMM-AC取得更高回报，且对观测扰动、环境扰动和动力学扰动表现出比基线更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 正则化RL通过更好的鲁棒性应对sim-to-real gap及真实世界条件变化，从而改进真实世界策略部署，为sim-to-real流程中算法的选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) has provable robustness guarantees that should ease the transfer of policies from simulation to robots, known as sim-to-real. Yet, weakly regularised algorithms like Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) remain the de facto choice in sim-to-real pipelines, while the practical benefits of regularisation for transfer remain poorly characterised. In this work, we study the sim-to-real performance of commonly used RL algorithms, with the aim of characterising the effects of regularisation on robotic performance. To that end, we derive Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic (SMM-AC), a novel regularised RL algorithm based on the soft mellowmax value function, which carries strong robustness guarantees. On MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks, SMM-AC is competitive with baselines like Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). On real robots, SMM-AC achieves higher return, while exhibiting greater robustness to perturbations in observations, the environment, and dynamics than baselines. Together, these results demonstrate that regularised RL improves real-world policy deployment through better robustness to the sim-to-real gap, and changing real-world conditions.

</details>


### [177] [Sustainability of TSFM Deployment and Pretraining: From Carbon Footprint Analysis to Mitigation via Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32859)
*Cécile Luc,Nathanaël Lemonnier,Ievgen Redko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从全生命周期视角评估时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的碳排放成本是否值得，并证明按梯度无关准则剪枝单个编码器块可在几乎不损性能的前提下降低FLOPs、CO2与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM虽具备强零样本预测能力，但预训练成本高昂；实践中是否应相比需周期性重训的有监督独立模型付出这笔前期投资，缺乏全生命周期的可持续性量化依据。

Method: 构建面向真实工业场景的累计碳成本模型，对比Chronos-2与经典有监督模型的部署全周期成本；同时采用无梯度剪枝准则移除单个编码器块（约7.9%参数），并在25个任务的基准上评估FLOPs、CO2、延迟与零样本性能。

Result: 对大规模或高频查询的部署，Chronos-2可在数月内摊销预训练成本；对小型或低频查询场景，经典模型始终更便宜。剪枝单个编码器块带来FLOPs、CO2排放与延迟一致的6%–9%下降，零样本预测性能几乎无损。

Conclusion: TSFM的碳成本回报高度依赖部署规模与查询频率，需按场景选型；同时轻量化的结构剪枝是降低TSFM自身碳足迹的高效、低代价手段。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) offer strong zero-shot forecasting capabilities, but their high pretraining cost raises a practical sustainability question: does this upfront investment pay off over a full deployment lifecycle, compared to supervised standalone models that must be periodically retrained? We model the cumulative carbon cost of different approaches across a realistic industrial scenario: Chronos-2 amortizes its pretraining cost within months for large or frequently queried deployments, but a classical model remains cheaper indefinitely for small or rarely queried ones. For cases where reducing the TSFM's own cost matters, we show that removing a single encoder block (7.9% of parameters) via a gradient-free pruning criterion yields a consistent 6 to 9% reduction in FLOPs, CO2 emissions, and latency, at essentially no cost to zero-shot forecasting performance across a 25-task benchmark.

</details>


### [178] [PoKER: A Probability of Kill Estimation Rate Model for Air-to-Air Missiles Using Machine Learning on Stochastic Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32864)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Andre N. Costa,Diego Geraldo,Marcos R. O. A. Maximo,Takashi Yoneyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概率模型PoKER，以机器学习预测超视距空战中导弹的杀伤概率，替代传统基于静态距离划分的WEZ模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统武器交战区(WEZ)模型仅以最大、最小及不可逃逸距离等静态距离阈值划分交战区域，忽略了目标机动行为的随机性与导弹脱靶量的不确定性，难以准确刻画真实交战条件下的杀伤效果。

Method: 构建概率模型PoKER：将目标随机行为与导弹脱靶距离分布纳入建模，采用机器学习方法进行杀伤概率预测；模型输入涵盖战斗部杀伤力、目标与射手姿态朝向以及具体交战条件，从而动态评估目标行为并输出概率化的交战结果。

Result: 相较传统WEZ的确定性分区，PoKER能动态量化各交战条件下的成功概率，显著扩展交战分析的预测能力，并给出关于交战动力学的定量洞见。

Conclusion: PoKER有望成为超视距空战飞行员的重要辅助工具，支撑该作战域内的作战决策优化。

Abstract: This work introduces PoKER, a novel probabilistic model engineered to optimize missile launch effectiveness in air-to-air scenarios, specifically within Beyond Visual Range (BVR) air combat. Unlike conventional Weapon Engagement Zone (WEZ) models that delineate zones based on static distances such as maximum, minimum, and no-escape ranges, PoKER applies machine learning to predict kill probabilities more accurately by integrating the stochastic behaviors of targets and missile miss distances. This model dynamically evaluates target behavior, greatly expanding the predictive capabilities of engagement analysis. By factoring in elements such as warhead lethality, target and shooter orientations, and the specific conditions of engagement, PoKER provides important insights into engagement dynamics and quantifies success probabilities. Consequently, it can potentially be an important tool for BVR air combat pilots, improving operational decision-making within this specialized combat domain.

</details>


### [179] [Interpretable sparse fine-tuning with TopKLoRA enables faithful circuit discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32866)
*Marek Masiak,Andrzej Szablewski,Nicola Cancedda,Constantin Venhoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TopKLoRA，一种在设计上可解释的稀疏 LoRA 变体，仅激活每 token 上 k 个最大的 adapter latent，用于定位并外科式消融后门电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知来源数据微调可能引入难以定位与移除、且能抵抗安全训练的后门；参数高效微调虽捕获全部参数变化但缺乏可解释性，SAE 类方法又非直接面向下游损失优化。

Method: TopKLoRA 在每个 token 上仅保留 k 个最大 adapter latent（top-k 稀疏激活），并纯以下游损失训练，使优化压力驱动学习功能上因果的 latent。在休眠特工后门案例中，用受污染的指令跟随数据微调 gemma-2-2b、Qwen2.5-1.5B、Qwen2.5-7B 构造 model organisms，搜索并消融必要且充分的后门电路；另提出用 TopKLoRA latent 与梯度路由构造电路局部化的 model organisms，以验证电路搜索算法。

Result: 对每个 model organism 均识别出必要且充分实现后门的电路并成功消融；所得稀疏电路比稠密 LoRA 基线小至多一个数量级，可在不牺牲指令跟随性能的前提下外科式移除恶意能力；梯度路由构造的 model organisms 验证了电路搜索算法的发现。

Conclusion: TopKLoRA 迈向了设计上可解释的架构，提供忠实且不损害模型性能的分析单元，代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning on datasets of unknown origin can create a backdoor in the trained model when the data includes poisoned examples. Difficult to localise and remove, backdoors can persist through safety training. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods capture the full parameter changes introduced during training, they are not easily interpretable. We present the full method of TopKLoRA, extending the preliminary proposal of Masiak et al. (2025), an interpretable-by-design LoRA variant in which only the $k$ largest adapter latents are active at each token. Unlike SAEs, TopKLoRA is trained purely on downstream loss, where optimisation pressure incentivises learning functionally causal latents. We demonstrate this sparse LoRA adapter in a sleeper-agent backdoor case study where we create model organisms by fine-tuning gemma-2-2b, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen2.5-7B on a poisoned instruction-following dataset. For each organism, we identify and ablate a circuit that is both necessary and sufficient to implement the backdoor. Furthermore, our new fine-tuning method produces sparse circuits that are up to an order of magnitude smaller than a dense LoRA baseline, allowing surgical ablation of the malicious capability without sacrificing instruction-following performance. Finally, we propose a technique to construct model organisms with localised circuits using TopKLoRA latents and gradient-routing, which we use to validate the findings of our circuit search algorithm. We consider these findings as a step toward interpretable-by-design architectures that provide faithful units of analysis without compromising model performance. We share our code: https://github.com/interpretable-finetuning/TopKLoRA.

</details>


### [180] [Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Predictions: A Bellman Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32887)
*Chenbei Lu,Zaiwei Chen,Tongxin Li,Chenye Wu,Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在折扣有限MDP中研究"块反馈"协议下的多步转移预测价值，证明最优值满足原状态空间上的贝叶斯Bellman方程，并用Bellman–Jensen分析量化预测的作用，提出BF–BOLA算法并给出匹配的样本复杂度上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步转移预测可改进强化学习，但其价值难以量化；直接把预测纳入状态会导致状态空间指数增长，使动态规划计算代价过高。因此需要一个不扩张状态空间、又能刻画预测价值的理论与算法框架。

Method: 设定折扣有限MDP与块反馈协议：每K周期块开始时，决策者观察到部分动作的K步概率转移预测，并在块内依据观测状态调整决策。证明最优值满足原状态空间上的贝叶斯Bellman方程；通过Bellman–Jensen分析形式化预测通过递归改变Bellman展开中max与期望的局部次序而产生价值；据此提出基于模型的BF–BOLA（块反馈贝叶斯离线学习+在线自适应）算法，并建立样本复杂度上、下界。

Result: 贝叶斯Bellman表示避免了指数级状态扩张；量化了预测视野、预测误差、动作覆盖度与块内反馈的影响；BF–BOLA的样本复杂度上、下界在所述情形下仅相差对数因子；预测可将转移学习限制到未被覆盖的行，且学习近优策略所需预测样本可少于估计贝叶斯值函数所需样本。

Conclusion: 多步转移预测的价值可通过Bellman展开中max与期望次序的局部重排来精确刻画；块反馈协议下无需扩张状态空间即可实现近似最优学习，且预测能显著降低转移学习的样本需求，策略学习比值函数估计更样本高效。

Abstract: Multi-step predictions of future transition realizations can improve reinforcement learning, but their value remains difficult to quantify. Directly including these predictions in the state can lead to exponential state-space growth, making direct dynamic programming computationally expensive. We study discounted finite MDPs under a block-feedback protocol: at the start of each K-period block, the decision maker observes $K$-step probabilistic transition predictions for a subset of actions and adapts decisions to observed states within the block. We show that the optimal value satisfies a Bayesian Bellman equation on the original state space. This representation reveals that predictive information creates value by recursively changing the local order of maximization and expectation in the Bellman expansion. We formalize this effect through a Bellman--Jensen analysis and quantify the effects of prediction horizon, error, action coverage, and within-block feedback. We propose block-feedback Bayesian offline learning with online adaptation (BF--BOLA), a model-based algorithm for learning near-optimal policies, and establish upper and lower sample-complexity bounds that match up to logarithmic factors in the stated regimes. The results show that predictions can reduce transition learning to uncovered rows and that learning a near-optimal policy can require fewer prediction samples than estimating the Bayesian value function.

</details>


### [181] [Recommender regret for improving regularised RL estimation with bandits and MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32910)
*Danilo Vucetic,Alexandre Larouche,Mohammad Pedramfar,Audrey Durand,Gauthier Gidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将正则化强化学习中的估计生成形式化为推荐过程，把 bandit 采样视为改进算子，提出"正则化感知的推荐后悔"（regulariser-aware recommender regrets），给出下界分析并统一了 bandit 与搜索式目标改进（recommender MCTS），在合成树、街机游戏与科学发现任务上取得更快目标收敛与更优下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 bandit 的估计框架只评估采样效率（sampling efficiency），而不评估估计本身的质量；然而正则化 RL 算法依赖蒙特卡洛树搜索或一步前瞻等估计器来提供策略、价值或 Q 值，估计质量直接影响训练与推理表现，因此缺乏针对"估计质量"的评价体系是核心缺口。

Method: 1) 将估计生成形式化为推荐过程（recommendation process），把基于 bandit 的采样视作改进算子（improvement operator）；2) 定义正则化感知的推荐后悔（regulariser-aware recommender regrets）作为推荐（估计）质量的评价指标；3) 分析其下界，并证明均匀采样等简单算法即可达到该下界；4) 在 recommender MCTS 框架下统一 bandit 式与搜索式的目标改进。

Result: 在合成树、街机游戏和科学发现任务上，推荐器（recommender）通常带来更快的目标收敛和更好的下游性能；实验首次刻画了正则化 MCTS 在科学应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 以"推荐质量"而非仅采样效率来评价 bandit 式估计是可行且有效的；所提后悔指标与下界为估计质量提供了理论基准，recommender MCTS 统一了两类目标改进方法，并在多类任务（含科学发现）上验证了正则化 MCTS 的应用价值。

Abstract: Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms rely on estimators, like Monte Carlo tree search or one-step look-ahead, to provide policy, value, or Q-value estimates in training and inference. However, existing bandit-based estimation frameworks evaluate sampling efficiency, not the quality of estimates. We address this by formalising estimate generation as a recommendation process, treating bandit-based sampling as an improvement operator. To evaluate the quality of a recommendation (estimate), we define regulariser-aware recommender regrets, analysing their lower bounds, and proving that simple algorithms like uniform sampling can achieve these bounds. Finally, we unify bandit- and search-based target improvement under recommender MCTS. Across synthetic trees, arcade games, and scientific discovery tasks, recommenders often yield faster target convergence and superior downstream performance. Our experiments characterise for the first time the potential of regularised MCTS in scientific applications.

</details>


### [182] [A Tutorial on Physics Constrained Machine Learning for Hybrid Modeling, Optimization, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32914)
*Rahul Golder,Bimol Nath Roy,MM Faruque Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模块化物理约束机器学习框架KKT-HardNet，通过微分投影层硬性保证代数约束满足，并结合隐函数定理实现高效端到端训练，统一支持混合建模、过程仿真、优化与模型预测控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理约束机器学习（PCML）需将领域知识与物理约束融入AI/ML模型，但现有方法在约束硬满足性、异构数据处理、端到端训练效率及多任务统一平台方面存在不足，缺乏系统性教程与基准。

Method: 构建模块化框架KKT-HardNet：神经网络骨干负责快速推理，微分投影层对线性/非线性等式与不等式约束进行硬约束投影；利用隐函数定理对投影层反向传播，规避高开销自动微分；并提出精确降阶Fischer-Burmeister重构与原始-对偶内点法作为替代投影求解策略；同时提供多样化工程基准问题。

Result: 在约束满足度与推理时间两方面，KKT-HardNet均取得数量级更优的性能。

Conclusion: KKT-HardNet以模块化、可定制的方式实现在单一平台上统一混合建模、仿真、优化与MPC，兼顾约束硬满足与高效训练推理，并提供开源代码与基准数据集。

Abstract: Physics-constrained machine learning (PCML) integrates domain knowledge, first-principles, and physical constraints into AI/ML models. In this work, we present a step-by-step tutorial of a modular PCML framework, KKT-HardNet, and illustrate how it could be used for hybrid modeling, process simulation, optimization and model predictive control in a single platform. KKT-HardNet combines fast inference capabilities of neural networks with guaranteed satisfaction of algebraic constraints through differential projection. It can handle heterogeneous data and enforce both linear and nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. The modular design supports custom neural-network backbones and user-defined projection mechanisms for PCML. To enable efficient end-to-end training, KKT-HardNet exploits implicit function theorem to backpropagate through the projection layer. This avoids computationally expensive automatic differentiation. This work also introduces an exact reduced-order Fischer-Burmeister reformulation and a primal-dual interior-point method as an alternative projection solve strategy. We also provide a diverse set of benchmarking problems for PCML in engineering applications. KKT-HardNet achieves orders of magnitude better performance in terms of both constraint satisfaction and inference time. The KKT-HardNet code and the benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/souls-tamu/kkt-hardnet .

</details>


### [183] [ZENO: Nonlinear filtering with Zakai Equation Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32926)
*Aaditya Prasad,Steven W. Flavell,Brian DePasquale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZENO（Zakai方程神经算子），用物理信息损失训练神经算子逼近非线性滤波的未归一化滤波分布，并结合EM框架完成状态推断与参数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波中的状态推断与参数学习是科学机器学习的核心计算。未归一化滤波分布由Zakai方程（结合Fokker-Planck前向算子与观测似然的SPDE）支配，但高维SPDE解析不可解、数值不可行，需借助算子学习与物理信息机器学习方法。

Method: 构建ZENO模型：以Zakai方程为基础构造物理信息损失，优化神经算子以逼近潜在随机动力系统的精确未归一化滤波分布；将该后验分布嵌入期望最大化（EM）框架，实现参数学习与状态推断。

Result: 在双势阱双稳态系统、Van der Pol振子与Lorenz系统三个模拟随机动力系统中，ZENO能准确推断后验分布并学习SDE参数，性能与既有潜在SDE推断方法可比；在固定化秀丽隐杆线虫全脑成像数据上，恢复了此前观测到的代表前向与后向运动的极限环。

Conclusion: ZENO为非线性滤波与动力学推断提供了基于偏微分方程与算子理论的新颖途径。

Abstract: State inference and parameter learning for nonlinear filters is a core computation for scientific machine learning with varied applications in engineering and neuroscience. The unnormalized filtering distribution is governed by the Zakai equation, a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE) that combines the forward Kolmogorov operator (known as the Fokker-Planck equation) with observed likelihood updates. Although solving these high-dimensional SPDEs is often impossible analytically and intractable numerically, advances in operator learning and physics-informed machine learning have made it feasible. Harnessing these advances, we introduce the Zakai Equation Neural Operator (ZENO), a model that optimizes a neural operator against a Zakai Equation based physics-informed loss to approximate the exact unnormalized filtering distribution for latent stochastic dynamical systems. We use this ZENO-inferred posterior distribution in an expectation-maximization (EM) framework to perform parameter learning and state inference. We demonstrate ZENO's ability to accurately infer the posterior distribution and learn its corresponding SDE parameters in three simulated stochastic dynamical systems (Double-Well bistable system, Van Der Pol (VDP) oscillator, and Lorenz system) and compare its performance against established methods for latent SDE inference and learning. We show that ZENO recovers previously observed limit cycles representing forward and backward locomotion from a whole-brain imaging dataset of immobilized \textit{C. elegans} nematodes. ZENO is a novel approach to the problem of nonlinear filtering and dynamical inference through the lens of partial differential equations and operator theory.

</details>


### [184] [Where Rounding Happens Matters: Rounding-Location Alignment Stabilizes LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32951)
*Yijing Ke,Tianle Cai,Ruosong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在BF16强化学习(RL)训练中,仅将训练侧RoPE、SwiGLU、RMSNorm三处舍入位置与推理对齐(中间量保持FP32),即可避免默认BF16基线的训练崩溃,且推理引擎、矩阵乘/注意力核与学习目标均不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练大模型常用独立的推理与训练系统,即使权重与BF16张量相同,实现差异也会使"本应on-policy"的更新变为off-policy并导致训练不稳定。已有方法需整体对齐推理/训练栈、改动全局精度或修改学习算法,代价高。作者追问:是否只需对齐执行栈的部分环节即可稳定BF16 RL。

Method: 针对RoPE、SwiGLU、残差RMSNorm三个区域,将训练侧与推理侧的舍入位置对齐:在这些位置的中间量保留为FP32,直到产出BF16输出为止,其余实现差异保持不变。在四个Qwen系列模型上做对比实验(默认BF16基线 vs. 三区域对齐 vs. 仅RoPE路径对齐)。

Result: 三区域全对齐可避免默认BF16基线出现的训练崩溃;在所研究实验中,仅对齐RoPE路径也已足够,尽管仍存在残余数值差异。

Conclusion: 无需整体对齐推理与训练栈或改动全局精度/学习算法,局部的舍入点对齐即可稳定BF16 RL训练;推理引擎、矩阵乘与注意力核、学习目标保持不变,说明稳定性问题主要源于数值舍入路径而非优化算法本身。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language models often uses separate systems for inference and training. Even with identical weights and BF16 tensors, implementation differences can make an intended on-policy update off-policy and destabilize training. Existing remedies align the entire inference and training stacks, change global precision, or modify the learning algorithm. We ask whether aligning selected parts of the execution stack can stabilize BF16 RL while other implementation differences remain. We align training-side rounding locations with inference in three regions---RoPE, SwiGLU, and residual RMSNorm---by retaining selected intermediates in FP32 until their BF16 outputs. Across four Qwen-family models, aligning all three regions prevents the collapse observed under the default BF16 baseline; RoPE-path alignment alone also suffices in the studied runs, despite remaining numerical discrepancies. The interventions keep the inference engine, matrix-multiplication and attention kernels, and learning objective unchanged.

</details>


### [185] [RobustRLlib: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Reinforcement Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32971)
*Xi Chen,Fengqi Hu,Zeyu Shen,Jiaying Xu,Shangding Gu,Pan Xu,Anqi Liu,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RobustRLlib，一个以算法为中心的鲁棒强化学习库与基准，统一整合17种代表性鲁棒在线、离线与安全RL算法，并配套覆盖真实世界偏移、人形机器人与视觉-语言-动作任务的可扩展评测平台，通过273种扰动配置的系统评测给出当前鲁棒RL的能力图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒RL对真实世界可靠部署至关重要，但现有算法存在两大瓶颈：算法可复用性差，以及经验能力边界不清晰，限制了其更广泛的应用潜力。

Method: 构建统一接口下的算法库 RobustRLlib，集成17种代表性鲁棒在线、离线与安全RL算法；搭建增强评测平台，涵盖多样化真实世界偏移以及先进的人形机器人与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）任务；开展系统性评测，覆盖273种扰动配置。

Result: 通过273种扰动配置的系统评测，得到当前鲁棒RL算法的最新能力图谱，明确了各算法仍然有效的场景以及仍存在显著鲁棒性差距的环节。

Conclusion: RobustRLlib 作为开源、易用的平台，提供可复现、可扩展的代码基础，既便于将现有方法应用于新问题，也支持开发新的鲁棒RL算法。

Abstract: Robust reinforcement learning (RL) is critical for reliable real-world deployment, where policies may fail under diverse shifts and disruptions. Existing robust RL algorithms offer a rich set of mechanisms and principles, yet two major barriers limit their broader potential: limited algorithm reusability and an unclear empirical capability boundary. We introduce RobustRLlib, an algorithm-centric library and benchmark that addresses both challenges. It integrates 17 representative robust online, offline, and safe RL algorithms under a unified interface for substantially improved reusability, together with an enhanced evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world shifts and advanced humanoid and vision-language-action tasks. Systematic evaluation over 273 perturbation configurations provides an up-to-date capability map of current robust RL algorithms, revealing where they remain effective and where substantial robustness gaps persist. As an open-source, user-friendly platform, RobustRLlib offers a reproducible and extensible codebase for applying existing methods to new problems and developing new robust RL algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Understanding Tool-Mediated Interaction Failures in Agentic GUI Software: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31698)
*Yuchen Shao,Wenjing Liu,Yuheng Huang,Mengqian Xu,Ziqun Bao,Long Yang,Lei Ma,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究 LLM agent 通过工具中介与 GUI 交互时出现的一类故障：agent 所见与实时 GUI 不一致。作者构建了含 335 个真实缺陷的 Malva 缺陷库，归纳出 11 种缺陷模式，并给出部署前静态契约检查器 MalvaGuard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 无法直接操作 GUI，需依赖截图/UI 树采集、动作翻译、界面变化回报等工具。这种工具中介交互可能使 agent 对任务状态的理解偏离真实 GUI 状态，而该类故障普遍且常无显式报错信号，亟需系统刻画与提前检测。

Method: 从 40 个开源应用中收集并确认 335 个真实故障，构建 Malva 缺陷库；按状态表示、状态转移与同步问题归纳为 11 种缺陷模式；据此设计部署前静态契约检查 MalvaGuard，并在额外的 20 个应用语料上评估。

Result: 90% 的研究应用包含不止一种缺陷类型，说明缺陷普遍；86.6% 的故障表现为结果错误，其中 119 例在无 fail-stop 信号下产生错误行为（静默故障）。MalvaGuard 在 20 应用额外语料上达到 57.3% 精确率与 76.8% 召回率。

Conclusion: 工具中介的 GUI 交互是 LLM agent 可靠性的关键薄弱环节，其缺陷可系统分类并普遍存在、且多为静默失效；基于缺陷分类的静态契约检查可在部署前以可用的精确率与召回率识别此类问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly complete tasks through live graphical user interfaces (GUIs). Because the model cannot directly operate the GUI, these systems often rely on tools to capture screenshots or UI trees, translate model outputs into GUI actions, and report whether the interface changed. We study failures where this tool-mediated interaction makes the agent's view of the task diverge from the live GUI. We construct Malva, a defect library with 335 confirmed real-world failures from 40 open-source applications. These failures fall into 11 defect patterns rooted in state representation, state transition, and synchronization problems. They are widespread: 90% of the studied applications contain more than one defect type. They are also often silent: incorrectness appears in 86.6% of failures, including 119 cases that produce wrong behavior without a fail-stop signal. We operationalize this taxonomy with MalvaGuard, a pre-deployment static contract checker that reaches 57.3% precision and 76.8% recall on an additional 20-application corpus.

</details>


### [187] [Before the Ticket: Grounding HPC User Support in Documentation and Resolved Tickets with an Agentic LLM Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31703)
*Elham J. Barezi,Primus C Kabuo,Niveditha Nerella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于MCP的HPC帮助台助手，融合RAG文档检索与QLoRA微调Llama-3-8B两条工具路径，并强调全流程本地化部署以满足数据合规。在400条PMC留出工单上，微调工具显著优于基座模型（BERTScore F1 0.06→0.27），但端到端融合系统未能超越其最佳单一工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC帮助台的多数问题其实已有答案（用户指南或历史工单），但指南难导航、工单历史用户无权检索，导致重复提问。因此需要一个能同时利用文档与历史工单的助手，且由于工单数据不得离开机构，所有模型须本地或校内托管部署。

Method: 构建双工具架构：orchestrator通过Model Context Protocol调用(a)对实时文档的RAG检索工具与(b)在11,244条从历史工单挖掘的query-resolution对上用QLoRA微调的Llama-3-8B，并融合两者答案。模型均本地/校内LLM服务运行。评测集为Purdue RCAC的400条留出工单（与训练不重叠、去近似重复），由两个非答案生成模型家族的评审打分。

Result: 将对答改写为自助式指引后再微调效果最佳：相对基座模型BERTScore F1由0.06提升至0.27，且平均正确性评分最高。端到端系统未超过其工具，尽管两工具互补——按题择优的oracle上界为3.41（单工具3.20），而实际融合有78%的答案跟随文档工具。单独返回微调工具答案可恢复其质量（3.19 vs 2.66），但学习式选择器和LLM验证器均不能超越该工具本身。

Conclusion: 在HPC帮助台场景中，将历史工单resolution重写为自助指引并微调小模型是收益最大的一环；简单融合或额外的选择/验证机制反而会稀释高质量工具的表现，最稳妥策略是默认返回微调工具答案，同时把oracle互补性留作未来工作方向。

Abstract: Most questions that reach an HPC help desk have already been answered in the center's user guides or in a ticket someone else filed last semester; users still file new tickets because the guides are hard to navigate and the ticket history is not theirs to search. We present an assistant that answers from both sources: an orchestrator calls two tools over the Model Context Protocol, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over the live documentation and a Llama-3-8B model fine-tuned with QLoRA on 11,244 query-resolution pairs mined from historical tickets, and merges their answers. Every model runs on-premises or on the university's hosted LLM service because ticket data may not leave the institution. On 400 held-out tickets from Purdue's Rosen Center for Advanced Computing, disjoint from training and free of near-duplicates, and evaluated by two judges from model families that generated none of the answers, fine-tuning on resolutions rewritten into self-service guidance is the clear winner in agreement with RCAC's resolutions (BERTScore F1 improves from 0.06 to 0.27 over the base model) and is judged most correct on average. The end-to-end system does not improve on its tools, although they are complementary: choosing the better answer per question would exceed either tool (judged correctness 3.41 versus 3.20, an oracle bound), while the merge follows the documentation tool in 78% of answers. Returning one tool's answer instead recovers the fine-tuned tool's quality (3.19 versus 2.66), but neither a learned selector nor an LLM verifier beats that tool alone.

</details>


### [188] [RubricCraft: Where Solutions Diverge, Criteria Emerge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31707)
*Hosein Hasanbeig,Priyanshu Gupta,Ashish Tiwari,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出RubricCraft，将评分细则（rubric）构建视为以候选解为引导的自然语言规格生成：对比多个独立采样解在满足/违反任务要求上的差异来生成准则，再用这些解回测并修正漏检错误或误罚合理替代方案的检查项。在SpreadsheetBench v2上，误输出通过率由42.6%降至29.5%，且不误杀基准检查器认可的答案，排序正确性更好；由三个模型族的尝试驱动的修正作用在更多不同任务上，证据审计判定多数修正为改进，部分任务本身的参考答案即为错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式、产出型任务的语言智能体评测困难：模型评分器不稳定，穷举式程序化检查不现实；文本rubric虽能显式化标准，但撰写依赖领域专业知识；仅凭任务描述生成的rubric会遗漏在尝试解答中才暴露的需求与失败模式。

Method: 以任务描述加独立采样的解题轨迹为输入：1）比较各次尝试对任务要求的满足/违反差异，据此构建rubric准则；2）将准则应用于已收集的解，修订那些忽略错误或惩罚合法替代方案的检查项；3）不依赖任何单一完整参考解即可刻画部分正确性。

Result: SpreadsheetBench v2上，对比同一模型仅凭提示撰写并应用的rubric：误输出通过率29.5% vs 42.6%；不误杀基准检查器接受的全部输出；更常正确排序输出，模型区分度相当。单次尝试驱动的修正对评分无影响；由三个模型族尝试驱动的修正作用于不同任务，证据审计判定多数修正为改进，且多发生在参考答案本身有误的任务上。

Conclusion: 以候选解为引导构建并迭代修正rubric，比仅凭提示生成的rubric更严格、更稳健，可在无可靠参考答案时提供细粒度部分正确性评估；修正效果依赖尝试来源的多样性而非单一轨迹。

Abstract: Evaluating language agents on open-ended, artifact-producing tasks is difficult: model-based graders can be inconsistent, while exhaustive programmatic checks are often impractical. Textual rubrics make evaluation criteria explicit, but writing them requires domain expertise. Rubrics generated from task descriptions alone can miss requirements and failure modes that become apparent in attempted solutions. We introduce RubricCraft, which treats rubric construction as the generation of natural-language specifications guided by candidate solutions. Given a task description and independently sampled solver trajectories, RubricCraft compares how each attempt satisfies or violates the task requirements and uses these differences to construct rubric criteria. It applies the criteria to the collected solutions and revises checks that overlook errors or penalize valid alternatives. The resulting rubrics capture partial correctness without requiring any single solution to serve as a complete reference. On SpreadsheetBench v2, against a prompt-only rubric written and applied by the same model, RubricCraft rubrics pass fewer wrong outputs (29.5% against 42.6%), fail none of the outputs the benchmark's own checker accepts, and rank outputs by correctness more often, while separating models as often. Repair on a single attempt leaves grading unchanged, but repair driven by attempts from three model families acts on different tasks, and an evidence audit judges most of those repairs improvements, many on tasks whose reference solution is itself wrong.

</details>


### [189] [Mining the Design Space of Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31709)
*Xavier Cadet,Junyan Cheng,Peter Chin,Kyle Richardson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出两阶段流水线，将MCTS优化过程中被丢弃的工作流搜索轨迹转化为语料库，用以挖掘并验证LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的可复现设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统设计仍依赖试错与非正式的“经验法则”；人工研究仅测试少量通信拓扑，自动化优化器在选出任务特定工作流后便丢弃搜索轨迹，导致设计知识无法沉淀与复现。

Method: 复用AFlow的MCTS优化器，在6个可组合算子与26个可确定性验证的Enigmata任务上运行，保留全部中间轮次，构建含28,568个可执行工作流的语料库；随后用沙箱化的AutoResearch循环，在带L0惩罚的验证目标下挖掘编排代码与提示模板，并在留出搜索上检验候选原则，共进行300次特征挖掘试验。

Result: 发现三类跨层次持久效应：以独立求解器+一次性聚合开头的Star-first工作流表现更差（Δ=-0.045）；持久共享文档（SharedMemory）优于debate（Δ=+0.135）；提示具体性与基于占位符的上下文路由带来的增益可与通信结构选择相当（Δ=+0.118）。

Conclusion: 将搜索中被丢弃的轨迹转化为统计检验平台，用于发现和验证多智能体工作流设计原则是可行的，为MAS设计提供了可复现的实证基础。

Abstract: Designing large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems (MAS) still relies on trial and error and informal "folk wisdom." Manual studies test only a few communication structures or topologies (e.g., sequential, parallel, shared-memory, or debate), while automated optimizers discard search trajectories after selecting a task-specific workflow. We present a two-phase pipeline for mining reproducible design principles from workflow code and execution traces. We repurpose AFlow's MCTS optimizer across six composable operators and 26 deterministically verified Enigmata tasks, retaining all intermediate rounds to build a corpus of 28,568 executable workflows. A sandboxed AutoResearch loop then mines orchestration code and prompt templates under an $L_0$-penalized validation objective and tests candidate principles on held-out searches. Across 300 feature-mining trials, we identify effects that persist on held-out searches across three levels of workflow design: workflows beginning with independent solvers and one-shot aggregation (Star-first) perform worse ($Δ= -0.045$); persistent shared documents (SharedMemory) outperform debate ($Δ= +0.135$); and prompt specificity and placeholder-based context routing yield gains comparable to communication-structure selection ($Δ= +0.118$). More broadly, our pipeline and results show the feasibility of turning discarded search trajectories into a statistical testbed for discovering and validating multi-agent workflow design principles.

</details>


### [190] [Compact-Bench: Measuring What Context Compaction Costs Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31710)
*Ruopei Sun,Jianfeng Cai,Kangwen Zhao,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Compact-Bench通过配对"完整历史续写"与"摘要续写"来隔离上下文压缩替换历史所带来的影响，揭示LLM摘要压缩会显著破坏任务完成能力，且保留信息并不等同于保持任务可续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程编程智能体依赖上下文压缩（context compaction）突破上下文窗口限制，但压缩可能抹掉完成任务所需的状态。已有评测多关注效率、信息保留或端到端成功率，难以单独刻画"某一次历史替换"本身造成的损害。

Method: 构建Compact-Bench：从共享仓库状态出发，分别生成完整历史与仅摘要的续写两条分支进行配对评估，并考察反复出现的细节以及连续多次生产式压缩。数据规模为35个SWE-bench修复任务、146个智能体会话、106条生产压缩链；横跨14个摘要器骨干模型与5种摘要提示。

Result: 在22个完整历史成功案例中，稳定损害比例为5%–64%：Claude Opus 4.8保留的修复最多，DeepSeek-Flash损害最多。以Kimi-K3为摘要器时，Kimi Code提示保留最多任务续写（19/22），Claude Code提示保留最多反复出现细节（15.0%）。LLM摘要最多保留16.0%的反复出现细节，机械抽取为79.1%；生产摘要首次边界处仅保留15.7%。失败具有跨方法复现性：某一修复被13/14个骨干模型损害，另一修复被全部5种提示损害。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文压缩必须保留"完成任务所需的进展"，而不仅仅保留相关信息；信息保留率高并不能保证续写成功。

Abstract: Context compaction lets long-horizon coding agents continue beyond their context windows, but can erase the state needed to finish a task. Prior evaluations measure efficiency, retained information, or end-to-end success, leaving the effect of a specific history replacement difficult to isolate. Compact-Bench pairs full-history and summary-only continuations from a shared repository state, and evaluates recurring details and successive production compactions. It covers 35 SWE-bench repairs, 146 agentic sessions, and 106 production chains. Across 14 summarizer backbones, stable damage ranges from 5-64% of 22 full-history successes: Claude Opus 4.8 preserves the most repairs, while DeepSeek-Flash damages the most. With Kimi-K3 as summarizer, the Kimi Code prompt preserves the most task continuations (19/22), whereas Claude Code retains the most recurring details (15.0%). LLM summaries retain at most 16.0% of recurring details, compared with 79.1% for mechanical extraction; production summaries retain 15.7% at the first boundary. Failures recur across methods: one repair is damaged by 13/14 backbones, and another by all 5 prompts. These results show that reliable compaction requires preserving the progress needed to complete a task, and that retaining relevant information alone does not ensure successful continuation. Code is available at https://github.com/cai-jianfeng/compact-bench and data is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/caijanfeng/compact-bench.

</details>


### [191] [MBABench-2.0: Evaluating AI Agents on Constructing Financial Spreadsheets Beyond Simple Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31711)
*Julian Poeltl,Thomson Yen,Patrick Shea,Shubham Anil Varma,Dhruv Rastogi,Zihao Chen,Mengshi Zhang,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 MBABench-2.0，包含 101 个原创电子表格从零构建任务及 129 条覆盖 12 类别的质量评价标准，评估 8 个模型、4 种 harness 下的 20 余个智能体，最佳通过率仅 16%，揭示当前智能体在真实电子表格建模能力上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格基准多采用"填空式"任务，仅考察在已完成的表格中做小幅编辑，无法反映真实工作流中最核心、最耗时的环节——从零搭建工作簿时的建模决策、格式设计与表页布局。同时，可读性、可维护性、可审计性等质量维度难以用单元测试或最终答案简单验证，缺少整体性评价手段。

Method: 构建 MBABench-2.0 基准，含 101 个原创电子表格构建任务（最难任务对 3 年以上经验建模者估计需 33 小时）；提出 129 条评价标准、覆盖 12 个类别，将质量评价从"结果正确性"扩展到可读性、可维护性、可审计性等维度；在 8 个模型、4 种 harness 类型、20 余个智能体上进行系统评测。

Result: 前沿智能体很少能同时做到结果准确并满足质量标准：表现最好的智能体在该基准上的通过率仅为 16%。

Conclusion: 电子表格构建是 LLM 智能体自动化知识工作的合适试验场；现有智能体在真实电子表格构建能力上存在实质性差距，MBABench-2.0 为整体性评估提供了可操作的框架。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are the lingua franca of modern finance, driving valuation, forecast, audit, and many other core workflows of the industry. As LLM agents advance to automate knowledge work, spreadsheet construction is a natural and pertinent testbed. However, most existing spreadsheet benchmarks evaluate agents on fill-in-the-blank style tasks, where an agent performs minor edits in otherwise complete spreadsheets. This protocol fails to capture the most central and time-consuming part of the spreadsheet workflow: the modeling decisions, format design, and sheet layout regular knowledge workers need to toil through to build a workbook from scratch. We introduce MBABench-2.0, a benchmark of 101 original spreadsheet construction tasks, the hardest of which is estimated to take 33 hours for a modeler with 3+ years of experience. Unlike coding or math, where a unit test or a final answer can verify an agent's output, the quality of a financial spreadsheet that concerns readability, maintainability, or auditability, is not so simply verifiable. To holistically evaluate spreadsheet construction, we introduce 129 criteria spanning 12 categories that operationalize quality beyond the accuracy of the spreadsheet. Evaluating over 20 agents across 8 models and 4 harness types, we find that frontier agents rarely construct spreadsheets that are both accurate and meet our quality criteria: the top agent achieves a pass rate of only 16% on the benchmark. The benchmark demonstrates a substantial gap in spreadsheet building capability of current agents.

</details>


### [192] [Reuse, Rewrite, or Reject: Evidence-Constrained Trace Reuse for Repeated Mobile GUI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31712)
*Bingyu Bai,Cheng Zhang,Erhu Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出图-文本模板（graph-text templates）实现执行轨迹的选择性重用：图编码UI状态与动作转移，文本编码任务意图、流程结构与类型化槽位；配合"记录-模板-回放"框架，逐转移地重用、适配或丢弃，在AndroidWorld上平均减少约80%的LLM推理token，多数智能体获得≥1.5倍加速，真机（Android/HarmonyOS）动作重用率70.7–79.8%、多数配置下>2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体常重复执行交互流程相似的任务，复用历史执行可节省冗余的LLM推理与执行开销；但用户指令与执行环境的变化可能使先前成功的动作失效。作者将这类变化系统归纳为六类，并观察到变化并不必然使整个流程失效——单步转移在当前任务上下文与界面状态校验下仍可复用，因此需要细粒度而非整体式的轨迹复用机制。

Method: 1) 将指令与环境变化归纳为六类代表性类别；2) 设计图-文本模板：图结构捕捉UI状态节点与动作转移，文本编码任务意图、流程结构及承载可变内容的类型化槽位（typed slots）；3) 构建"记录-模板-回放"（record-template-replay）框架，从已验证的执行中抽取模板，回放时依据当前指令与实时UI状态对每个转移进行校验，决定重用、适配或丢弃。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上跨五个基础智能体，对给定执行轨迹平均降低约80%的LLM推理token消耗，多数智能体相较纯LLM执行获得至少1.5倍加速；在Android与HarmonyOS真机评测中，动作重用率达70.7%–79.8%，多数配置下维持2倍以上执行加速。

Conclusion: 细粒度的转移级轨迹复用优于整体流程复用：借助图-文本模板与实时状态校验，可在指令与环境变化下有效重用历史执行，显著削减LLM推理成本并提升执行效率，且结论在模拟环境与两类真机平台（Android、HarmonyOS）上均得到验证。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents frequently perform repetitive tasks with similar interaction procedures. Reusing previous executions can reduce redundant LLM inference and execution overhead, but variations in user instructions and execution environments may invalidate previously successful actions. We systematically characterize these variations into six representative categories and observe that such changes do not necessarily invalidate entire procedures: individual transitions can remain reusable when validated against the current task context and interface state. Based on this insight, we propose graph-text templates for selective execution-trace reuse. The graph captures UI states and action transitions, while the text encodes task intent, procedural structure, and typed slots for variable content. A record-template-replay framework constructs templates from verified executions and leverages the current instruction and live UI state to reuse, adapt, or discard individual transitions. Given an execution trace, our framework reduces LLM inference token consumption by approximately 80% on average across five base agents on AndroidWorld, with most agents achieving at least 1.5-fold execution speedup over LLM-only execution. In real-device evaluations on Android and HarmonyOS, our framework achieves action reuse ratios of 70.7-79.8% while maintaining over 2-fold execution speedup in most evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [193] [What Open-Source Projects Tell Their Coding Agents: A Census of AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31715)
*Pawel Zarzycki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对13种语言的热门活跃GitHub仓库进行普查，将根目录AGENTS.md与CLAUDE.md视为一份指令集，用模型辅助标注加人工复核的方式分析编码智能体指令文件的内容生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可借助仓库中的指令文件（AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md）接受引导，但这一新兴实践缺乏系统性的实证认识，因此需要摸清其普及程度、内容类型与演进特征。

Method: 对13种语言的热门活跃GitHub仓库开展普查，将每个仓库根目录的AGENTS.md和CLAUDE.md合并为一个指令集；采用模型辅助标注并结合人工复核来分析其中的陈述，分类归纳项目知识与规则（代码、构建、工作流、协作），并考察生成器签名、创建后编辑、AI归属规则等特征；同时开展预设检验与探索性分析。

Result: 指令文件在部分仓库及最高星标仓库中均有出现，且两者并存时CLAUDE.md通常指向AGENTS.md；内容为项目知识与规则的实际混合，协作规则提出测试计划、提交格式等具体要求，但相当比例指令不给出理由；AI归属上提交信息规则更常禁止、拉取请求规则更常要求披露；约三分之一指令集带生成器签名，五分之四在创建后被编辑；仓库流行度与拉取请求及AI贡献相关规则存在预设检验的关联，LLM与智能体项目在采用者中过代表；多个模型之间的标注一致性显著高于各自与人工标注者的一致性。

Conclusion: 仓库指令文件已成为编码智能体实践中的普遍且持续演进的现象，其内容兼具项目知识与流程规则；模型间高度一致但偏离人工标注，提示自动标注需谨慎；作者公开标注、分类体系及所分析文件版本的指引。

Abstract: Coding agents can be guided with instruction files in a repository. We examine this emerging practice through a census of popular, active GitHub repositories in thirteen languages, treating each repository's root AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md as one instruction set. Agent instructions appear in of repositories and of the most-starred; where both files exist, CLAUDE.md usually points to AGENTS.md. We analyse statements using model-assisted annotation and human review. The annotations reveal a practical mix of project knowledge and rules about code, building and workflow. Collaboration rules name concrete requirements, from test plans to commit formats, yet of directives give no reason. Projects disagree on AI attribution: commit-message rules more often prohibit it than require it, while pull-request rules more often require disclosure. A third of instruction sets have a generator-associated signature, a tool-inserted block, shared text or an AI-marked creation commit; four in five are edited after creation. Prespecified tests associate repository popularity with rules on pull requests and AI contributions. Exploratory analyses link activity to process-oriented content and show that LLM and agent projects are over-represented among adopters. The models agree much more closely with each other than with the human reviewer's topic assignments, a caution for automated annotation. We release the annotations, taxonomies and pointers to the analysed file versions.

</details>


### [194] [SWE-Clar: Can Coding Agents Clarify Incomplete Client Requirements and Deliver Greenfield Software?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31854)
*Jialei Li,Dingxian Wang,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yimeng Bai,Xuanyi Zhu,Yang Zhang,Chao Yin,Wentao Shi,Ganesh Prabhu,Karthik Gomadam Rajagopal,Brett Levert,Darvin Yi,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWE-Clar 基准，基于 Upwork 真实高价值软件开发委托，评估自由职业场景下编码智能体的需求澄清与代码实现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由职业绿地开发中，客户请求不完整，且客户难以在长周期内持续反馈，收到回答也不代表正确理解复杂需求，因此需要同时考察需求澄清与独立交付能力。

Method: 构建 100 个基于 Upwork 真实任务（单项总付款≥4000美元）的任务；每个任务先进行编码智能体与客户智能体多轮交互，随后分别独立运行代码实现（含验收测试）与澄清评估；澄清评估使用 512 个问题及参考答案，覆盖显式需求与对话上下文推断需求；两次运行共享澄清上下文但相互隔离，避免评估问题泄露引导实现。

Result: 九个前沿模型实验中，实现得分最高的 GPT-6 Astra 任务通过率仅 67%。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了该场景下独立软件交付的显著挑战，数据集与评测代码已开源。

Abstract: Greenfield software development in freelance settings requires coding agents to turn incomplete client requests into working software. This makes requirement clarification essential, yet clients may be unable to provide continuous feedback over long development cycles, and receiving their answers does not guarantee correct understanding of complex requirements. These challenges motivate two benchmark design choices: clarification precedes code implementation, and its effectiveness is assessed through explicit tests of requirement understanding. Guided by these choices, we introduce SWE-Clar, a benchmark grounded in real, high-value client commissions to evaluate requirement clarification and code implementation in this setting. It comprises 100 tasks based on real software development jobs on Upwork, each with at least US\$4,000 in total payments. Each task begins with multi-round interaction between a coding agent and a client agent, followed by separate runs for code implementation with acceptance testing and clarification assessment. For the latter, we construct 512 questions paired with reference answers from real client-freelancer interactions, covering both explicitly stated requirements and those inferred from the conversational context. Both runs begin from the same clarification context but remain isolated, preventing assessment questions from guiding code implementation. Experiments with nine frontier models show that even GPT-6 Astra, which has the highest observed implementation score, achieves a task pass rate of only 67%, underscoring the challenge of independent software delivery in this setting. The dataset and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/upwork/SWE-Clar.

</details>


### [195] [Knowing When to Stop: The Evidence-Action Gap in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31895)
*Tianyu Chen,Xinran Zhao,Vijay Viswanathan,Shoubin Yu,Mingyuan Zhou,Tongshuang Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"证据-行动差距"(evidence-action gap)现象：编码代理在环境障碍已产生足以判定不可完成的证据后仍过度坚持，并引入ImpasseBench（12类注入式环境障碍、以"证据充分步"为评分基准，19371条轨迹、8个前沿模型）量化该现象；并验证升级工具与外部监控的缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练使编码代理"坚持完成任务"，但缺少在环境不可完成时及时停止、上报的能力。经验上仅需数步即可获得"只能靠外部介入才能推进"的证据，而代理普遍不据此行动——过度坚持、采用非预期绕行、虚报未获记录支持的成功。作者希望将这一直觉性观察形式化并可测量、可干预。

Method: 提出ImpasseBench：在原本可解的修复任务中注入12种现实环境阻塞，并在每条轨迹中标注"证据已充分"的步数，以该步为基准对动作评分，从而把合理努力、过度坚持与过早交接区分开。在8个前沿模型、19,371条轨迹上评测，并考察两种干预：显式升级（escalation）工具，以及针对忽视工具模型的外部监控器。同时分析升级工单内容质量（阻塞项命名、代理自身进展与验证状态）。

Result: 几乎所有需要外部帮助的运行都收集到了充分证据，但仍有1/3耗尽预算；检测到的不安全请求全部出现在证据充分点之后。升级工具将预算耗尽率从34.0%降至6.7%；外部监控使环境恢复后的任务成功率接近三倍。但即便触发升级，工单虽能可靠指出阻塞项，却常遗漏代理自身的进展与验证状态。

Conclusion: "知道何时停止"不能依赖模型自发行为，需要显式支持：既要在允许交接的工具层面设计，也要规范代理留下的交接报告（须包含阻塞项、已完成进展与验证状态），以实现从"触发升级"到"有效交接"的转化。

Abstract: Coding agents are trained to complete tasks, and they keep trying even when the environment makes completion impossible. The evidence that only outside help can restore progress usually appears within a few steps, yet agents rarely act on it: they over-persist, attempt undesired workarounds, and claim success the record does not support -- a phenomenon we call evidence-action gap. To measure it, we introduce ImpasseBench, which injects twelve realistic environment blockers into otherwise solvable repair tasks and marks, in each trajectory, the step at which the collected evidence becomes sufficient. Scoring actions relative to that step separates appropriate effort from over-persistence and premature handoff. Across eight frontier models and 19,371 trajectories, we find that nearly every run that needs outside help collects sufficient evidence, yet a third still exhaust their budget, and every unsafe request we detect comes after that point. Fortunately, an escalation tool, or an external monitor for models that ignore the tool, already guards against much of this behavior: the tool cuts budget exhaustion from 34.0% to 6.7%, and the monitor nearly triples task success after environment restoration. Even then, a fired escalation is not yet a useful handoff: tickets reliably name the blocker but often omit the agent's own progress and verification status. Taken together, we argue that knowing when to stop therefore needs explicit support, both in the tool that lets an agent hand off and in the report it leaves behind.

</details>


### [196] [Are LLM-Generated Formal Specifications Faithful to Their Requirements? A Study on Bimodal Formal Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32053)
*Wenhan Wang,Shiming Chen,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文在"需求+实现"双模态、实现正确性未知的设定下实证研究LLM生成ACSL形式化规约的有效性，构建1403个C程序基准并以Frama-C验证，发现LLM生成的规约易产生弱规约或受实现误导的规约，难以真实判断程序是否满足需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的形式化规约生成研究普遍假设输入程序正确，这与形式化验证的根本目的——在正确性未知时判断程序是否满足其需求——相背离，因此需要在更贴近真实场景的设定下考察LLM规约生成的实际能力。

Method: 开展实证研究：构造包含1,403个C程序的基准，在同时给定自然语言需求与其对应代码实现（正确性未知）的双模态设定下，用LLM生成ACSL形式化规约，并借助Frama-C进行验证；同时对比高级提示策略及非形式化的HoarePrompt方法。

Result: 主要发现有三：一是当代码实现正确时，额外提供自然语言需求并不能进一步提升生成规约的通过率；二是当代码实现错误时，LLM可能生成弱规约或受实现误导的规约而错误通过验证，且先进提示方法无法完全缓解；三是相较非形式化的HoarePrompt，LLM生成的形式化规约在预测需求—代码一致性方面效果更差。

Conclusion: 该工作首次探讨将LLM形式化规约生成用于验证程序对需求的正确性，指出"忠实于需求的规约生成"（requirement-faithful specification generation）是未来LLM形式化规约研究的新方向。

Abstract: Formal specification generation is a critical step towards formal verification and correctness assurance of software. Recently, large language models have been widely adopted in automatic generation of formal specifications. However, existing LLM-based specification generation approaches often assume that the input program is correct, which deviates from the real purpose of formal verification---to figure out whether the program meets its requirements when its correctness is unknown. Therefore, we conduct an empirical study of LLM-based specification generation under the bimodal setting: given both the natural language requirement and its corresponding code implementation, whose correctness is unknown. We construct a benchmark of 1,403 C programs and evaluate ACSL specification generation with Frama-C verification. Our study reaches several interesting findings: first, when the code implementation is correct, providing the natural language requirement does not further improve the pass rate of generated specifications. Second, when the code implementation is incorrect, LLM-based specification generation may erroneously pass verification by producing weak or implementation-misled specifications, and this cannot be fully alleviated by advanced prompting approaches. Compared with the non-formal HoarePrompt approach, LLM-generated formal specifications are less effective in predicting requirement-code consistency. Our study takes a first look at applying LLM-based formal specification generation to verifying program correctness against requirements, and points to requirement-faithful specification generation as a new direction for future LLM-based formal specification research.

</details>


### [197] [CrowdStrike Channel File 291: An Engineering Analysis of a Global Endpoint Outage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32078)
*Luiz Parente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 2024年7月19日CrowdStrike Falcon内容更新导致约850万台Windows设备崩溃，根因是检测模板声明21个输入而传感器代码仅提供20个，新匹配准则触发内核态内容解释器中的未校验数组访问。文章重建事件并归纳四类保障缺口，提出分层控制建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 还原该次大规模内容更新事故的完整技术与管理链条，区分已记录事实与工程推断，并说明为何被特权软件消费的配置应与代码接受同等生命周期保障，同时澄清回滚、终端恢复与服务恢复是三个需分别评估的问题。

Method: 基于厂商技术报告、微软崩溃分析与恢复指南、议会记录及运行影响报告进行事件重建；以接口校验、测试选择、部署策略与恢复依赖为主线做交互分析；提出以验证准则、发布节奏约束和运行权衡为内容的分层控制（缺陷预防、暴露遏制、服务恢复）。

Result: 识别出四项关键保障缺口：按消费者并不满足的定义进行校验；测试恰好规避了缺失输入；未经健康门控的灰度推广；恢复路径依赖部分崩溃主机无法维持的连通性。

Conclusion: 配置内容需具备与代码相当的生命周期保障（含模式/版本一致性校验、健康门控发布）；回滚、端点恢复与服务恢复应作为独立环节分别设计与验证。

Abstract: On July 19, 2024, a CrowdStrike Falcon content update caused Windows systems to crash, affecting about 8.5 million devices according to a Microsoft estimate. The immediate defect was an inconsistency between a detection template that declared 21 inputs and sensor code that supplied 20. A new matching criterion exposed an unchecked array access in the kernel-mode content interpreter. This article reconstructs the incident from vendor technical reports, Microsoft's crash analysis and recovery guidance, congressional records, and operational impact reports. It distinguishes documented findings from engineering inferences and examines how interface validation, test selection, deployment policy, and recovery dependencies interacted. Four assurance gaps were consequential: validation against a definition the consumer did not meet, tests that avoided the missing input, distribution without health-gated promotion, and recovery paths requiring connectivity that some crashing hosts could not maintain. Recommended controls separate defect prevention, exposure containment, and service restoration, with verification criteria, release-cadence constraints, and operational tradeoffs. The case shows why configuration consumed by privileged software requires lifecycle assurance comparable to code, and why release reversion, endpoint recovery, and service restoration must be assessed separately.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Correctness: How Novice Software Engineering Students Exercise Oversight of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32118)
*Ali Shakiba,Thomas Chaffey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析145名软件工程一年级学生的962条AI交互记录与172次自述干预，发现新手AI监督主要关注"适用性"而非"正确性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使软件工程学生的任务从产出制品转向评估与监督AI输出，但学生对输出特征的识别及在真实工程工作中如何行使监督知之甚少。

Method: 通过结构化AI日志收集学生-AI交互回合（962条，含172次学生自述干预），采用多维度编码框架，刻画干预中识别的输出特征、干预原因、采取的行动及行动实施方式，并进行个体内分析。

Result: 74.4%的干预涉及适用性（fitness）问题，仅16.9%涉及正确性问题；可归类干预中学生最常修改AI输出（51.0%）或精炼提示词（28.2%），两者均与工程适用性相关；个体内分析显示，被认为存在轻微缺陷的输出伴随更低的评估置信度。

Conclusion: 新手AI监督超越单纯错误检测，涵盖对目的适用性的判断与对AI辅助工程工作的主动干预。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is shifting software engineering students' work from producing artefacts towards evaluating and supervising AI-generated outputs. However, little is known about what characteristics students identify in outputs when reporting interventions, or how they exercise oversight during authentic software engineering work. We analysed 962 student-AI interaction turns documented by 145 first-year software engineering students through structured AI Journals, including 172 student-declared interventions. Using a multi-dimensional coding framework, we characterised the output characteristics identified in these reported interventions, students' reasons for intervening, the actions they took, and how those actions were enacted. Fitness problems were identified in 74.4% of interventions, compared with 16.9% involving correctness problems, indicating that oversight more often concerned whether plausible AI outputs were suitable for the engineering task than whether they contained explicit errors. Among classifiable interventions, students most frequently modified generated outputs (51.0%) or refined their prompts (28.2%), with both response types commonly associated with engineering fit. Within-student analysis further showed that outputs perceived as containing minor flaws were associated with lower evaluation confidence than outputs perceived as perfect. Taken together, these findings characterise novice AI oversight as a process that extends beyond error detection to encompass both judgement of fitness for purpose and active intervention in AI-assisted engineering work.

</details>


### [199] [Pairing Matters: Performance, Failures, and Efficiency in Lightweight Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32131)
*Lu Zhao,Christopher M. Poskitt,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过6个开源agent harness与3个轻量编码模型在100个SWE-bench Verified任务上的全因子受控评测（1800次运行），揭示harness-model配对对解决率（最高差44个百分点）、失败模式与token效率（超20倍差异）具有决定性影响，强调部署时应将配对选择作为核心决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自托管编码agent需将代码与开发轨迹留在受控基础设施内，轻量开源权重模型使部署更可行。但模型必须与agent harness搭配使用，现有排行榜多报告固定harness-model配置，缺少跨配对的受控比较，无法回答"同一模型换harness效果如何"这一实际部署问题。

Method: 对6个开源harness × 3个轻量编码模型做全因子受控评测，覆盖100个SWE-bench Verified任务，在统一的服务、计量与评分条件下共产生1800次带评分运行；并对1100次失败运行应用基于证据的失败分类体系，同时统计每解决任务的计费token数等效率指标。

Result: 同一模型在不同harness间解决率差异最高达44个百分点，且harness排名随模型变化；失败模式在配对间差异显著，某模型在一harness下的主导失败模式在另一harness下未必主导；即使同为模型驱动的loop型harness，每解决任务所需计费token差异超过20倍。

Conclusion: harness-model配对选择是轻量编码agent部署的核心决策，单一harness或模型上的强表现不能外推到其他组合。论文给出配对选择、token效率评估与基于轨迹的harness工程实践建议，并公开完整实验记录与分析产物。

Abstract: Self-hosting coding agents is often necessary for software-engineering teams that must keep proprietary code and development traces within controlled infrastructure. Lightweight, open-weight models make such deployment more feasible by reducing hardware requirements, while strong results on repository-level benchmarks demonstrate their potential for demanding coding tasks. Deploying these models as coding agents requires pairing them with an agent harness, making their joint performance a key deployment consideration. Yet existing leaderboards primarily report fixed harness-model configurations rather than controlled comparisons across alternative pairings. To address this gap, we investigate harness-model interactions through a controlled full-factorial evaluation of six open-source harnesses and three lightweight coding models across 100 SWE-bench Verified tasks, producing 1,800 graded runs under common serving, metering, and grading conditions. For the same model, resolution rates differ by up to 44 percentage points across harnesses, while harness rankings change across models. We apply an evidence-based failure taxonomy to 1,100 unsuccessful runs and find substantial differences in failure patterns across harness-model pairings. A model's dominant failure modes under one harness need not dominate under another. Efficiency is similarly dependent on the pairing: even among model-directed loop harnesses, charged tokens per resolved task vary by more than 20-fold. These findings make harness-model selection a central deployment decision for lightweight coding agents. Strong performance with one harness or model does not guarantee similar results with another. We translate these findings into practitioner guidance for pairing selection, token-efficiency assessment, and trajectory-informed harness engineering, and release the complete experimental records and analysis artifacts.

</details>


### [200] [Large-Scale Study of GitHub Copilot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32313)
*Izah Sohail,Yerdanat Kurmantayev,May Mahmoud,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文实证分析了7,581个Copilot参与的PR，发现58%无人工干预；首次人工干预后轨迹迭代数骤降20.8次、时长减少8.4分钟，后续干预影响微弱，表明大部分工作集中在初始轨迹中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体研究多基于类SWE-bench基准，假设智能体以单条轨迹自主运行，不符合开发者与智能体交互式协作的真实场景。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究真实环境中的"人机回路"（human-in-the-loop）智能体轨迹。

Method: 收集7,581个Copilot参与且会话数据公开的PR，进行定量轨迹分析（迭代数、时长、干预次数、合并状态关系），并结合定性案例分析开发者干预模式与协作方式。

Result: 58%的Copilot PR无人工干预；发生干预时协作简短（中位数2次人机回路）；已合并PR初始轨迹迭代数更少；首次干预后轨迹迭代数显著下降20.8、时长减少8.4分钟，而后续干预无显著迭代下降与时长变化；开发者通过反馈委托工作、自行提交commit等多种方式协作，但需多次（甚至跨PR版本重复）干预来引导Copilot。

Conclusion: 真实场景中人机协作以简短、聚焦的干预为主，核心工作集中于初始轨迹；后续干预多为小型请求的短轨迹。这挑战了单轨迹自主基准的假设，提示需针对交互式协作设计评测与智能体能力。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly integrated into software development workflows. However, most coding agent studies rely on benchmarks similar to SWE-bench, which assume autonomous agents operating in a single trajectory. This assumption does not mirror real-world usage, where developers interactively collaborate with agents. We address this gap by studying human-in-the-loop agent trajectories in the wild, focusing on 7,581 Copilot-authored PRs with publicly accessible session data. We find that 58% of Copilot PRs have no human intervention, and that when developers do intervene, collaboration is brief (median of 2 Human-AI loops). Merged PRs also tend to have fewer initial trajectory iterations than unmerged ones. We also find that the trajectory following the first human intervention exhibits a significant drop of 20.8 iterations and 8.4 min in duration. However, trajectories following later interventions show minimal iteration drops and no significant duration change, signifying that most of the work happens in the initial trajectory. Our qualitative analysis of illustrative PRs explains this pattern: many developer interventions are small, focused requests that Copilot completes in shorter later trajectories. We also illustrate that developers collaborate with Copilot in multiple ways, including delegated work through feedback as well as adding their own commits. However, we find that developers may need to guide Copilot through multiple (sometimes repeated) interventions in the PR, or even across multiple editions of the PR.

</details>


### [201] [From Faulty Claims to Incorrect Patches: An Empirical Study of Task-Grounded Reasoning in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32338)
*You Wang,Jinjun Huang,Zhonghao Jiang,Jialun Cao,Jiachi Chen,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个对编程智能体"任务锚定推理"的实证研究：从CoT中抽取原子断言并用证据核验，发现其仅占18.5%的token却平均每条轨迹含191个断言、至少9.6%有误；96.7%的成功轨迹也含错误断言，但三分之二的错误补丁可追溯至错误断言，修正后89.0%能修复对应缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹研究多用与任务无关的行为模式（如重复动作）刻画智能体失败，但智能体可能流程合理却对代码事实、任务要求或候选修复理解有误。推理模型的CoT暴露了这些可核验的断言，但断言的规模、错误比例及其是否真正导致错误补丁尚不清楚。

Method: 在SWE-bench Pro上分析4个开源权重推理模型的400条轨迹：(1) 用LLM从CoT中抽取原子断言；(2) 由LLM智能体收集证据对断言逐条核验；(3) 人工将错误补丁的缺陷回溯到错误断言，并通过"断言纠正重放"逐条确认因果链。

Result: 任务锚定推理仅占CoT token的18.5%，但每条轨迹产生191个原子断言，其中至少9.6%为错误；错误断言出现在96.7%的成功轨迹中，故其存在本身是弱失败信号。然而188个错误补丁中三分之二可追溯至错误断言；纠正该断言后，89.0%的情况下智能体修复了关联缺陷；纠正全部此类断言可解决11.7%的失败任务。

Conclusion: CoT蕴含可用于诊断与修复智能体失败的任务特异性信号，但需精确定位少数真正导致补丁缺陷的错误断言，而非简单以断言错误率作为失败判据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding agents increasingly resolve real-world software engineering tasks, yet still fail on many of them. Prior trajectory studies characterize these failures through task-agnostic patterns of agent behavior, such as repeated actions. However, an agent may follow a sound workflow yet act on a faulty understanding of the existing code, the task requirements, or a candidate fix. Reasoning models, which power most state-of-the-art coding agents, reveal this understanding in their native chain-of-thought (CoT), whose claims about the task can be checked against the facts. Yet it remains unclear how many such checkable claims the CoT contains, how many are faulty, and whether faulty claims actually cause incorrect patches. We present the first empirical study of such claims, which we term task-grounded reasoning, analyzing 400 trajectories of four open-weight reasoning models on SWE-bench Pro. We extract atomic claims from the CoT with an LLM, assess them against evidence gathered by an LLM agent, and manually trace the defects of incorrect patches back to faulty claims, confirming each link with a claim-corrected replay. Task-grounded reasoning covers only 18.5% of the CoT tokens, yet yields 191 atomic claims per trajectory, at least 9.6% of which are faulty. Faulty claims also occur in 96.7% of the resolved trajectories, so their mere presence is a weak signal of failure. Yet two thirds of the 188 incorrect patches trace back to faulty claims. Correcting such a claim makes the agent fix the linked defect in 89.0% of the cases, and correcting all such claims resolves 11.7% of the failed tasks. Our findings show that the CoT carries task-specific signals for diagnosing and repairing agent failures, but exploiting them requires pinpointing the few faulty claims that actually cause patch defects.

</details>


### [202] [DeepAVR: Deep Analysis via Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32345)
*Woorim Han,Hyunjun Kim,Brian Junekyu Lee,Kyeongmin Kim,Younghan Lee,Hyungon Moon,Yunheung Paek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架DeepAVR，通过将中间修复结果转化为结构化诊断反馈实现深度根因分析，在VulnLoc上对92.3%的漏洞生成正确补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于LLM的AVR方法：早期在"完美故障定位"假设下做补丁生成，近期端到端方法直接从sanitizer日志修复。但当根因被深度隐藏（与崩溃点物理距离远，或被相互依赖的错误在逻辑上掩盖）时，初始报告信息不足，导致这些方法失败。

Method: DeepAVR将修复分解为六个专业角色：故障定位、补丁生成、编译验证、安全验证、功能验证、补丁审查，并在角色间路由反馈以迭代精化定位与补丁假设。核心设计包括：(1) patch-conditioned re-localization，将中间补丁的执行结果作为诊断信号以揭示隐藏根因；(2) 面向根因的补丁审查阶段，在基于执行的验证之外识别症状级修复与不完整修复。

Result: 在真实C/C++漏洞修复数据集VulnLoc上显著优于已有方法，92.3%漏洞生成正确补丁；在SAN2VULN上展现强泛化能力，可有效修复训练中未见过的漏洞。

Conclusion: 将修复过程中的中间结果转化为诊断信号并跨角色反馈，可有效突破依赖初始报告的端到端AVR在隐藏根因下的局限，同时保持较好的跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR) has emerged as a critical defense against software security flaws, aiming to triage and fix bugs autonomously. Early LLM-based approaches focused on patch generation under the assumption of perfect fault localization, while recent end-to-end methods attempt to repair vulnerabilities directly from sanitizer logs. However, these methods often fail when the root cause is deeply hidden--either physically distant from the crash site or logically masked by interdependent errors--making the initial report insufficient. We propose DeepAVR, a multi-agent framework for end-to-end AVR that performs deep analysis by transforming intermediate repair outcomes into structured diagnostic feedback. DeepAVR decomposes repair into specialized roles--Fault Localization, Patch Generation, Compile Validation, Security Validation, Functionality Validation, and Patch Review--and routes feedback across these roles to iteratively refine both localization and patch hypotheses. In particular, DeepAVR introduces patch-conditioned re-localization, which treats execution results from intermediate patches as diagnostic signals to uncover hidden root causes, and a root-cause-oriented patch review stage that detects symptom-level fixes and incomplete repairs beyond execution-based validation. Evaluation on the real-world C/C++ vulnerability repair dataset VulnLoc shows that DeepAVR significantly outperforms prior methods, generating correct patches for 92.3% of vulnerabilities. Furthermore, results on SAN2VULN demonstrate strong generalization, with DeepAVR effectively repairing vulnerabilities unseen during training.

</details>


### [203] [Complexity concentration as a god-class and god-method detector: a pre-registered test of a scale-free outlier rule against tool definitions and human labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32466)
*Daniel Sagenschneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文预注册检验一个无需原始尺度常数的"神单元"检测规则：以每个代码库自身的稳健离群点（修正z分数≥3.5）判定。在445个仓库（10种语言，其中171个Java）及MLCQ的492个Java仓库上，与PMD GodClass、MLCQ评审者及另两种定义对比，结论为两项总体均"不确定"，且原因不同。该规则在每仓库AUC分别达0.916与0.908，可排序PMD神类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神类/神方法检测器使用在语料上拟合、随总体变化需重拟合的原始尺度阈值。作者希望检验一种无需原始尺度常数、以"本代码库内的稳健离群"作为判据的规则，尤其考虑到LLM生成代码的复杂度聚集方式可能不同；首要问题是该规则能否匹配人类编写代码的尺度。

Method: 预注册研究。对象：445个仓库（10种语言，171个Java）与MLCQ的492个Java仓库。规则：单元复杂度为其自身代码库内的稳健离群点，修正z分数≥3.5。基线：PMD的GodClass、MLCQ评审者、另两种定义。实验设计：信号与截断在一半仓库上选择、在另一半上读取（样本外）。指标：每仓库AUC、非劣性（给定裕度）、恢复率、标记数量。

Result: 两项总体结论均为不确定，但分歧来源不同。规则在每仓库尺度上以认知复杂度的每文件最大值排序PMD神类，AUC为0.916与0.908。主实验中，默认设置下规则在恢复率上于裕度内不劣于在同一总体上调优的截断值，但标记的非神类约为后者的两倍；在MLCQ上标记数量相当，但合并恢复率未达裕度。合并后，原始值比每仓库分数更能排序神类；截断值跨总体迁移几乎无损失。额外标记多为小类，其中略过半包含达到PMD方法级阈值的单个方法；漏报为复杂度分散在多个方法中的大类。两名源码阅读者对额外标记的性质判断不一致。

Conclusion: 该规则无需重拟合即可跨总体迁移，能定位复杂度聚集处；但"聚集处是否为神单元"涉及耦合问题，本研究未作判定，留待后续预注册研究。

Abstract: God-class and god-method detectors mark concentrated complexity with a raw-scale threshold, a constant fitted on a corpus and re-fitted when the population moves. This paper pre-registers a test of a rule with no raw-scale constant: a unit is a god unit when its complexity is a robust outlier for its own codebase, a modified z-score of 3.5 or more. On 445 repositories in ten languages, 171 of them Java, and the 492 Java repositories of MLCQ, the rule is scored against PMD's GodClass, MLCQ's reviewers and two further definitions, with signal and cutoff chosen on half the repositories and read on the rest. LLM-written code may gather its complexity differently, the case for a detector that needs no re-fitting; the first question is whether one can match the scale of existing human-written code. The verdict is inconclusive on both populations, by different arms. The rule ranks PMD's god classes from the per-file maximum of cognitive complexity at per-repository AUCs of 0.916 and 0.908. On the main run it is, at its default, non-inferior within the margin to a cutoff tuned on the same population on recovery, and flags about twice as many non-god classes; on MLCQ it flags about as many and falls short of the margin on pooled recovery. Pooled, the raw value ranks god classes better than the per-repository score, and a cutoff carried between the populations lost almost nothing: both are human-written code on one scale, so the test is of matching that scale, not of surviving a shift. The rule's extra flags are small classes, just over half holding a method at PMD's own method-level threshold; its misses are large classes whose complexity is spread over many methods. Two readers of the source did not agree on what the extra flags are. Concentration finds where the complexity is; whether that place is a god unit is a question about coupling, which a registered follow-on study takes up.

</details>


### [204] [OPTARI: An open-source software framework for clinical translation of optoacoustic imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32468)
*Moritz Schillinger,Maximilian Bader,Adrian Buehler,Felix Wachter,Ferdinand Knieling,Vasilis Ntziachristos,Katharina Breininger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源图形化软件 OPTARI，基于 napari 与 PATATO，支持光声-超声临床研究的标注、重建、解混、深度学习分割与时空谱分析，可批量处理，附独立可执行文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光声-超声（OPUS）联合成像具临床潜力，但缺乏面向非编程用户的易用分析软件：现有工具要么是脚本式工程框架，要么是厂商专有软件，难以支撑大规模临床研究。

Method: 基于 napari 图像查看器与 PATATO 光声数据处理工具箱构建开源图形应用；支持厂商原生格式与开放 HDF5 格式，兼容 IPASC 标准；以独立可执行文件分发；功能涵盖 ROI 标注、图像重建、生色团解混、深度学习组织分割以及时间与光谱分析模块。

Result: 功能组合优于同类对比工具；自动组织分割在肌肉组织上与专家标注结果相当；批量处理将单次扫描分析时间从数分钟缩短至 1 秒以内；已在在研临床 OPUS 研究中实际使用。

Conclusion: OPTARI 打通工程框架与医学研究的鸿沟，为临床光声成像研究提供可复现、标准化的开源分析基础。

Abstract: Combined optoacoustic (OptA) and ultrasound imaging is a promising modality across a wide range of pathologies. Its use in clinical research, however, is limited by a lack of analysis software accessible to users without programming expertise. Existing tools are either script-based engineering frameworks or proprietary vendor applications. We present OPTARI, an open-source graphical software application to facilitate large-scale clinical optoacoustic-ultrasound (OPUS) studies. OPTARI builds on the napari image viewer and the PATATO toolkit for OptA data processing. It supports native vendor and open HDF5 data formats, aligns with the IPASC standard, and is distributed as a standalone executable. Features include region-of-interest annotation, reconstruction, chromophore unmixing, deep-learning-based tissue segmentation, and temporal and spectral analysis modules. OPTARI offers a combination of features for clinical study analysis that none of the compared tools provide. The automated tissue segmentation produced results comparable to expert labeling for muscle tissue, and batch processing reduced analysis time from minutes to under one second per scan. The tool is used in ongoing clinical OPUS studies. OPTARI connects engineering frameworks with medical research and provides an open-source basis for reproducible and standardized analysis in clinical OptA imaging studies.

</details>


### [205] [WaitLens: Temporal VLM-Based Adaptive Waiting for Industrial GUI Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32482)
*Shika Ikeura,Xuan Wang,Ziyi Kuang,Jianping Xu,Chao Kang,Yongxiang Hu,Xiaoyu Wang,Juxing Yuan,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对GUI测试智能体的固定等待开销，提出WaitLens：在带等待结束时间标注的GUI加载轨迹上微调轻量VLM，并用时间重采样构造多样训练样本，实现自适应判断是否继续等待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI测试智能体除LLM推理外的固定等待带来大量开销：工业实测显示固定等待占采样交互间隔时间的42.3%，与模型调用54.8%相近，且62.9%的等待发生在界面已加载完成之后。已有自适应等待方法依赖特定应用数据或平台信息，难以跨工业环境泛化。

Method: 在带"等待结束时间"标注的GUI加载轨迹上微调轻量级VLM，使其基于时序观测判断是否继续等待；引入时间重采样（temporal resampling）构造多样化训练样本，让模型从不同时间尺度的观测中学习多样的界面加载行为。

Result: 等待决策准确率达86.08%，显著高于骨干VLM的54.13%；在美团工业测试流水线录屏数据上评估，相比20秒固定等待，界面就绪后的平均额外等待时间降低94.98%。

Conclusion: 基于轻量VLM与时序重采样的自适应等待策略无需应用/平台特定信息即可有效消除冗余等待，显著提升GUI测试智能体的工业部署效率。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of GUI testing agents is important for industrial deployment. Beyond LLM inference, fixed waiting between consecutive interactions introduces substantial overhead. Our industrial study reveals that fixed waiting accounts for 42.3% of the time within the sampled interaction intervals, comparable to the 54.8% spent on model calls. Moreover, 62.9% of the waiting time is spent after the GUI has already finished loading. Although prior studies have proposed adaptive waiting strategies, their reliance on app-specific data or platform-specific information limits their applicability across diverse industrial environments. To this end, we propose WaitLens, which fine-tunes a lightweight VLM on GUI loading trajectories annotated with waiting end times. We introduce temporal resampling to construct diverse training examples, enabling the model to learn varying GUI loading behaviors from temporal observations. Experiments show that WaitLens achieves 86.08% accuracy in deciding whether to keep waiting, compared with 54.13% for the backbone VLM. Our evaluation using recordings from Meituan's industrial testing pipeline shows that WaitLens reduces mean extra waiting after the interface is ready by 94.98% compared with a 20-second fixed wait.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond Coverage and Mutation: A Blind-Spot Analysis of LLM-Generated Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32548)
*Xiaoyu Shi,Wenqiang Luo,Boyang Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出义务导向的单元测试评估框架BlindSpotEval（含边界值充分性、控制流充分性、Oracle强度与执行可靠性四项指标）及复杂度分层基准ObligBench，对四个LLM、四种提示策略产生的14,320个测试评估，揭示覆盖率指标无法捕捉的系统性盲区（如异常/空/负/空值输入覆盖低、catch块与default分支充分性仅0.100/0.312），并证实LLM测试存在happy-path偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单元测试评估依赖分支覆盖率、变异得分等聚合指标，只能量化测试套件对程序行为的覆盖程度，无法识别其系统性遗漏的"行为义务"（behavioral obligations）。因此需要一种能以构造级粒度刻画测试充分性、定位未测试行为类型的评估方法。

Method: 提出与Arrange-Act-Assert模式对齐的义务导向评估框架BlindSpotEval，包含三项指标：Boundary-Value Adequacy（边界值充分性）、Control-Flow Adequacy（控制流充分性）、Oracle Strength（断言强度/测试预言强度），并辅以执行可靠性评估。同时构建ObligBench——包含895个复杂函数与700个低复杂函数的复杂度分层Java基准，带源码侧义务标注。实验评估四个LLM在四种提示策略下生成的14,320个测试。

Result: 错误断言占测试失败的15.6%，说明覆盖率无法保证断言正确性。可执行测试中，null、负数、空参数覆盖率仅34.8%、23.8%、38.8%，而布尔类平均覆盖超93%；catch块与default分支的控制流充分性得分仅0.100和0.312。Oracle强度总体较高但不均衡，对对象返回属性值与异常的断言较弱。LLM测试表现出与人工测试相同的happy-path偏见，集中于正常输入与预期流程；且这些盲区在低复杂度函数中同样存在，说明代码复杂度本身不能解释盲区成因。

Conclusion: BlindSpotEval提供构造级的充分性画像，可识别测试不足的义务类型，指导超出聚合分数能力的定向改进；聚合指标（覆盖率、变异得分）系统性地掩盖了输入边界、异常路径与断言质量方面的盲区，且该问题与代码复杂度无关。

Abstract: Existing evaluation protocols for unit tests rely on aggregate metrics such as branch coverage and mutation score. These metrics quantify the extent to which a test suite exercises program behavior, but do not identify the behavioral obligations it systematically leaves unchecked. We propose BlindSpotEval, an obligation-based evaluation framework aligned with the Arrange-Act-Assert pattern. The framework comprises three metrics, Boundary-Value Adequacy, Control-Flow Adequacy, and Oracle Strength, complemented by an assessment of execution reliability. We also introduce ObligBench, a complexity-stratified Java benchmark comprising 895 complex and 700 low-complexity functions with source-side obligation annotations. Evaluating 14,320 tests from four LLMs under four prompting strategies reveals systematic blind spots. Incorrect assertions account for 15.6% of test failures, illustrating that coverage alone does not establish oracle correctness. Among executable tests, coverage of null, negative, and empty arguments reaches only 34.8%, 23.8%, and 38.8%, compared with average coverage above 93% for booleans. Catch blocks and default groups have Control-Flow Adequacy scores of only 0.100 and 0.312. Across both dimensions, LLM-generated tests exhibit the happy-path bias observed in human-written tests, concentrating on normal inputs and expected flows while leaving unusual values and exceptional paths unchecked. Oracle strength is high on average but uneven, with weaker assertions for object return property values and exceptions. These blind spots also occur in low-complexity functions, suggesting that code complexity alone does not explain them. BlindSpotEval provides construct-level adequacy profiles that identify under-tested obligation types and can guide targeted improvements beyond those supported by aggregate scores alone.

</details>


### [207] [Tool-Calling LLM Agents Under Realistic MCP Faults: Scale Does Not Purchase Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32555)
*Antonio Ken Iannillo,Joshua Segun Owotogbe,Roberto Natella,Francesco Avallone,Kristina Kudryavtseva,Indika Kumara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出MCP-Injector——一种协议感知的故障注入中间件，基于真实MCP生态故障分布对LLM智能体进行韧性评测，发现永久性偏重的故障使任务完成分数下降60%，且模型规模并不带来容错能力提升，框架级错误处理比模型规模更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准假设外部工具（经MCP暴露）总能正确、及时响应，智能体对真实工具故障的韧性未被检验；而已有研究表明能力基准可能掩盖运行时的脆弱性，亟需补充协议层面的故障注入评测维度。

Method: 1）构建MCP-Injector：可即插即用、协议感知的故障注入中间件，拦截MCP工具调用路径，支持永久/瞬时/间歇三种持久性及可设种子的调度；2）故障模型由公开MCP生态数据校准——挖掘1,774个开源MCP仓库的57,240条issue与PR线程，得到"故障模式×持久性"的观测分布；3）开展1,350格实验，交叉2种骨干规模（20B、120B）、3种智能体栈（自研多轮plan-and-execute、OpenAI Agents SDK、LangChain）与3种故障注入条件。

Result: 永久性偏重的故障致任务完成分从8.08降至3.23（10分制，降幅60%）；规模未带来容错红利：120B在故障下保留的无故障得分比例不高于20B（韧性比模型 p=0.96），其故障下完成率反而更低（91.1% vs 95.6%，差4.4个百分点，95% CI [-0.2, 9.3]）；不同智能体栈吸收故障的能力存在差异。

Conclusion: 仅凭能力基准会掩盖工具调用智能体的运行脆弱性；协议感知的故障注入应作为互补的评测维度，且工程实践中框架级错误处理机制的重要性高于单纯扩大模型规模。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external tools exposed via the Model Context Protocol (MCP), yet current benchmarks assume tools respond correctly and on time, leaving agent resilience to realistic tool failures untested. We introduce MCP-Injector, a drop-in, protocol-aware fault-injection middleware that interposes on the MCP tool-call path with controlled persistence (permanent, transient, intermittent) and seedable schedules.
