Its fault model is calibrated to the observed incident distribution (failure mode x persistence) of the public MCP ecosystem, mined from 57,240 issue and pull-request threads across 1,774 open-source MCP repositories. In a 1,350-cell experiment crossing two backbone sizes (20B, 120B), three agent stacks (custom multi-round plan-and-execute, OpenAI Agents SDK, LangChain), and three fault injection conditions, we find that faults drawn from this permanent-heavy stress profile cause severe quality degradation (task-completion score falls by 60%, from 8.08 to 3.23 out of 10), and that scale does not purchase fault tolerance for the two gpt-oss backbones we test: the 120B retains no larger share of its fault-free score under faults (resilience-ratio model, $p = 0.96$), and its completion rate under faults is, if anything, lower (91.1% vs. 95.6%; difference 4.4 points, 95% CI $[-0.2, 9.3]$). Agent stacks differ in how well they absorb faults, which suggests that framework-level error handling matters more than model scale. These results demonstrate that capability benchmarks alone can mask operational brittleness, motivating protocol-aware fault injection as a complementary evaluation dimension for tool-calling agents.

</details>


### [208] [Lessons Outlive Their Failures: A Case Study of Rules for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32578)
*Hassan Hassanzadeh Aliabadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过为期六周的单一仓库案例研究，考察AI编码代理"harness"（规则文件、技能、运行时钩子与自动检查）的长期有效性，发现规则可超出其修复的失败而长期存续，而文档漂移会持续存在并需要机械检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于harness的研究多基于单一时点或短时基准测试，缺乏对真实日常使用场景中规则长期效用的观察，因此作者希望探究失败、修复与复现之间的关系。

Method: 在一个纯Markdown个人记录仓库上，让三种AI代理工具在共享规则集下每日编辑，持续六周；建立包含118次代理失败、其修复及是否复发的台账；用47条已知过时语句与20条植入错误检验月度LLM文档审计；并在隔离git克隆中重放5个历史失败（有无修复各三次）。

Result: 文档漂移为最大失败类别（118次中48次，其中35次集中于某一审计日）；单次审计约发现半数已知漂移（Claude Sonnet 5.5均值50%），且至少93%的发现正确；Sonnet 5.5完成5个重放任务中的4个，其中3个无论有无修复均未复现失败；仅"计数过时"失败在无检查时复现，而检查在所有运行中均阻止了它；em-dash检查成功拒绝了违规输出；关于运行时间估计的书面规则未改变Sonnet 4.5的过高估计。

Conclusion: harness中的经验可超出其当初针对的失败而继续有效，而漂移持续存在、仍需机械检查；持续演进harness的工具会不断累积新教训而少淘汰旧规则，因此应定期重测并淘汰失效规则。由于仅基于单一仓库、每条件三次运行，结果属探索性。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly work inside a harness: rule files such as AGENTS.md, on-demand skills, runtime hooks and automated checks. Most studies of harnesses look at a single point in time or at short benchmark runs. I report a six-week case study of my personal system of record, a plain-markdown repository that three AI agent tools edited daily under one shared rule set. I built a ledger of 118 agent failures, their fixes and whether they came back. I also tested the system's monthly LLM documentation audit against 47 known stale statements and 20 planted errors. Finally, I replayed five past failures in isolated git clones, with and without their fixes. All results come from one repository with three runs per condition, so I report them as exploratory. Stale documentation (drift) was the largest failure class (48 of 118), although 35 of those were found on one audit day. A single audit run found about half of the known drift (Claude Sonnet 5.5 mean: 50%), and at least 93% of its findings were correct. On Sonnet 5.5, the agent finished four of the five replayed tasks. In three of those four, the failure did not happen, with or without its fix. Only the stale-count failure came back without its check, and the check stopped it in every run. On older models, an agent drafted an em-dash once while the em-dash lint check was active, and the check rejected it. A written rule on run-time estimates did not change Sonnet 4.5's overestimates. These results suggest that harness lessons can outlive the failures they were written to fix, while drift persists and still needs mechanical checks. Tools that evolve a harness add lessons faster than they retire them, so they should re-test their rules from time to time and retire the obsolete ones.

</details>


### [209] [Can AI Agents Help Robotic Engineers? Lessons from Two Focus Days in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32636)
*Salim Fares,Steffen Herbold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一家使用工业机器人的中小企业（SME）的案例研究，考察AI代理执行多步编程任务是否能迁移到机器人工程领域。结果显示代理总体未提升工程师生产力（仅高度重复性代码生成任务除外），但效果随时间改善；工程师的AI使用经验比机器人工程经验更能预测成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发中AI代理已能自主完成多步编程任务，但其能否迁移到机器人工程尚不清楚——该领域工程师使用小众、厂商专用的语言编程工业机器人，并依赖仿真工具的视觉反馈，与通用软件开发场景存在显著差异。

Method: 对一家编写工业机器人程序的中小企业开展案例研究，观察工程师在实际任务中使用AI代理的情况，分析生产力变化、任务类型差异以及经验因素（AI经验 vs. 机器人工程经验）的影响。

Result: AI代理未使工程师生产率提高，唯一例外是需生成高度重复代码的任务；随时间推移，工程师学会使用代理、代理也被适配到该领域，使用效果逐步改善；AI使用经验对代理成功的贡献大于机器人工程经验。

Conclusion: AI代理并非即插即用的解决方案。企业需培训工程师使用代理、为学习曲线预留时间，并确保代理配置匹配领域与开发者需求。观察到的显著学习曲线表明，关于AI生产力增益的研究应采取纵向设计，否则会遗漏工程师在实践中采纳代理的真实过程。

Abstract: Software developers increasingly use AI agents that execute multi-step programming tasks on their own. However, it is unclear if and how this transfers to robotic engineering, where engineers program industrial robots in niche, vendor-specific languages and rely on visual feedback from simulation tools. Within this paper, we report a case study on the use of AI agents by an SME that programs industrial robots. Our results show that the agent did not make the engineers more productive, with the exception of a task that required the generation of highly repetitive code. However, the results with the agent improved over time, as the engineers learned how to use it and the agent was adapted to the domain. Moreover, the experience with AI mattered more for the success with the agent than the experience with robotic engineering. This shows that AI agents are not a plug-and-play solution. Instead, companies must train their engineers on the usage of agents, plan time for a learning curve, and ensure that agent configurations match the domain and developer requirements. The strong learning curve we observed further implies that studies on productivity gains with AI should be longitudinal, as they otherwise miss how engineers adopt agents in practice.

</details>


### [210] [StateMirror: Pre-Execution Detection of Hallucinated Tool Calls in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32648)
*Shiwen Shan,Yintong Huo,Zhiying Wu,Yuxin Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 StateMirror，一种在执行前拦截并检测 LLM 编码智能体"幻觉工具使用"的轻量检测器，通过 AST 与命令分析提取状态谓词并对活仓库做确定性校验，检测 F1 达 0.817、延迟降至 1.50 ms 且零 token 消耗，并显著降低执行时间与 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体会提出在真实执行环境中不成立的动作（如访问不存在的路径、缺失的包），称为"幻觉工具使用"。对 4,398 条轨迹的初步研究表明该类幻觉普遍且代价高：平均需额外 3 个交互步骤、22.4K tokens 才能恢复。现有框架依赖执行后的事后反馈，浪费运行时资源。而智能体执行环境由具体代码库与 harness 组件构成，许多动作前置条件可在执行前提取并校验。

Method: StateMirror 为轻量级执行前检测器：在 harness 边界拦截动作，通过 AST 与命令分析提取状态谓词，并对实时仓库状态进行确定性验证；在执行前精确定位环境不匹配，从而提前阻止无效动作及其后续恢复循环，并提供执行前反馈供智能体修正。

Result: 在 2,508 个动作上，检测精度 0.940、F1 0.817，较最强 LLM judge 的 F1 高出 35.3%；中位检测延迟由 17.73 s 降至 1.50 ms，token 消耗为零。执行前反馈使智能体修正超过 95% 的幻觉工具调用；在 79 个任务的在线缓解中，执行时间降低 15.8%、token 消耗降低 11.8%，同时保持任务解决率。

Conclusion: 基于确定性环境状态的执行前校验可高效替代事后反馈机制，在近乎零开销下检测并缓解幻觉工具使用，兼顾效果与效率，为编码智能体的可靠性提供实用方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding agents may propose invalid actions that are not applicable in the execution environment, such as accessing non-existent paths or missing packages. We term this mismatch hallucinated tool use. A preliminary study of 4,398 agent trajectories reveals that these hallucinations are pervasive and costly, requiring a median of 3 additional interaction steps and 22.4K tokens to recover. Existing frameworks rely on reactive, post-execution feedback, wasting runtime resources. Nevertheless, since an agent's execution environment is grounded in concrete codebase and harness components, many action prerequisites can be extracted and validated prior to execution. To this end, we introduce StateMirror, a lightweight pre-execution detector. StateMirror intercepts actions at the harness boundary, extracts state predicates via AST and command analysis, and deterministically validates them against the live repository. By pinpointing exact environment mismatches before execution, StateMirror preempts invalid actions and subsequent recovery cycles. Evaluation on 2,508 actions shows StateMirror achieves 0.940 precision and 0.817 F1 in detecting such hallucinations, surpassing the strongest LLM judge by 35.3% in F1 while reducing median detection latency from 17.73 s to 1.50 ms with zero token consumption. For practical mitigation, StateMirror's pre-execution feedback enables agents to rectify over 95% of hallucinated tool calls. During live mitigation on 79 tasks, StateMirror reduces agent execution time by 15.8% and token consumption by 11.8% while maintaining task resolution.

</details>


### [211] [CC4Q Workbench: From Quantum Repositories to Analyzable Code Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32651)
*Zenghui Zhou,Yuechen Li,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出开源工具 CC4Q Workbench，将量子 SDK 代码注释的提取、量子相关分类与人工核验整合为可复用工作流，并发布六个 SDK 数据集（39,593 个 SCCU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 SDK 的高层 API 使用需理解数学原理、电路语义与硬件属性，而代码注释可提供 API 调用中未显式表达的自然语言/结构性解释；但目前研究量子 SDK 注释需分别执行注释提取、量子特定分类和人工核验，步骤割裂、集成成本重复。

Method: 构建开源工作台 CC4Q Workbench：给定指定 revision 的 Python 仓库，自动抽取代码-注释对，将注释切分为句级单元（SCCU），并用两个已训练分类器分别识别量子相关内容与赋予量子知识类别；提供 Web 界面支持数据集浏览、代码片段查看、新仓库语料构建、隐藏模型预测的二元标注以及基于导出标注的分类器微调。

Result: 发布六个 SDK 数据集聚合统计，共 39,593 个句级代码注释单元，其中 17,517 个可在 Web 界面浏览；在原始数据集之外三个应用层 Qiskit 仓库上无需改代码即产出 11,517 个 SCCU；对 379 个 SCCU 的人工核验显示，人工标注与二元分类器预测一致率为 85.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作台把量子注释分析流程工具化、可复用化，降低了跨仓库重复集成成本，并具备良好的跨仓库泛化性与可接受的分类可靠性。

Abstract: Quantum software development kits (SDKs) expose high-level APIs whose use requires an understanding of underlying mathematical principles, circuit semantics, and hardware properties. Code comments can provide natural-language or structural explanations that are not explicit in API calls. However, studying comments in a new quantum SDK still requires researchers to coordinate separate steps for comment extraction, quantum-specific classification, and manual verification, resulting in repeated integration effort. To address this problem, we present CC4Q Workbench, an open-source workbench that integrates these steps into a reusable workflow. Given a Python repository at a specified revision, CC4Q Workbench automatically extracts code-comment pairs, segments comments into sentence-level units, and applies two trained classifiers to identify quantum-specific content and assign quantum-knowledge categories. Its web interface supports exploration of bundled datasets, inspection of code snippets, corpus construction from new repositories, manual annotation with model predictions hidden during binary labeling, and classifier fine-tuning using exported annotations. The release provides aggregate statistics for six SDK datasets comprising 39,593 sentence-level code comment units (SCCUs), of which 17,517 are browsable in the web interface. Applied to three additional application-layer Qiskit repositories outside the original CC4Q dataset, the pipeline ran without code changes and produced 11,517 SCCUs. Manual verification of 379 SCCUs showed 85.0% agreement between human annotations and the binary classifier's predictions.

</details>


### [212] [OpenDraco: A Platform for Engineering and Experimenting with Configurable Multi-Agent Architectures for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32686)
*Xiang Feng Ye Pan,Vincenzo De Martino,Matias Martinez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenDraco：面向LLM多智能体系统迭代实验的开源平台，以版本化JSON规格统一建模与执行，在自动程序修复任务上验证了配置间成本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统（MAS）实验涉及建模配置、执行与评估、结果比较、系统设计迭代的循环，而现有方法对这一实验生命周期的端到端支持有限。

Method: 提出开源平台OpenDraco，将上述活动整合进单一工作流：以可导出、版本化的JSON规格表示智能体、提示词、工具、模型、参数与通信拓扑；将其转译为支持不同交互结构的可执行LangGraph工作流；支持用户使用本地或云端LLM修改配置并重新评估。以自动程序修复（APR）为实例，在合成问题与真实SWE-bench Lite问题上评估一个顺序基线及七种重构配置，并补充概念验证任务。

Result: 修复效果相近的配置之间存在显著成本差异；基线使用开源LLM解决了77个SWE-bench问题中的12个；额外概念验证任务显示其适用性可超出APR场景。

Conclusion: OpenDraco支持对异构MAS配置的迭代实验，使研究者能在统一环境中考察其结果与成本。

Abstract: Experimenting with LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) involves an iterative cycle of modeling configurations, executing and evaluating them, comparing results, and refining system designs. Existing approaches provide limited end-to-end support for this experimentation lifecycle. We present OpenDraco, an open-source platform that integrates these activities into a single workflow. OpenDraco represents agents, prompts, tools, models, parameters, and communication topology in exportable, versioned JSON specifications, translated into executable LangGraph workflows supporting different interaction structures. Users can modify and re-evaluate configurations using local or cloud-based LLMs. We instantiate OpenDraco for automated program repair (APR) and evaluate a sequential baseline and seven reconstructed configurations on synthetic and real-world SWE-bench Lite issues. The results reveal substantial cost differences among configurations with similar repair outcomes, while the baseline resolves 12 of 77 SWE-bench issues using open-source LLMs. Additional proof-of-concept tasks illustrate applicability beyond APR. OpenDraco supports iterative experimentation with heterogeneous MAS configurations, enabling researchers to examine their outcomes and costs within a common environment. Demonstration video: https://youtu.be/278sOR4lBEk.

</details>


### [213] [Trusting the Tool: Asymmetric Deference of LLM Vulnerability Auditors to Static-Analysis Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32876)
*ZhiHong Liu,Shuyao Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文测量LLM能否区分静态度分析产出的真伪证据，发现模型对虚假“保证”存在方向性盲从，并可通过逐条核验声明来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与静态分析结合的流水线让模型把分析器输出当作证据，但模型是否区分真伪证据、其审视是否随证据内容方向而变化，此前未被量化。

Method: 构建C/C++过程内分析器生成证明义务；对PrimeVul的漏洞与补丁函数在六种仅注释不同的证据条件下审计，含伪造“缺失检查已存在”的保证与伪造“新增检查缺失”的告警。覆盖4个不相交集共916对、4家厂商11种模型配置、16,572次审计，无训练或微调；另做7个新模型的内部注册复现，以及“分调用核验每条声明并展示被驳斥声明”的预注册干预。

Result: 真证据仅提升准确率+0.02；假证据呈不对称性：相对真注释，虚假保证使真实漏洞检出率最多下降19个百分点，虚假告警则无显著影响且更常被质疑。复现显示虚假保证比虚假告警更能将判定拉向其主张（平均不对称+0.16，95%CI +0.10~+0.24）。提示词指令无法可靠降低盲从，最佳指令未能复现。分调用核验在虚假保证下提升12个百分点（p=0.002）且真证据下无损；第二个模型增益更小且不显著。

Conclusion: 流水线应在模型依赖工具保证前先行核验，研究应以方向感知方式测量盲从；模型对“安全感”类虚假证据的偏信是主要风险。

Abstract: Pipelines that combine large language models (LLMs) with static analysis let the model consume the analyser's output as evidence, but whether the model distinguishes true from false evidence, and whether its scrutiny depends on what the evidence claims, has not been measured. We built an intraprocedural analyser for C/C++ that produces proof obligations and audited vulnerable and patched functions from PrimeVul under six evidence conditions that differ only in the notes, including fabricated assurances that a missing check exists and fabricated alarms that an added check is missing. The study covers 916 pairs in four disjoint sets, eleven model configurations from four vendors and 16,572 audits; no model is trained or fine-tuned. True evidence barely improved accuracy (+0.02). False evidence acted asymmetrically: relative to true notes about the same operations, false assurances lowered the detection of real vulnerabilities by up to 19 points, whereas false alarms never significantly lowered accuracy and were disputed far more often. An internally registered replication on seven new models confirmed that false assurances move verdicts towards their claim more than false alarms do (mean asymmetry +0.16, 95% CI +0.10 to +0.24). Instructions in the audit prompt did not reliably reduce deference; the best one failed to replicate. Verifying each claim in a separate call and showing the refuted claims raised accuracy under false assurances by 12 points in a pre-specified test (p=0.002) without loss under true evidence; for a second model the gain was smaller and not significant. Pipelines should therefore verify a tool's guarantees before the model relies on them, and studies should measure deference in a direction-aware way.

</details>


### [214] [Green Code or Costly Code? Evaluating the Energy Costs of Iterative LLM-Based Code Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32891)
*Sadra Emami,Benjamin Weigell,Mohammad Reza Mousavi,Gunel Jahangirova,Hugo Araujo,Bernhard Bauer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过硬件级能耗测量，实证检验LLM代码优化流程中"提速即节能"的假设不成立：LLM自精炼产出的代码相较人类参考解虽更快但更耗能，且优化过程本身的推理能耗难以被运行时节省抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究与实践普遍假设LLM性能优化带来的执行加速自然对应整体能耗降低，但该假设缺乏实证检验；同时优化过程（推理）自身的能源成本是否可被后续执行节能抵消，也未被量化。

Method: 在EffiBench全部1000道题上评估11个开源模型、4种解码配置，全程采集进程级硬件能耗；对比LLM自精炼生成代码与规范人类参考解的单题执行能耗，并核算推理能耗与摊销/盈亏平衡点。

Result: 推理能耗跨度0.79–8.68 MJ（约相当于250–2800次GPT-4.1查询）；限制最大输出token可降低能耗最高69%；44个配置中42个的生成代码单题执行能耗高于人类参考解，超出幅度+4%至+886%；两个例外仅边际增益，盈亏平衡需10^6–10^7次执行；MoE架构推理能耗显著低于同规模稠密模型且不损失正确率。

Conclusion: 性能优化与能效并非一致目标，LLM自精炼系统性产出"更快但更耗能"的代码；架构选择（MoE）比解码参数更具可持续性影响，输出token上限是最有效的能耗杠杆。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to optimize code for performance. This practice often assumes that improvements in execution speed naturally translate into overall lower energy consumption. We challenge this assumption empirically by measuring whether performance-optimized code produced by LLM self-refinement pipelines is also energy-efficient relative to canonical human reference solutions, and whether the energy cost of the optimization process can be offset by subsequent execution-time savings. We evaluate 11 open-source models across four decoding configurations on all 1,000 problems of EffiBench, a state-of-the-art performance benchmark, while collecting per-process, hardware-level energy measurements throughout. Inference energy spans 0.79 to 8.68 MJ across model-variant pairs, which is roughly equivalent to the energy of 250 to 2800 GPT-4.1 queries. Limiting the maximum number of output tokens has the largest impact, reducing energy use by up to 69% compared to the default configuration. On the execution side, 42 of 44 configurations produce code that consumes more energy per problem than the canonical human reference, with overheads ranging from +4% to +886%. The two exceptions achieve only marginal gains, with break-even points on the order of 10^6 to 10^7 executions. Mixture-of-Experts architectures consume substantially less inference energy than equivalently-sized dense models without sacrificing correctness, making architectural choice a more consequential sustainability lever than decoding parameters. Performance optimization and energy efficiency are not always aligned goals: LLM self-refinement consistently produces faster-seeking but energy-heavier code relative to what a human engineer would write. All artifacts are available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23598170.

</details>


### [215] [Runtime BiDi Structural Verification Catches Right-to-Left Layout Defects that Accessibility, Actionability, and Logical-Property Linting Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32934)
*Karan Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出两种运行时结构不变量检测器（零金标模式与双向文本保守差分模式），在14个重构自真实修复提交的RTL缺陷基准上检出13个且无误报，并发现Bulma 1.0.4真实缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具无法定位RTL布局缺陷：axe-core等可访问性引擎、Playwright等可操作性引擎以及Stylelint现行核心逻辑属性规则均无法本地化这类缺陷，因为它们是物理方向性CSS在dir="rtl"下未镜像导致的静默视觉不对称。

Method: 在无头浏览器中施加两类运行时结构不变量：(1) 零金标模式，仅基于计算样式与几何量做自洽性检查；(2) 双向文本保守差分模式，比较LTR与RTL渲染的结构差异。基准为从Bootstrap与Material Components Web带日期的修复提交中重构的14个真实RTL缺陷，并设置负样本（16个LTR孤岛、4个设计系统的43个未修改组件、3种RTL架构）。

Result: 检出13/14（零金标2个、差分11个），补丁版本零误报；在真实提交的bootstrap.rtl.css上检出2个中的1个；16个LTR孤岛与43个未修改组件上0误报（rule-of-three 95%上界≤7%）；发现Bulma 1.0.4真实方向性缺陷。对照：Stylelint核心规则在Bootstrap LTR与正确RTL构建上输出相同的1744条告警，伪本地化全部漏检，朴素像素镜像差分在调参后F1仅0.87。

Conclusion: 两种模式互补：零金标是装饰/样式不对称类缺陷的唯一检出者（5/6），但会对正确的border-inline-end产生误报；差分模式对该类无检出，却从不误报且在块级inset/margin类上经验精确。本文限定于检测，修复为未来工作。

Abstract: Bidirectional (right-to-left, RTL) layout defects arise when interfaces authored with physical directional CSS (margin-left, border-left, text-align:left) are rendered under dir="rtl" without being mirrored. Accessibility engines (axe-core), actionability engines (Playwright), and even Stylelint's current core logical-property rule fail to localise these defects. We present runtime structural invariants -- a zero-golden mode over computed style and geometry, and a bidi-conservative differential mode -- that catch them in a headless browser. On a held-out benchmark of 14 real RTL bugs reconstructed from dated fix commits in Bootstrap and Material Components Web, the detector recovers 13/14 (2 via zero-golden, 11 via the differential mode), with no false alarm on any patched version; on the actual committed historical bootstrap.rtl.css it catches 1 of 2 attempted. It reports 0 false positives on 16 negative LTR-island cases and, in both modes, on 43 unmodified real components across four design systems and three RTL architectures (rule-of-three 95% upper bound <= 7%), and it surfaced a real directional defect in the current Bulma 1.0.4. By contrast, Stylelint's core rule emits an identical 1744 warnings on Bootstrap's LTR and correct-RTL builds, pseudo-localisation detects none of the mirroring bugs, and a naive pixel mirror-and-diff reaches only F1 0.87 with a test-set-tuned threshold. The two modes are complementary: zero-golden is the sole detector of the decoration/styling-asymmetry class (5/6) but false-flags the correct logical border-inline-end, while the differential detects none of that class yet never false-alarms and is empirically exact on the block-level inset/margin class. We scope this paper to detection; repair is future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [MICE to Meet You: From Re-ranking Only to End-to-End Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31932)
*Victor Morand,Yuxuan Zong,Mathias Vast,Josiane Mothe,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MICE-P 是首个端到端的 MICE 检索流水线，基于 PLAID 对上下文化文档 token 表示进行索引，从而在保持 cross-encoder 级效果的同时达到 late-interaction 级检索吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Late-interaction 模型（如 ColBERT，采用 MaxSim 交互）可预计算并索引文档表示，效率-效果权衡优秀，但交互机制过于简单，效果弱于 cross-encoder。MICE 这类重排序架构通过 transformer 打分获得 cross-encoder 级效果并借助文档侧预计算降低推理开销，但此前没有可行的文档表示存储方案，因而无法实现端到端检索。

Method: 引入 MICE-P：以 PLAID 索引 MICE 的上下文化文档 token 表示，构建首个端到端 MICE 检索流水线。同时在匹配实验条件下，使用 Ettin 系列训练可比的 MICE、ColBERT 与 cross-encoder 模型，进行受控对比。

Result: MICE-P 达到与 late-interaction 检索相当的吞吐量，同时保留 cross-encoder 的效果水平，形成其自身特有的效率-效果权衡。

Conclusion: MICE-P 定义了一类新的检索架构：通过可索引的上下文化 token 表示，将 cross-encoder 式表达性打分与 late-interaction 式检索效率统一起来，突破了原有 MaxSim 类模型的性能上限。

Abstract: Late-interaction models offer state-of-the-art efficiency-effectiveness trade-offs for neural retrieval by enabling document representations to be pre-computed and efficiently indexed. Yet, their relatively simple interaction mechanisms, typically MaxSim, can limit their effectiveness compared to cross-encoders. MICE, a recently introduced re-ranking architecture, aims to combine cross-encoder-like effectiveness through expressive transformer-based scoring with reduced inference-time computation through document-side pre-computation. However, no practical way to store these document representations was explored. We address this limitation here and introduce MICE-P, the first end-to-end retrieval pipeline with MICE, based on PLAID to index the contextualized document token representations. We conduct a controlled comparison of MICE, ColBERT and cross-encoders by training comparable models using the Ettin Family under matched experimental conditions. Our results show that MICE-P defines a new class of retrieval architecture with an efficiency-effectiveness trade-off of its own: it matches late-interaction retrieval throughput while retaining the effectiveness of cross-encoders.

</details>


### [217] [A Model-Agnostic Correction Framework for Short-Term Building Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31959)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Huanbo Lyu,Holger Schöner,Zipeng Wu,Jessica Deuschel,Freya Wentzel,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的WeL-RAC框架，在推理阶段检索相似历史案例（基于近期负荷状态与对齐时域气象预报），聚合其真实未来轨迹以校正基础预测器输出，无需重训练；在31栋建筑、3个数据集、8个主干、1–18小时四类时域上，96组数据集级对比中90组获得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期建筑负荷模式随气象与运行工况快速变化，数据驱动模型在当前工况稀有或在训练中表征不足时可靠性下降，即便历史记录中其他地方存在相似情形也难以被模型利用。检索增强时间序列预测为推理时直接复用相关历史案例提供了思路。

Method: WeL-RAC为模型无关的"气象—负荷"检索增强校正框架：以近期负荷状态与horizon-aligned气象预报为检索键，从历史库中检索相似案例，聚合其观测到的未来负荷轨迹，对基础预测器的初始预测进行后处理式校正；整个过程不重训练、不修改基础模型。

Result: 在3个数据集、31个建筑案例、8种预测主干、1–18小时四个预测时域上评测，96组数据集级比较中90组优于对应基础模型。成功与失败案例的追踪分析显示检索到的历史证据如何改善或恶化预测，体现框架的可解释性。

Conclusion: 显式历史检索可作为建筑负荷预测的有效且可解释的补充手段，与现有数据驱动预测器互补，提升对工况变化的适应性与预测可靠性。

Abstract: Short-term building load forecasting remains challenging because load patterns can change rapidly with weather and operating conditions. Data-driven forecasting models may become less reliable when the current operating regime is rare or poorly represented during training, even when similar situations exist elsewhere in the historical record. Recent advances in retrieval-augmented time series forecasting offer a promising way to adapt predictions to changing conditions by directly reusing relevant historical cases at inference time. Motivated by this idea, we propose WeL-RAC, a model-agnostic Weather-Load Retrieval-Augmented Correction framework that explicitly retrieves such evidence at prediction time. WeL-RAC retrieves historical cases that are similar to the current condition based on both recent load states and horizon-aligned weather forecasts. Their observed future load trajectories are then aggregated and used to correct the initial prediction, without retraining or modifying the base forecaster. We evaluate WeL-RAC on 31 building cases across three datasets, eight forecasting backbones, and four horizons from 1 to 18 hours. The framework improves the corresponding base forecasters in 90 of 96 dataset-level comparisons. Beyond accuracy, success and failure case studies further illustrate the framework's interpretability by tracing how retrieved historical evidence improves or degrades forecasts. These findings support explicit historical retrieval as an effective and interpretable complement to the building load forecasting.

</details>


### [218] [From Item Scores to Semantic Tokens: Test-Time Collaborative Adaptation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32098)
*Xiongfeng Shan,Xing Tang,Hanchong Chen,Fuyuan Lyu,Lingjie Li,Jingyan Jiang,Duguang Liu,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练的TCBSA方法，将判别式推荐器的协同分数转为语义前缀边势，在束搜索剪枝前介入，提升生成式推荐的准确率与目标路径存活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语义标识符的生成式推荐器逐token决策并以束搜索解码，束搜索会不可逆地剪掉有潜力的物品路径；当近期少量交互暗示新兴兴趣而早期行为偏向既有偏好时问题尤为严重。测试时已有一个独立训练的判别式推荐器能对同一历史给出高度互补的排序分数，但如何把这些分数用于剪枝之前仍未被解决：合并已完成的推荐列表无法改变此前的束决策，而整物品级分数又无法直接作用于多个物品共享的语义前缀。

Method: TCBSA（Test-Time Collaborative Beam Search Adaptation）在解码阶段将判别式分数的margin转换为共享语义边上的势能，并用置信度门控调节其对累计路径分数的贡献，从而在束选择之前注入协同信息；生成器与判别器均保持冻结、无需重训。理论上给出全局束竞争下目标前缀存活的充分条件，并给出低判别置信度下分数扰动的界。

Result: 全目录实验显示，在多个数据集以及不同生成式/判别式推荐器组合上均稳定优于强基线；对新兴兴趣与解码行为的分析表明推荐准确率与目标路径存活率均有提升，针对性干预实验支持共享前缀修正的贡献；相比单纯加宽原生束宽，TCBSA在准确率与推理时延之间取得更优折中。

Conclusion: 测试时协同适配可在不重训的前提下有效弥补生成式束搜索的剪枝缺陷，共享前缀层面的分数融合是关键，并具备理论保证与实际效率优势。

Abstract: Generative recommenders using semantic identifiers predict items through successive token decisions, but beam search may irreversibly prune promising item paths. This limitation is particularly consequential when a few recent interactions signal an emerging interest while earlier behavior favors established preferences. Yet an independently trained discriminative recommender may rank the same items highly from the same interaction history. Such complementary scores are available at test time, offering a way to adapt a frozen generator without retraining either model. The challenge is to use these scores before pruning: combining completed recommendation lists cannot alter earlier beam decisions, while scores over complete items cannot directly inform choices among semantic prefixes shared by multiple items. We propose \textbf{T}est-Time \textbf{C}ollaborative \textbf{B}eam \textbf{S}earch \textbf{A}daptation (\textbf{TCBSA}), a training-free method that incorporates collaborative information into semantic decoding. TCBSA transforms discriminative score margins into potentials over shared semantic edges and uses a confidence gate to regulate their contributions to cumulative path scores before beam selection. We establish a sufficient condition for target prefix survival under global beam competition and bound score perturbations under low discriminative confidence. Full-catalog experiments show consistent improvements over strong baselines across datasets and different generative and discriminative recommenders. Analyses of emerging interests and decoding behavior demonstrate gains in recommendation accuracy and target path survival, while targeted interventions support the contribution of shared prefix corrections. TCBSA also offers a favorable balance between recommendation accuracy and inference latency compared with simply widening the native beam.

</details>


### [219] [PersonalQuery-Benchmark:Evaluating Retrieval Robustness to Personalized User Expression Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32164)
*Wenyu Liang,Hongzhi Yin,Tongtong Wu,Fan Liu,Kexin Dai,Weiqing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 PersonalQuery-Benchmark (PQB)，从用户历史文本建模个性化句法表达与个性化书写错误，构建 6,516 条查询实例，揭示在信息需求固定时个性化表达差异仍会引起可测的排序波动，且检索效果与表达鲁棒性不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化信息检索方法主要通过用户偏好与查询意图刻画需求差异，忽视了不同用户表达同一检索需求时的个性化表达差异。真实查询数据通常缺乏稳定用户身份、长期表达历史和意图匹配的对照样本，难以系统评估个性化表达差异对检索结果的影响。

Method: 提出 PQB 基准：从用户历史文本中建模个性化句法表达与个性化书写错误，分别构造个性化句法查询和个性化书写错误查询；通过 LLM 评估与独立人工评估验证基准质量。覆盖 Amazon 三个商品领域共 6,516 条查询实例，在词典模型、学习型稀疏、稠密双塔、后交互和混合架构五类检索模型及两种基于 LLM 的排序模型上评测。

Result: 在信息需求保持固定的条件下，个性化表达差异仍带来可测量的排序变化；不同检索模型对句法变化与书写错误变化的敏感度差异明显；整体检索效果更高并不必然意味着对表达变化具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PQB 为系统研究个性化表达鲁棒性提供了新的基准与评估框架，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Existing personalized information retrieval methods mainly model differences in user needs through user preferences and query intent, while paying little attention to the personalized expression differences that arise when different users express the same retrieval need. Existing real query data typically lack stable user identities, long-term expression histories, and intent-matched counterparts, making it difficult to systematically evaluate the impact of personalized expression differences on retrieval outcomes. To this end, we propose PersonalQuery-Benchmark (PQB). PQB models personalized syntactic expression and personalized writing errors from users' historical texts, constructs personalized syntactic expression queries and personalized writing-error queries, and validates benchmark quality through LLM assessment and independent human evaluation. PQB covers three Amazon product domains with 6,516 query instances in total, and is evaluated on five retrieval models representing lexical, learned sparse, dense bi-encoder, late-interaction, and hybrid architectures, together with two LLM-based ranking models. Results show that even when the information need is held fixed, personalized expression differences still induce measurable ranking variation; different retrieval models exhibit clearly different sensitivities to syntactic variation and writing-error variation, while higher overall retrieval effectiveness does not necessarily correspond to stronger robustness against expression variation. PQB provides a new benchmark and evaluation framework for systematically studying personalized expression robustness. The associated code and dataset are available at the following address: https://github.com/cpxjazz123/PersonalQuery-Benchmark.

</details>


### [220] [SkillRep: Characterizing and Understanding the Representation Design Space of Agent Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32336)
*Tianyi Sun,Pengfei Yao,Yanze Wang,Hongda Chen,Yunyun Han,Jun Sun,Chuanrui Hu,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出技能检索器 SkillRep，首次将五种表征选择作为同一检索器的模块进行联合评估，并在 SRA-Bench、SkillsBench 与自建 111,247 技能池 UniPool 上取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 需从大规模技能库中检索正确技能，但请求与技能的写作目的和用词不同，形成"请求-技能差距"。已有检索器各自只采用一两种表征选择来缩小该差距，其增益能否叠加未知；且此前工作未度量差距本身，因此不清楚每种选择缩小的是差距的哪一部分。

Method: 定义由五种表征选择构成的设计空间：编码器微调、查询侧扩展、文档侧扩展、背景投影、词法通道。以 0.6B 编码器加 LLM 扩展构建统一检索器 SkillRep，将五种选择作为模块联合评估；并用三个量刻画差距：background（无法区分技能的嵌入分量）、unsaid vocabulary（请求未说出的金标技能内容）、dilution（编码为单向量时的信息损失）。

Result: 在 SRA-Bench 上，配合官方技能池 MRR@10 较最强同规模检索器提升 7.02 点，配合自建 UniPool（111,247 个技能）提升 8.79 点；在 SkillsBench 上亦为最佳。各选择的价值相互依赖：微调会反转四个免训练模块的排序，并使其由互补转为重叠。三量度量揭示了各模块缩小的差距部分及机制。

Conclusion: 模块价值取决于组合语境，单一表征选择的增益不能简单叠加。缩小同一差距部分的模块相互竞争，缩小不同部分的模块相互互补，这为技能检索器的模块化设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Retrieving the right skill from a large library is essential for LLM agents, yet requests and skills are written for different purposes and in different words. Existing retrievers narrow this request-skill gap with one or two representation choices each, so it is unknown whether their gains hold in combination. No prior work measures the gap itself, so it is also unclear which part of it each choice narrows. We define a representation design space by five choices -- encoder fine-tuning, query-side and document-side expansion, background projection and a lexical channel -- and evaluate them together for the first time, as modules of one retriever, SkillRep. With a 0.6B encoder and LLM expansion, SkillRep improves MRR@10 on SRA-Bench over the strongest same-size retriever by 7.02 points with the official skill pool and 8.79 with UniPool, a 111,247-skill pool we build from public registries, and is best on SkillsBench. The value of each choice depends on the others. Fine-tuning, for instance, reverses the ranking of the four training-free modules and turns them from complementary into overlapping. To understand why, we measure the gap with three quantities: background, the embedding component that does not separate skills; unsaid vocabulary, the gold skill's content a request leaves unsaid; and dilution, the information lost when a skill is encoded into a single vector. They show which part each module narrows and by what mechanism. This reveals that modules narrowing the same part compete for it, while those narrowing different parts complement each other.

</details>


### [221] [Hierarchical GTV Estimation: Bridging the Gap between Container-Level Ranking and Item-Level Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32420)
*Zerong Lan,Fan Zhang,Chuang Chen,Tianchun Huang,Teng Zhang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对直播、O2O等"容器式"电商场景中平台在容器层级（直播间/门店）排序而交易发生在商品层级的粒度错配问题，提出分层GTV预估框架HGE，通过集合感知编码、代理标签学习与残差校正融合，在工业数据上显著优于SOTA并已全量上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴电商平台（直播、O2O）采用"容器式"展示范式，平台在容器层级进行排序与预估，而真实交易发生在容器内商品层级，存在结构性粒度错配。该错配导致GTV预估面临三大挑战：标签方差高、标签稀疏严重、商品构成动态异构。

Method: 提出Hierarchical GTV Estimation（HGE）框架，包含三个组件：(1) Hybrid Set-Aware Encoder（HSAE）建模商品的动态组成与特征交互；(2) Proxy-Label Learning（PLL）策略，将点击后GTV分解为购买概率、数量与单价以缓解标签方差；(3) Residual Correction and Ensemble Fusion（RCEF）模块，融合商品层级聚合与容器层级推断以应对标签稀疏。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上，HGE显著优于现有先进基线，离线XAUC提升+0.63%，在线A/B测试中每搜索收入（RPS）提升+1.42%。

Conclusion: HGE有效解决了容器式电商中容器—商品粒度错配下的GTV预估难题，兼顾方差、稀疏性与动态异构性，且已全量部署于主流量，具备实际工业价值。

Abstract: Emerging e-commerce platforms, such as live-streaming and Online-to-Offline (O2O) services, inherently operate under a "Container-based" display paradigm. In this setting, a critical granularity mismatch exists: the platform performs ranking and estimation at the container level (e.g., livestream rooms or stores), whereas the actual transactions occur at the item level within these containers. This structural discrepancy complicates Gross Transaction Value (GTV) estimation, introducing unique challenges including high label variance, severe label sparsity, and dynamic item heterogeneity. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical GTV Estimation (HGE). HGE employs a Hybrid Set-Aware Encoder (HSAE) to model the dynamic composition of items and feature interactions. To mitigate label variance, we introduce a Proxy-Label Learning (PLL) strategy that decomposes post-click GTV into purchase probability, quantity, and unit price. Furthermore, a Residual Correction and Ensemble Fusion (RCEF) module is designed to handle label sparsity by effectively combining item-level aggregation with container-level inference. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that HGE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a +0.63% lift in XAUC offline and a +1.42% increase in Revenue Per Search (RPS) in online A/B tests. HGE framework has been fully deployed into our main traffic.

</details>


### [222] [Agentic Memory Management Is the Next Frontier of Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32464)
*Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"智能体记忆管理"(AMM)是信息检索(IR)研究的自然延伸，给出系统级框架与记忆对象计算分类学（constants/functions/operators），并规划研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体越来越多地代替用户消费、保留并对信息采取行动，信息管理转移到了智能体内部，以"记忆管理"的形式发生；IR社区需要重新审视自身定位并做出独特贡献。

Method: 观点/立场论文(perspective paper)：1) 提出AMM的系统级框架，涵盖记忆信息的结构、分析、组织、存储、搜索、检索与使用；2) 给出记忆对象的计算分类学——常量、函数、算子，各自具有不同的检索语义；3) 将AMM与既有IR研究进行比较，厘清差异与联系；4) 列出研究议程。

Result: 梳理出AMM与IR的差异与联系，并提出研究议程，包括基准测试(benchmarks)、记忆效用的候选原则(candidate principles of memory utility)、混合参数化与非参数化更新的优化流程等。

Conclusion: AMM是IR的自然扩展，IR社区可在此领域发挥独特作用；作者希望吸引IR内外更多研究者从IR视角思考AMM问题与研究。

Abstract: Information retrieval has long been focusing on connecting people with information they need to complete their tasks. Recently, the meaning of this has changed dramatically as more and more AI agents now consume, retain, and act on information on a user's behalf. Consequently, information management now also takes place \emph{inside} the agent in the form of memory management. In this perspective paper, we argue that \emph{agentic memory management} (AMM), which studies the structure, analysis, organization, storage, searching, retrieval, and \emph{use} of information in an intelligent agent's memory, is a natural extension of IR research and an area in which the IR community can make distinctive contributions. Specifically, we first present a system-level framework for AMM and introduce a computational taxonomy of memory objects as \emph{constants}, \emph{functions}, and \emph{operators}, each with distinct retrieval semantics. Then, we compare AMM with established IR research and identify their difference and connections. Last but not least, we outline a research agenda covering benchmarks, candidate principles of memory utility, optimization procedures that mix parametric and non-parametric updates, and more. We hope this paper could attract more people, both inside and outside the IR communities, to think more about AMM problems and research from IR perspectives.

</details>


### [223] [UAE GovTrust Scope Filtering Gains and Routing Failures in Bilingual Public Service Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32608)
*Aleksandar Mihajlovic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 UAE GovTrust 双语检索诊断集，用角色过滤融合把含多地点查询的正确答案首中率从 65.3% 提升到 95.8%，但范围推断仍极脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务查询常提及多个地点，但仅一个地点决定所请求的服务；需要区分"提及位置"与"作用域位置"，并检验现有检索与路由方法能否利用这一区分。

Method: 构建受控英阿双语诊断集 UAE GovTrust：22 篇人工撰写服务摘要、50 个场景、110 个查询变体；比较 12 种方法，涵盖词法与稠密排序、排序融合、作用域过滤、多语言交叉编码器与直接服务查找；并在新环境中复现执行。

Result: 在 72 条有双语证据的可回答查询上，角色过滤倒数排名融合首条相关结果为 69 条（95.8%），未过滤融合为 47 条（65.3%）；过滤稠密检索与直接查找各 68 条（94.4%）。仅英文证据下，剔除零分词法候选使过滤融合由 52 提升至 64。但未改动的路由器在 168 个审计后扰动中仅 2 个正确选择动作与作用域。全部 1,320 个排序与动作可复现。

Conclusion: 正确的范围元数据可显著改善排序，而脆弱的作用域推断会移除回答所需证据；该研究仅评估检索与路由，不涉及生成答案或法律正确性，仍需独立阿拉伯语与领域验证。

Abstract: Public-service queries can mention several locations while only one controls the requested service. We study this distinction in UAE GovTrust, a controlled English-Arabic retrieval diagnostic comprising 22 authored service summaries and 50 scenarios with 110 query variants. Twelve methods separate lexical and dense ranking, rank fusion, scope filtering, a multilingual cross-encoder, and direct service lookup. On 72 matched answerable queries with bilingual evidence, role-filtered reciprocal rank fusion retrieves a relevant first result for 69 queries (95.8%), versus 47 (65.3%) for unfiltered fusion; filtered dense retrieval and direct lookup each achieve 68 (94.4%). With English-only evidence, excluding zero-score lexical candidates raises filtered fusion from 52 to 64 correct first hits. However, the unchanged router selects the correct action and scope for only 2 of 168 post-audit perturbations covering paraphrases, irrelevant facts, negation, and corrections. These perturbations are deliberately authored diagnostics, not held-out evaluation. Fresh-environment execution reproduces all 1,320 original ranking orders and actions. The results show that correct scope metadata can improve ranking while brittle scope inference can remove the evidence needed to answer. The study evaluates retrieval and routing, not generated answers or legal correctness; independent Arabic and domain validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [224] [From archive to resource: bringing legacy data back to life at scale with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32616)
*C. H. Vieira-Vieira,I. Schneider Chernov,A. Serra,M. Correa Marrero,P. L. Bhavani Mohan,G. Duchateau-Nguyen,T. Y. Doktorova,A. Kreuchwig*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 两家企业团队各自开发的基于LLM的抽取流水线，可从异构的遗留临床前研究报告文本中恢复"研究情境"元数据（如禁食、麻醉、仪器），返回带页码/章节的逐字证据以便人工核验；400余份文件评估显示与专家参考值高度一致，抽取时间由人工25分钟降至1.6分钟，并证明禁食状态与临床化学参数存在物种与待测物特异性关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药企积累了大量遗留临床前研究报告，其中禁食、麻醉、实验室仪器等研究情境细节难以被结构化数据捕获，但这些信息与临床病理参数值相关，缺失会限制历史数据的复用、对照选择和跨研究可比性（数据合并、虚拟对照）。传统人工抽取成本高、不可扩展。

Method: 由两个工业团队独立构建、可扩展的LLM抽取流水线：(1) 识别并规范化元数据字段；(2) 返回逐字证据并附页码与章节引用，以支持可追溯性；(3) 结合人工核验进行提示迭代优化（human-in-the-loop refinement）；(4) 以专家整理的参考值作为基准，对400余份异构报告进行评估；(5) 以抽取的禁食状态为案例，分析其与临床化学参数的关联。

Result: 在广泛的元数据字段集合上与专家参考值高度一致；对报告方式一致的字段表现最佳，对受语言表述或报告结构变异影响的描述符效果较弱。单份报告抽取时间由人工基线的25分钟降至1.6分钟。生物学验证表明：从文本中抽取的禁食状态与物种特异、待测物特异的临床化学参数变化相关，可用于改进历史对照的选择。

Conclusion: LLM可将异构、非结构化的遗留报告转化为证据可溯源、可复用的结构化元数据，核心优势为可扩展性、可追溯性及弥补现行行业标准缺失信息的能力；主要局限为依赖原始报告质量及前期专家投入（模式定义与迭代）。该工作提升了实验可比性，为可靠的数据合并与知情虚拟对照选择奠定基础。

Abstract: Pharmaceutical companies maintain vast archives of legacy preclinical study reports that contain study-context details, such as fasting, anesthesia, and laboratory instrumentation, that are rarely captured in structured data formats, yet they can correlate with clinical pathology parameters values and limit the reuse of historical data. This work demonstrates practical, scalable solutions to recover such descriptors from heterogeneous report texts using large language model (LLM)-based extraction pipelines developed independently by two industry teams. The pipelines identify and normalize metadata fields and return verbatim evidence with page and section reference, hasting human verification and iterative refinement. Evaluation of over 400 documents against expert-curated reference values showed high agreement across a broad set of metadata fields. Performance was strongest for consistently reported items and lower for descriptors subject to linguistic or report structure variability. Extraction time has shrunk from 25 minutes in the manual benchmark to 1.6 minutes. As a concrete biological demonstration, we show that fasting status extracted from report text was associated with species- and analyte-specific shifts in clinical chemistry parameters, illustrating how recovered metadata can improve selection of historical controls. Key strengths of the approach are scalability, traceability, and the ability to recover information missing from current industry standards. Main limitations are dependence on source-report quality and initial expert effort to define and refine schemas. These results establish how LLMs can convert heterogeneous, unstructured legacy reports into evidence-grounded, reusable and structured metadata, leading to better experimental comparability, and thus enabling reliable data pooling or informed virtual control selection.

</details>


### [225] [Knowledge Distillation across Item and Code Spaces for Parallel Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32943)
*Yixuan Xu,Hailin Liu,Linxiang Peng,Can Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为并行语义ID生成式推荐提出两种知识蒸馏目标：Item Distribution Matching (IDM) 与 Code Marginal Matching (CMM)，将序列推荐教师的软分布知识迁移至语义ID生成学生模型，显著优于现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义ID推荐器仅用观测下一物品的硬标签训练，虽能定位目标，却缺乏对同一用户历史下其他候选物品的显式分布信息。序贯推荐器可提供这种物品级预测知识，但教师与学生分别工作在物品空间与代码空间，迁移存在困难。

Method: 针对并行语义ID推荐器（同时预测全部代码位、无自回归前缀条件），设计两种互补蒸馏目标：(1) IDM 由学生并行代码预测重建目录级物品分布，并与教师物品分布对齐；(2) CMM 利用固定物品—代码映射将教师物品概率投影至各代码位，得到代码边际分布并与对应学生预测头匹配。二者在保留学生并行预测结构的同时迁移教师的软分布知识。

Result: 基准数据集实验表明两种方法均持续优于现有最先进方法：IDM 平均提升 Recall@10 15.39%，CMM 平均提升 NDCG@10 8.28%。

Conclusion: 在重建物品空间或代码边际空间进行蒸馏，均能实质性增强并行生成式推荐的硬标签监督，为跨空间知识迁移提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generative recommendation with semantic IDs represents each item as a sequence of discrete codes derived from its semantic representation and predicts these codes to generate recommendations. Existing semantic-ID recommenders are typically trained with hard labels for the observed next item. Although such supervision identifies the target, it provides no explicit distributional information about alternative items conditioned on the same user history. Sequential recommenders can supply this item-level predictive knowledge, but transferring it to semantic-ID generation is challenging because the teacher and student operate in item and code spaces, respectively. We propose two complementary knowledge-distillation objectives for parallel semantic-ID recommenders, which predict all code positions simultaneously without autoregressive prefix conditioning. Item Distribution Matching (IDM) reconstructs a catalogue-level item distribution from the student's parallel code predictions and aligns it with the teacher's item distribution. Code Marginal Matching (CMM) projects the teacher's item probabilities onto each code position using fixed item-to-code assignments, then matches the resulting code marginals to the corresponding student heads. Both objectives transfer the teacher's soft distributional knowledge while preserving the student's parallel prediction structure. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that both methods consistently outperform existing state-of-the-art approaches. On average, IDM improves Recall@10 by 15.39%, while CMM improves NDCG@10 by 8.28%. These results demonstrate that distillation in either reconstructed item space or code-marginal space can substantially strengthen hard-label supervision for parallel generative recommendation.

</details>


### [226] [Separating Thinking from Spelling for Efficient Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32980)
*Danil Gusak,Anna Volodkevich,Ivan Oseledets,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Deco将生成式推荐的“读历史”与“写SID”解耦：骨干网络以融合token读取历史并一次性压缩为下一个物品概念，轻量KV缓存解码器在该概念与已生成前缀条件下完成整个trie约束beam search，在保持SID、trie与自回归分解不变的前提下，以3.2–8.6×吞吐达到与标准生成式推荐器和全骨干教师相当或更优的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归接口对SID长度K付出双重代价：历史T个物品以TK个code token进入骨干（读历史代价），且每次beam-search步都需过骨干，返回B个候选需O(BK)骨干解码token（写标识代价）。后者常占主导，并随骨干规模、SID深度与候选预算增长，限制了候选池与模型的扩展。

Method: 提出Deco（decoding from concepts）：骨干将每个历史物品读作一个融合token，仅一次将历史压缩为紧凑的下一物品概念向量；再由一个轻量、带KV缓存的解码器以该概念与已生成前缀为条件执行全部beam search。物品标识、trie约束与自回归分解保持不变。训练上仅用交叉熵即可逼近全骨干精度，辅以蒸馏进一步提升。

Result: 在电商、电影与音乐流媒体共七个数据集上，Deco准确率与标准生成式推荐器及采用融合输入的全骨干教师相当或更好；在生产风格serving流水线、50 ms p95延迟预算下吞吐为其3.2–8.6×。Deco生成1000个候选快于教师生成100个；在1.5B参数LLM骨干下，其吞吐与69M参数教师持平且准确率更高。

Conclusion: 将beam search从骨干移交轻量概念条件解码器，可有效消除O(BK)的重复骨干解码开销，使更大候选池与更大backbone在实践中可行，为生成式推荐的高效serving提供了与现有SID/trie体系兼容的即插即用方案。

Abstract: Generative recommenders retrieve items by writing their semantic IDs (SIDs) code by code under trie-constrained beam search, an approach now deployed on large web platforms. Standard autoregressive interfaces pay for the SID length K twice: a history of T items enters the backbone as TK code tokens, and every beam-search step runs through the backbone, so returning B candidates costs O(BK) backbone decode tokens per user. The second cost often dominates, and it grows with the backbone, the SID depth and the candidate budget.
  We introduce Deco (decoding from concepts), which separates reading the history from writing the identifier. The backbone reads each history item as one fused token and condenses the history once into a compact next-item concept; a light KV-cached decoder conditioned on the concept and the generated prefix runs the entire beam search, while the identifiers, the trie and the autoregressive factorization stay unchanged. The decoder approaches full-backbone accuracy with cross-entropy alone, and distillation improves it further. On seven datasets from e-commerce, movies and music streaming, Deco achieves comparable or better accuracy than both a standard generative recommender and the full-backbone teacher with fused inputs, while delivering 3.2--8.6$\times$ their throughput under a 50 ms p95 latency budget in a production-style serving pipeline. Delegating beam search makes larger candidate pools and models practical: Deco generates 1000 candidates faster than its teacher generates 100, and with a 1.5B-parameter LLM backbone, it matches the 69M-parameter teacher's throughput while achieving higher accuracy.

</details>
